Together these results show that a modest amount of predicted information, of the right kind, is enough to accelerate decentralized routing well below Kleinberg's classical bound, and that even when nodes reveal no coordinates at all, reliable delivery remains achievable.

</details>


### [143] [Online Treasure Hunt in Vertex-Permuted Dynamic Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11013)
*Kamran Ayoubi,Bernard Mans,Lata Narayanan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究顶点置换动态环（VP）中k个agent的寻宝问题，给出不可行性下界、VP(δ)可行条件、单agent与多agent最优算法及随机置换场景的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在顶点不断被置换的动态环拓扑中，探索与寻宝的目标（静止或移动的treasure）能否被agent找到、需要多少时间，是该模型下的基础理论问题。此前缺乏对顶点置换动态环中寻宝问题的可行性边界、搜索时间下界以及多agent加速效果的系统刻画。

Method: 1) 在一般VP模型中对k个agent证明当k≤n-3时寻宝不可行；2) 引入VP(δ)模型，要求任意顶点对(i,j)的边在δ步内出现，分析其可行条件；3) 针对单agent给出最坏情况搜索时间与竞争比的紧界Θ(δn)（δ≥2n）；4) 设计k个agent的最优算法；5) 在随机置换R-VP模型下分别针对oblivious与adaptive adversary分析期望步数。

Result: - 一般VP：k≤n-3时寻宝不可能。\n- VP(δ)：仅当δ≥⌈(n-1)/2⌉时可行。\n- 单agent：δ≥2n时最坏搜索时间与竞争比均为Θ(δn)紧界。\n- k个agent：给出最优算法，最坏搜索时间获得k倍加速。\n- R-VP：对oblivious adversary期望Θ(n)步，对adaptive adversary期望Θ(n log n)步。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了顶点置换动态环中寻宝问题的可行性与复杂度：给出了不可行阈值、VP(δ)的可行条件、单agent的紧界、k-agent的k倍加速以及随机置换下两类对手的期望复杂度，为动态环搜索理论提供了紧确的上下界。

Abstract: We study the problem of treasure hunt by a group of $k \geq 1$ agents in vertex-permuted dynamic rings (VP). In this model, the $n$ vertices remain on a ring but are permuted at each time step. We first show that treasure hunt is impossible for any $k \leq n-3$ agents, if there are no restrictions on the sequence of permutations used in the dynamic ring. We then study the $VP(δ)$ setting, in which for every pair $i, j$ of vertices, the edge $(i, j)$ is guaranteed to appear within $δ$ steps. We show that the class $VP(δ)$ is feasible only for $δ\geq \left\lceil \frac{n-1}{2}\right\rceil$. For the one-agent case, we show a tight bound of $Θ(δn)$ on the worst-case search time as well as competitive ratio of any online algorithm for treasure hunt, provided $δ\geq 2n$. We then give an optimal algorithm for $k$ agents, thereby showing that $k$ agents can obtain a speedup of $k$ on the worst-case search time. Finally, in the R-VP setting, in which in every step, the vertices are arranged as a ring according to a random permutation, we show that treasure hunt takes expected $Θ(n)$ steps against an oblivious adversary and $Θ(n \log n)$ steps against an adaptive adversary.

</details>


### [144] [Entwine: Coordinating Tiled Computation and Fine-Grained Communication across GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11562)
*Kai Ma,Quanfeng Lv,Jingguo Ge,Bowei Dai,Kefan Ruan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 Entwine，通过协调分块计算顺序、细粒度通信与 SM 资源分配，最大化 GPU 张量计算的计算-通信重叠效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 GPU 将张量切分为 tile 以提升数据复用与并行度，单个 tile 完成早于整体张量计算，从而具备计算与通信重叠的机会。但计算与通信进度不匹配会限制该机会：无数据时通信停顿，数据突发到达时通信滞后；通信还会占用共享资源拖慢计算，抵消重叠收益。

Method: Entwine 从三方面协同优化：（1）重排 tile 计算顺序，使通信数据的产生节奏更规整；（2）将该调度与基于 SM 的细粒度通信耦合，以低延迟、低开销处理 tile 结果；（3）由于通信 kernel 也消耗 SM 资源，进一步协调资源分配，在通信进度与计算减速之间取得平衡。

Result: 在具有代表性的张量并行 LLM 工作负载上，Entwine 相比 cuBLAS+NCCL 取得 1.232x 几何平均加速（最高 1.433x），并在几何平均上超过当前最优重叠基线 3.1–9.8%。

Conclusion: 通过将 tile 计算调度、细粒度通信与 SM 资源分配统一协调，可有效缓解计算-通信进度失配，显著提升张量并行 LLM 负载的端到端性能。

Abstract: Modern high-performance GPU computations partition tensors into tiles to exploit data reuse and parallelism. Individual tile computations complete earlier than the full tensor computation, creating opportunities to overlap computation and communication. However, a mismatch between computation and communication progress can limit these opportunities. Communication stalls when no data is ready, and may lag when data arrives in bursts. Communication can also slow computation by consuming shared resources, offsetting the benefits of overlap.
  We present Entwine, which coordinates tile computation order, fine-grained communication, and SM resource allocation to minimize overall completion time. Entwine reorders tile computation to produce data for communication at a more regular pace. Entwine couples this schedule with fine-grained SM-based communication to process tile results with low latency and low overhead. Since the communication kernel also consumes SM resources, Entwine coordinates their allocation to balance communication progress against computation slowdown. Across representative tensor-parallel LLM workloads, Entwine achieves a geomean speedup of 1.232x (up to 1.433x) over cuBLAS+NCCL, and outperforms state-of-the-art overlap baselines by 3.1-9.8% in geomean. We will open-source our implementation upon publication.

</details>


### [145] [The Computing Channel: How Modulation Programs the Airwaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11145)
*Saeed Razavikia,Carlo Fischione*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"数字函数导向通信"框架，通过联合设计有限字母符号表示与接收端判决，使多址叠加直接编码目标函数，从而在数字通信流程中以有限精度实现空中计算（OAC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算与分布式人工智能需要频繁交换中间结果，但许多应用只需聚合结果而非单设备消息。传统系统先恢复每条消息再聚合，而空中计算（OAC）利用同时传输直接得到聚合。然而主流OAC依赖模拟信令，与有限精度数据及数字通信流程不匹配，因此需要一种数字化的函数计算方案。

Method: 提出数字函数导向通信（digital function-oriented communication）：联合设计有限字母（finite-alphabet）符号表示与接收端判决，使多址叠加（multiple-access superposition）直接编码所需函数而无需恢复个体输入。文中引入其"计算星座（computational constellation）"原理、主要设计方法、扩展及实现挑战，并以联邦边缘学习为例说明该框架。

Result: 该框架能在直接处理量化模型更新（quantized model updates）的同时，减少与用户数相关（user-dependent）的承载数据资源。

Conclusion: 数字函数导向通信将有限字母表示与接收判决联合设计，弥合了OAC与数字通信/有限精度之间的鸿沟，为分布式计算与联邦边缘学习提供了一种减少数据承载资源开销的数字实现路径。

Abstract: Distributed computing and distributed artificial intelligence require frequent exchanges of intermediate results, although many applications need only an aggregate rather than messages from individual devices. Conventional systems recover each message before computing the aggregate, whereas over-the-air computation (OAC) exploits simultaneous transmission to obtain it directly. However, dominant OAC implementations rely on analog signaling, creating a mismatch with finite-precision data and digital communication procedures. This article presents digital function-oriented communication, in which finite-alphabet symbol representations and receiver decisions are jointly designed so that multiple-access superposition encodes the desired function without recovering individual inputs. We introduce its computational-constellation principle, main design approaches, extensions, and implementation challenges. Federated edge learning illustrates how the framework can reduce user-dependent data-bearing resources while operating directly on quantized model updates.

</details>


### [146] [Can AI Remediate Backend Failures Safely? GuardedAct with Blast-Radius-Aware Sandboxing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11264)
*Wanrong Cai,Tianyu Yu,Shaorui Pi,Xiaoxuan Sun,Wenrui Ma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GuardedAct 是一个"沙箱优先"的微服务修复框架，在 LLM 生成修复动作与生产环境之间插入基于爆炸半径的验证层，经数字孪生沙箱模拟与回滚置信度门控，仅自动执行低风险动作。在 DeathStarBench 五类故障场景下，恢复率达 87.4%，附带损害从 25.6% 降至 5.2%（降幅 79.7%），平均恢复时间仅增加约 8 秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 虽能为微服务故障生成修复动作，但直接在生产环境执行存在级联性附带损害的风险，缺少执行前的安全验证机制。

Method: GuardedAct 分四阶段：(1) 摄入诊断报告、实时系统拓扑与近期遥测数据；(2) 提示 LLM 生成排序后的候选修复动作列表；(3) 在轻量级数字孪生沙箱中仿真每个动作，估计爆炸半径并赋予风险标签；(4) 通过回滚置信度门控，仅自动执行低风险动作，高风险动作升级至人工审查。

Result: 在 DeathStarBench 社交网络应用注入的五类故障场景中，GuardedAct 总体恢复率为 87.4%，相比直接 LLM 执行将附带损害从 25.6% 降至 5.2%（降低 79.7%），代价是沙箱引入的平均恢复时间增加约 8 秒。消融实验确认各组件对安全—速度权衡均有实质性贡献。

Conclusion: 沙箱优先验证与风险门控可有效遏制 LLM 自动修复的附带损害，并以可接受的时延代价换取显著安全性提升，适用于生产环境的微服务故障修复。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promising capabilities in generating remediation actions for microservice failures. However, directly executing AI-generated repair actions in production risks cascading collateral damage. We propose GuardedAct, a sandbox-first remediation framework that interposes a blast-radius-aware verification layer between the LLM action generator and the production environment. GuardedAct operates in four phases: (1) ingesting a diagnosis report together with the live system topology and recent telemetry, (2) prompting an LLM to produce a ranked list of candidate remediation actions, (3) simulating each action in a lightweight digital-twin sandbox that estimates the blast radius and assigns a risk label, and (4) enforcing a rollback-confidence gate that auto-executes only low-risk actions while escalating high-risk ones for human review. We evaluate GuardedAct on five fault scenarios injected into the DeathStarBench social-network application. Experimental results show that GuardedAct achieves an overall recovery rate of 87.4% while reducing collateral damage by 79.7% relative to direct LLM execution (from 25.6% to 5.2%), at the cost of a modest sandbox-induced increase in mean time to recovery (approximately 8 s). Ablation studies confirm that each component contributes meaningfully to the safety-speed trade-off.

</details>


### [147] [Rethinking Sparse Formats for RISC-V: A Hierarchical Approach to High-Performance SpMV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11352)
*Anna Pirova,Anastasia Vodeneeva,Konstantin Kovalev,Alexander Ustinov,Maksim Zagriadskov,Daniil Litvyakov,Arthur Kulik,Evgeny Kozinov,Valentin Volokitin,Iosif Meyerov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文比较了8种稀疏矩阵存储格式的向量化SpMV实现，并提出适用于RISC-V处理器的HCSR格式，开源为RVVLASparse库。实验表明合适格式平均加速1.6倍，HCSR在广泛稀疏矩阵上执行时间最短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SpMV是线性代数基础计算核心，广泛应用于科学工程仿真的迭代求解器。不同存储格式对SpMV性能影响显著，而RISC-V平台上缺乏系统的格式比较与优化研究，因此需要针对RVV 1.0的向量化实现与更优存储格式。

Method: 基于RVV 1.0 intrinsics实现8种已有稀疏矩阵存储格式的向量化SpMV，并提出CSR的改进格式Hierarchical CSR（HCSR），全部实现以开源C++库RVVLASparse形式发布；在SpacemiT K1与K3 RISC-V开发板上进行对比实验。

Result: 选用合适的矩阵存储格式可使SpMV计算平均加速1.6倍；所提HCSR格式在广泛类别的稀疏矩阵上取得所有对比格式中最短的执行时间。

Conclusion: 存储格式选择对RISC-V上的SpMV性能至关重要，HCSR是一种高效的通用选择，RVVLASparse为后续研究提供了可复用的开源基础。

Abstract: The sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV) algorithm is a fundamental computational kernel of linear algebra and serves as a building block for numerous applications, primarily iterative solvers for systems of linear equations used in scientific and engineering simulations. This paper compares vectorized implementations of the SpMV algorithm across eight established sparse matrix storage formats and proposes a novel modification of the CSR format, Hierarchical CSR (HCSR), which enhances SpMV performance on RISC-V processors. Our SpMV implementations utilize RVV 1.0 intrinsics and are publicly available as an open-source C++ library named RVVLASparse. Computational experiments conducted on SpacemiT K1 and K3 RISC-V boards demonstrate that selecting an appropriate matrix storage format accelerates SpMV computations by an average of 1.6x, while the proposed HCSR format achieves the shortest execution time among all considered formats across a broad class of sparse matrices.

</details>


### [148] [Taming Bitwise Behavior in GPU Kernels with Tensor Core: Black-Box Reconstruction, Compiler Enforcement, and Static Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11356)
*Ziteng Yang,Nicholas J. Riasanovsky,Warren Deng,Vivek Sarkar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文研究GPU核函数的确定性数值可复现问题，指出浮点归约顺序、部分和精度、融合乘加与舍入位置是位级差异的主要来源，并围绕GEMM归约顺序刻画、平衡树归约强制与数据布局优化、位等价静态检查器三部分展开工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统对GPU核函数的确定性与数值可复现性需求上升，但即便采用确定性实现，同一核函数仍会逐位不同。归约顺序是主因，而这些算术选择可能由手写代码、Triton等块级语言或cuBLAS/rocBLAS等闭源库决定；为性能选择的tile形状同时决定了算术行为，可能破坏批次不变性，固定顺序可能带来最高20%的开销，且自动调优器无法识别哪些配置在位级上等价。

Method: 1) 刻画决定归约与GEMM位级行为的因素，提出GEMM归约顺序描述符（含split-K GEMM中K的划分），据此首次对闭源库算术进行黑盒重建，构建Triton GEMM族以位级匹配cuBLAS。2) 在Triton lowering中强制平衡树归约，并引入数据布局优化。3) 开发针对编译后GPU核函数之间位等价性的可靠静态检查器，首次覆盖NVIDIA PTX与AMD GCN，并集成到Triton自动调优器中以约束搜索到单一位置等价类。

Result: Triton GEMM族在Blackwell与Hopper上所有测试用例中均与NVIDIA cuBLAS位级匹配；对于带融合epilogue的真实LLM形状，性能达到或超过torch.compile。平衡树归约与布局优化使GB300和H100上27个核函数中的19个进入自由顺序性能10%以内。位等价静态检查器可跨NVIDIA PTX与AMD GCN进行等价性判定，并限制自动调优搜索空间。

Conclusion: 通过对归约位级行为的系统刻画与工具化实现，该工作实现了对闭源库算术的黑盒重建、降低了强制确定性归约的性能代价，并提供了跨厂商的位等价静态检查能力，为GPU核函数的确定性数值可复现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Determinism and numerical reproducibility are increasingly required of GPU kernels in machine learning systems, yet deterministic implementations of the same kernel can still differ bit for bit. Floating-point reduction order is the primary cause, alongside partial-sum precision, fused multiply-add operations, and rounding placement. These choices may be hand-coded, selected by a block-level language such as Triton, or hidden inside a closed-source library such as cuBLAS or rocBLAS. A tile shape chosen for performance therefore also determines the arithmetic, potentially breaking batch invariance. Preserving a fixed order can cost up to 20 percent, while an autotuner cannot identify which configurations are bitwise equivalent.
  We characterize the factors determining the bitwise behavior of reductions and general matrix multiplication (GEMM). First, we introduce a descriptor of GEMM reduction order, including the partitioning of K in split-K GEMM. Using it, we perform the first black-box reconstruction of a closed-source library's arithmetic for bit-level correctness. Our family of Triton GEMMs matches NVIDIA cuBLAS in all tested cases on Blackwell and Hopper. For realistic LLM shapes with fused epilogues, it matches or exceeds torch.compile performance. Second, we enforce balanced-tree reduction during Triton lowering and introduce a data-layout optimization that brings 19 of 27 kernels on GB300 and H100 within 10 percent of free-order performance. Third, we develop sound static checkers for bitwise equivalence between compiled GPU kernels, including the first checker spanning NVIDIA PTX and AMD GCN. Integrated into Triton's autotuner, the checker restricts search to a single bit-equivalence class.

</details>


### [149] [Building py-kvcache: A Performance Characterization of External KV Caching for vLLM with NVMe SSDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11744)
*Joseph Kanichai,Tiziano De Matteis,Animesh Trivedi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 py-kvcache，一种面向 vLLM 的 KV 卸载连接器，通过异步直接 I/O、有界共享暂存与调度感知预取，在合适场景下显著降低长上下文请求 TTFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存可复用已计算的 KV 状态以降低 TTFT，但对短前缀或高性能 GPU，重新计算可能快于从外部缓存加载。现有评估多聚焦设备带宽，忽略了传输粒度、中间内存使用及传输进入请求调度时机对缓存收益的影响，因此需要刻画该权衡并据此设计更优的缓存方案。

Method: 在 vLLM 上跨 GPU、CPU、NVMe 三级存储，使用合成负载、长上下文基准、生产轨迹进行权衡刻画；据此设计 py-kvcache 连接器，采用异步直接 I/O、有界共享暂存、调度感知预加载，在请求等待时即发起磁盘读取并与计算重叠。

Result: 80k token 下，py-kvcache 从磁盘加载比 LMCache 快 2.0 倍，其中预加载贡献 1.34 倍。GPU+CPU+磁盘缓存均开启时，比 LMCache 快 1.23 倍，与原生 vLLM KV Offload 差距约 4%。LongBench 与 SCBench 显示收益可延伸至不规则前缀链与多轮负载。Bailian 轨迹回放在较弱 GPU 上改善 TTFT，但在 H100 上平均请求低于盈亏平衡点，仅 GPU 内存即可保留足够前缀。

Conclusion: 外部 KV 缓存应被视为依赖具体部署环境的准入决策，其收益取决于前缀长度、GPU 性能与负载特征，而非单纯设备带宽。py-kvcache 已开源。

Abstract: Prefix caching can reduce the time to first token (TTFT) of long-context LLM requests by reusing previously computed key-value (KV) states, but for short prefixes or fast GPUs, recomputation can be faster than loading from an external cache. We characterize this tradeoff in vLLM across GPU, CPU, and NVMe tiers using synthetic workloads, long-context benchmarks, production traces, and find that cache performance depends on transfer granularity, intermediate memory use, and when transfers enter the request schedule, not only on device bandwidth. These findings motivate py-kvcache, a vLLM KV Offload connector with asynchronous direct I/O, bounded shared staging, and scheduler-aware preloading, which starts disk reads while requests are still waiting, overlapping with compute. At 80k tokens, py-kvcache loading from disk is 2.0x faster than LMCache, with preloading contributing 1.34x. With GPU, CPU, and disk caching enabled, it is 1.23x faster than LMCache and within approximately 4% of the native vLLM KV Offload implementation. LongBench and SCBench show that these benefits extend to irregular prefix chains and multi-turn workloads. Bailian trace replays improve TTFT on a weaker GPU, but on an H100 the average request falls below the break-even point and GPU memory alone retains enough prefixes. External KV caching should therefore be treated as a setup specific admission decision. The py-kvcacheimplementation is available at: https://github.com/atlarge-research/py-kvcache.

</details>


### [150] [GPU-CFR: 80x Faster Counterfactual Regret Minimization by Compiling the Game to Static Dataflow and CUDA Graph Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11923)
*Boning Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 GPU-CFR：将博弈树编译为静态数据流，消除 CFR 迭代中重复的框架开销，使 GPU 上的 CFR 相比最快 GPU 实现加速 29.8–80.4x，相比最快 CPU 实现加速 14–258x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFR 是少数在 CPU 上仍快于 GPU 的大规模数值负载之一：每次迭代需遍历含数十亿状态、数百万个微小且相互依赖的 gather/scatter 步骤的博弈树，并通过通用树接口发出。在 GPU 上每个 kernel 仅耗时微秒级，kernel 启动与框架调度成为主要开销，导致已有 GPU 实现不如优化后的 CPU 代码。作者观察到：对固定博弈，CFR 迭代中除数值外的一切在首次迭代前即已确定。

Method: GPU-CFR 由编译器与运行时构成：将博弈一次性编译为静态数据流——扁平化的边与信息集数组、预计算索引、按深度层级批处理的多趟执行，固定整个操作序列，迭代间仅求解器状态变化。采用静态机会折叠（static chance folding）、深度层级执行块（depth-level execution blocks）与双通道可达性缓冲（dual-lane reach buffer）减少框架操作；因形状、索引、缓冲地址恒定，使用 CUDA Graph Replay 录制一次迭代并以单次图启动重放。

Result: 在单张 A100 上，覆盖纸牌、骰子与棋类的八款博弈测试集中，GPU-CFR 比同加速器上最快的既有 GPU CFR 快 29.8–80.4x；在四款最大博弈上比 LiteEFG（最快开源 CPU 实现之一）快 14–258x。编译表示贡献主要优势：在八线程 CPU 无加速器时已比 GPU 基线快 2.2–51.1x。CPU 优化路径逐位复现参考迭代值，树构建与图捕获在首次求解内即可收回成本。

Conclusion: 在不改变更新规则的前提下，GPU-CFR 在中大型博弈上优于所有 CPU 与 GPU 基线，验证了"预编译静态数据流 + 图重放"范式可逆转 CFR 在 GPU 上的劣势。

Abstract: Counterfactual regret minimization (CFR) is one of the few large numerical workloads that still runs faster on CPUs than on GPUs. Each iteration sweeps a game tree with up to billions of states in millions of small, interdependent gather and scatter steps issued through a generic tree interface. On a GPU every kernel finishes in microseconds, so kernel launches and framework dispatch dominate the run time, and prior GPU implementations have lost to optimized CPU code. We observe that for a fixed game, everything about a CFR iteration except the numerical values is known before the first iteration runs. We propose GPU-CFR, a compiler and runtime built on this observation. It compiles any game once into static dataflow: flat edge and information-set arrays, precomputed indices, and depth-level batched passes fix the entire operation sequence, and only solver state changes between iterations. Static chance folding, depth-level execution blocks, and a dual-lane reach buffer cut the number of framework operations by up to 18.1x. Because shapes, indices, and buffer addresses never change, CUDA Graph Replay records the iteration once and replays it with a single graph launch. On one A100, across an eight-game suite that spans card games, dice games, and board games, GPU-CFR runs 29.8--80.4x faster than the fastest prior GPU CFR on the same accelerator, and 14--258x faster than LiteEFG, one of the fastest open-source CPU implementations, on the four largest games. The compiled representation carries most of that margin: on eight CPU threads with no accelerator it is already 2.2--51.1x faster than the GPU baseline. On the CPU the optimized path reproduces the reference iterates bitwise, and tree construction and graph capture pay for themselves within the first solve. GPU-CFR beats every CPU and GPU baseline on the mid-to-large games of the suite without changing the update rule.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [REACH: Controller-Managed Long-Span ECC for HBM AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10861)
*Rui Xie,Yunhua Fang,Asad Ul Haq,Linsen Ma,Sanchari Sen,Swagath Venkataramani,Liu Liu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: REACH 是一种 HBM 控制器微架构，利用内码纠正常见错误并标注未解决块，将长跨度外码保留用于已知擦除修复，从而在低开销下获得更强的纠错保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HBM 成本高昂，需要更强控制器保护以支持更宽的器件错误率范围。长跨度纠错码在相近码率下保护更强，但直接实现会将小访问与跨度级状态耦合，并在 HBM 带宽下带来昂贵译码开销；读主导的 LLM 解码场景为此提供了有利条件。

Method: REACH 使用成熟内码纠正常见错误并识别未解决 chunk，长外码仅用于已知擦除修复；采用差分 parity 限制写流量；协同设计端点保持 32\,B 事务且不引入额外数据突发。

Result: Ramulator2 在最高错误压力下维持 1.88\,TB/s 应用流量；单独的全接口尺寸分析配合 ASAP7 综合内核支持 2.69\,TB/s 应用目标。该解析目标下，REACH 标称组合相比所评估的 mean-work 直接长码设计，控制器面积减少 55.8\%，建模功耗减少 57.7\%。

Conclusion: 将长跨度恢复保留给异常请求可显著降低控制器面积与功耗，同时维持高应用带宽，验证了面向读主导负载的强纠错控制器设计路径。

Abstract: High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM) cost motivates stronger controller protection that can support a wider range of device error rates. Long-span error-correcting codes provide stronger protection at a comparable code rate, but a direct implementation couples small accesses to span-wide state and requires costly decoding at HBM bandwidth. Read-dominated LLM decode offers a favorable setting: sequential reads support span aggregation, while sparse writes limit parity-update traffic. This paper presents REACH, a controller microarchitecture that uses established inner codes to correct common errors and identify unresolved chunks, reserving a long outer code for known-erasure repair. Differential parity bounds write traffic, and a co-designed endpoint preserves 32\,B transactions without an extra data burst. Ramulator2 sustains 1.88\,TB/s of application traffic at the highest error stress, while separate full-interface sizing supports a 2.69\,TB/s application target using ASAP7-synthesized kernels. At this analytical target, REACH's nominal composition uses 55.8\% less controller area and 57.7\% less modeled power than the evaluated mean-work direct-long design, showing the benefit of reserving long-span recovery for exceptional requests.

</details>


### [152] [Fengshui: Demystifying Chiplet Ecosystem and Bespoke Neural Network Accelerator Codesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10970)
*Haoran Jin,Jirong Yang,Zhiheng Zhang,Justin Shin,Barry Lyu,Kangqi Zhang,Yunpeng Liu,Nathan Bleier*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Fengshui——一个chiplet生态与加速器协同设计框架，通过算子级解聚联合优化chiplet池组成与定制ASIC（BASIC）设计，仅用8个chiplet即可在多种神经网络上大幅降低能耗、EC、EDP、EDPC，并接近无约束异构设计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ML工作负载有严苛的延迟与能耗约束，难以在同构通用硬件上高效运行。算子级解聚（对每个算子定制微架构、批处理与存储层次）是突破瓶颈的关键，但高度定制的加速器NRE成本过高；chiplet集成可分摊NRE，然而“构建哪些chiplet、如何组合成加速器”存在循环依赖：chiplet池的价值取决于所构建的加速器，而加速器质量又受限于可用chiplet。

Method: Fengshui采用chiplet生态与加速器协同设计框架，联合优化chiplet池组成与定制ASIC设计。其通过算子级解聚构建BASIC，协同探索chiplet与存储异构性、张量融合以及流水线/张量/专家并行，并结合布局布线（place-and-route）验证以保证物理可实现性。

Result: 仅用8个策略性选取的chiplet（涵盖网络交换、存内计算单元及多种微架构加速器），Fengshui生成的BASIC相比同构加速器在能耗、EC、EDP、EDPC上分别降低48.5%、88.1%、93.0%、97.8%，且在多种神经网络上距无约束异构设计仅差4.1%。在数据中心MoE与稠密LLM服务中，prefill能耗与EC最多降低16.8%和28.7%；在边缘自动驾驶感知中，实时延迟约束下能耗与EC分别降低12.0%和23.6%。

Conclusion: Fengshui通过chiplet池与加速器的协同设计，有效破解了chiplet生态的循环依赖问题，在极小的chiplet规模下实现了接近无约束异构设计的性能，并在数据中心与边缘场景中显著降低能耗与成本，验证了算子级解聚结合chiplet集成的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Modern ML workloads, with stringent latency and energy constraints, are increasingly hard to run efficiently on homogeneous commodity hardware. We argue that operator-level disaggregation--tailoring microarchitecture, batching, and memory hierarchy to each operator--is essential to overcome these limitations, though the resulting highly bespoke accelerators incur prohibitive Non-Recurring Engineering (NRE) costs. Chiplet-based integration amortizes NRE across applications, but choosing which chiplets to build and how to compose them into accelerators is circularly dependent--a chiplet pool's value depends on the constructed accelerators, while accelerator quality is constrained by available chiplets. This paper introduces Fengshui, a chiplet ecosystem and accelerator co-design framework that jointly optimizes chiplet pool composition and bespoke application-specific integrated circuit (BASIC) design. Fengshui constructs BASICs through operator-level disaggregation, co-exploring chiplet and memory heterogeneity, tensor fusion, and pipeline/tensor/expert parallelism with place-and-route validation for physical implementability. With just 8 strategically selected chiplets, encompassing network switches, processing-in-memory units, and accelerators with diverse microarchitectures, Fengshui-generated BASICs achieve 48.5%, 88.1%, 93.0%, and 97.8% reductions in energy, energy-cost product (EC), energy-delay product (EDP), and energy-delay-cost product (EDPC) over homogeneous accelerators, while scoring within 4.1% of unconstrained heterogeneous designs across diverse neural networks. For datacenter MoE and dense LLM serving, Fengshui reduces prefill energy and EC by up to 16.8% and 28.7%, respectively; for edge autonomous vehicle perception, it achieves 12.0% energy and 23.6% EC reductions under real-time latency constraints.

</details>


### [153] [BEACON: A Versatile Accelerator for Computational Pathology Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11044)
*Sumanth Gudaparthi,Ananth Krishna Prasad,Lin Jia,Rajeev Balasubramonian,Srinivasan Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI+X"思路：以基线AI加速器为基础，仅增加极少逻辑以支持新领域算子，从而打造高产量、多用途芯片，并以计算病理学（Computational Pathology）为例，设计了BEACON加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI加速器已获商业成功，但其他专用领域难以复制该成功，主要因新加速器的研发与量产门槛高。作者希望通过复用基线AI加速器并做最小化扩展来降低这一门槛。

Method: 选择计算病理学作为目标领域，其多阶段流水线包含分割与特征提取、基于kNN的图构建、以及迭代式GCN（交替进行Aggregation与Combination）推理。作者首先分析这些算子在CPU、GPU、AI加速器和GCN加速器上的低效性，然后通过软件重构与对基线脉动阵列（systolic）AI加速器的小幅修改来解决问题：在处理单元间提供灵活数据通路与寄存器访问机制，并新增特征聚合、负载均衡执行、欧氏距离计算、分箱（binning）和计数器聚合等支持。

Result: 所提加速器BEACON仅使基线AI芯粒（chiplet）面积增加1.1倍，但避免了内存墙（memory wall）并实现高并行度，在计算病理学任务上相比基线CPU和GPU平台吞吐量提升超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: "AI+X"方法证明：以基线AI加速器为起点、辅以少量逻辑扩展与软件重构，即可高效支持新兴专用领域，为降低新领域加速器门槛提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While accelerators for AI have seen great commercial success, it is challenging to replicate that success for other specialized domains due to a number of factors. We make the case that barriers for new accelerators can be lowered by starting with a baseline AI accelerator, and adding minimal logic to support new operators demanded by new specialized domains. This leads to a versatile chip that can be manufactured at high volume and deployed for a range of popular applications. We refer to this as the AI+X approach. This paper explores its potential for the emerging domain of Computational Pathology, which involves analysis of large whole-slide tissue images with a multi-stage pipeline. The pipeline requires support for a number of different kernels and operators - early stages perform segmentation and feature extraction, followed by graph creation with k nearest neighbor (kNN) algorithms, and finally inference with an iterative graph convolutional network (GCN) that alternates between Aggregation and Combination. We show that these stages execute inefficiently on a range of baseline CPU, GPU, AI, and GCN accelerators. That inefficiency is addressed with a combination of software re-structuring and small modifications to a baseline systolic AI accelerator. Many of the above kernels can be mapped to a systolic accelerator by offering a flexible datapath between processing elements and register access mechanisms. We add support for feature aggregation, load balanced execution, Euclidean distance calculation, binning, and counter aggregation. This additional flexibility and logic grows the area of a baseline AI chiplet by 1.1x, but by avoiding the memory wall and offering high parallelism, the proposed accelerator BEACON yields over an order of magnitude higher throughput for Computational Pathology than baseline CPU and GPU platforms.

</details>


### [154] [Bio-inspired Learning and Decision-Making with Probabilistic In-Memory Computing Hardware: Part 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11288)
*Thomas Dalgaty,Eiji Kawasaki,Miguel de Prado,Tommaso Salvatori,Germain Haugou,Eric Flamand*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本报告(Part 2)指出，大规模概率能量基础模型在GPU上因HBM接口导致的顺序执行约束而出现严重延迟，看似不可扩展；通过构建概率模拟存内计算(AIMC)处理器的详细事务级模型(TLM)，消除HBM接口并在片上内存直接计算，可使同一能量模型比数据中心级硬件快超过1000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Part 1提出的基于能量的学习与决策模型依赖随机Langevin动力学持续演化神经元状态与模型权重的近似概率分布，但GPU实现证实这类大规模概率能量基础模型存在严重的可扩展性问题，主要源于过高的执行延迟。

Method: 作者将延迟归因于架构与模型特性的根本性错配：算术强度低、高度并行的能量基础模型被运行在依赖高带宽内存(HBM)接口的GPU架构上，HBM对本质可并行的模型施加了严苛的顺序执行约束。为此，作者构建了概率模拟存内计算(AIMC)处理器的详细事务级模型(TLM)，通过消除HBM接口、直接在片上内存中完成计算来验证其性能。

Result: 基于AIMC处理器的TLM仿真表明，同一能量基础模型的执行速度可比数据中心级硬件快1000倍以上。

Conclusion: 并非能量基础模型本身不可扩展，而是依赖HBM接口的GPU架构不适合这一类AI模型；采用存内计算架构可显著突破其可扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: This report extends our previous work (Part 1), which introduced an energy-based model for learning and decision-making under uncertainty. The model leverages stochastic Langevin dynamics to continuously evolve approximate probability distributions over neuron states and model weights. However, as noted in Part 1 and confirmed through GPU-based implementations, large-scale probabilistic energy-based models of this nature face significant scalability challenges due to excessive execution latency. This latency stems from a fundamental mismatch: massively parallel models with low arithmetic intensity (such as energy-based models) are being executed on processor architectures like GPUs that rely on high-bandwidth memory (HBM) interfaces. The HBM imposes brutally sequential execution constraints on inherently parallelizable models, creating the false impression that such models are unscalable. In reality, it is the GPU architecture itself, with its dependence on HBM interfaces, that is not a scalable processor architecture for this class of AI model. In this report, we demonstrate using a detailed transaction-level model (TLM) of a probabilistic analogue in-memory computing (AIMC) processor that the same energy-based model can execute well over 1000x faster than data-center-grade hardware by eliminating the HBM interface and performing computation directly within on-chip memory.

</details>


### [155] [PATTON: Enabling Commodity PIM for Production LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11392)
*Hangyeol Kim,Sanghyun Lee,Teokkyu Suh,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出PATTON，一个PIM运行时，通过分层粒度分配与Commit Zone机制在生产级LLM服务引擎与商用PIM之间架起桥梁，解决Value缓存中GEMV效率、单token写入效率与内存容量的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIM在加速访存受限的decode attention方面具有潜力，但仅加速attention不足以支撑生产级LLM服务：服务引擎需要动态地分配、填充、共享、缓存和回收逻辑KV cache块。在商用PIM上支持这一生命周期需要高效的物理内存分配、块到地址映射与命令生成。对于Value缓存，GEMV效率、单token写入效率与内存容量之间存在根本冲突：面向GEMV优化的布局会将新生成的Value向量分散到多行，使写入开销增大；而更细粒度的内存共享虽提升容量利用率，却会碎片化GEMV归约。

Method: PATTON引入分层粒度分配：块大小的Key和Value粒度与逻辑token块一一对应，固定其物理位置与命令；更粗的粒度将多个块分组以实现高效GEMV执行与内存利用。Commit Zone暂存部分Value块，以支持高效的单token写入，随后再提交至GEMV优化位置。PATTON跟踪这些放置以生成KV cache写入及QK-transpose/SV命令。该方法无需修改PIM处理单元。

Result: 在attention执行及运行时引发的prefill重计算中，PATTON相比所评估的基线平均获得1.95倍加速与4.83倍能效提升，无需修改PIM处理单元，并在vLLM中保持与原生GPU KV cache相当的KV cache命中率。

Conclusion: PATTON通过分层粒度分配与Commit Zone暂存机制，有效化解了PIM上Value缓存的GEMV效率、写入效率与容量三者的冲突，验证了商用PIM支持生产级LLM服务KV cache生命周期的可行性。

Abstract: Processing-in-Memory (PIM) is promising for accelerating memory-bound decode attention, but attention acceleration alone is insufficient for production LLM serving, where engines dynamically allocate, populate, share, cache, and reclaim logical KV cache blocks. Supporting this lifecycle on commodity PIM requires efficient physical memory allocation, block-to-address mapping, and command generation. For the Value cache, these requirements create a fundamental conflict among GEMV efficiency, single-token write efficiency, and memory capacity: GEMV-optimized layouts scatter newly generated Value vectors across rows, making writes costly, while finer-grained memory sharing improves capacity utilization but fragments GEMV reductions. We present PATTON, a PIM runtime that integrates production LLM serving engines with commodity PIM. PATTON introduces hierarchical granule allocation: block-sized Key and Value granules map one-to-one to logical token blocks, fixing their physical placements and commands, while coarser granules group blocks for efficient GEMV execution and memory utilization. A Commit Zone stages partial Value blocks for efficient single-token writes before committing them to GEMV-optimized locations. PATTON tracks these placements to generate KV cache writes and QK-transpose/SV commands. Across attention execution and runtime-induced prefill recomputation, PATTON achieves an average 1.95x speedup and 4.83x higher energy efficiency over evaluated baselines, requires no PIM processing-unit modifications, and maintains a KV cache hit rate comparable to the native GPU KV cache in vLLM.

</details>


### [156] [CHERI-D Reincarnate: efficient multicore CHERI temporal memory safety through allocation reincarnation (draft version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11590)
*Yuecheng Wang,Jonathan Woodruff,Simon W. Moore*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Reinc 是 CHERI 的架构扩展，通过“分配再生”将 ID 隔离替代内存隔离，高效且可扩展地实现时序内存安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前 CHERI-D 的 generation ID 位宽有限且位置固定，ID 耗尽时须隔离整个对象，导致内存清扫频繁、隔离内存开销大，无法及时复用已释放内存。

Method: 提出 Reinc：ID 耗尽时不再隔离内存槽，而是动态重新分配新 ID 实现分配再生，仅隔离并回收已耗尽的 ID；保持去中心化的 ID 组织与元数据共址，引入一致的 ID 缓存，并通过轻量反向映射与过滤机制将物理一致性事件连接至虚拟寻址的 ObjID 缓冲区以支持多核；以软硬件协同实现于 CHERI-Toooba、QEMU、LLVM/Clang 和 CheriBSD。

Result: 在所评估工作负载中，Reinc 显著降低了内存清扫频率与内存隔离开销，同时性能与硬件开销较低。

Conclusion: 通过隔离 ID 而非内存，Reinc 在提供 use-after-free 缓解的同时支持内存即时复用，实现了可扩展、高效的时序内存安全。

Abstract: We propose CHERI-D Reincarnate (Reinc), an architectural extension to CHERI for scalable and efficient temporal memory safety. Prior work CHERI-D has a finite-width generation ID stored at a fixed location, requiring an object to be quarantined when its ID is exhausted. Reinc further provides use-after-free mitigation while permitting immediate freed memory reuse for objects through allocation reincarnation: rather than quarantining an allocation slot upon ID exhaustion, Reinc dynamically assigns a new ID to that slot when its current ID is exhausted. Exhausted IDs are quarantined and later reclaimed, while the underlying memory remains available for immediate reuse. By quarantining IDs rather than memory, Reinc enables continuous reuse of memory in the common case, substantially reducing both memory-sweep frequency and quarantine memory overhead.
  Reinc further introduces coherent ID caching while retaining a fully decentralized ID organization. Temporal metadata remains colocated with the memory it protects, preserving locality while avoiding centralized metadata structures. To support multicore execution, Reinc connects physical coherence events to the virtually addressed ObjID buffer using lightweight reverse-map and filter-based mechanisms.
  We implement Reinc as a hardware-software co-design spanning CHERI-Toooba (superscalar FPGA softcore), QEMU, LLVM/Clang and CheriBSD. Across our evaluated workloads, Reinc substantially reduces memory-sweep frequency and memory quarantine while incurring low performance and hardware overhead.

</details>


### [157] [AccelForge: Comprehensive Modeling and Co-Design Framework for AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11906)
*Tanner Andrulis,Michael Gilbert,Vivienne Sze,Joel S. Emer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出AccelForge，一个用于张量代数负载（如深度神经网络）的加速器建模框架，在能力、速度和易用性上优于现有框架，统一并整合了多项前期工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量代数负载在现代数据中心和边缘部署中能耗巨大，需要加速器来提升能效与吞吐。为快速评估和迭代加速器设计，亟需一个能刻画设备、电路、架构、负载等关键属性并优化负载到硬件映射的建模框架。

Method: AccelForge将多项工作统一到一个框架中，包含三部分：(1) 可组合、用户自定义且可修改的设备、电路与架构模型；(2) 快速映射器，可在数量级更少的（机器与人工）时间内完成准确评估；(3) 易用、易扩展且高性能的Python实现，涵盖模型与映射器，便于快速研究和扩展至新颖优化。

Result: （摘要未给出定量实验结果）框架在能力上整合了多项前期工作，在评估速度上相比现有方法可快数个数量级，并保持了高准确度；同时提供高性能且易扩展的Python实现。

Conclusion: AccelForge在能力、速度和易用性三方面改进了现有加速器建模框架，支持快速研究迭代与向新优化的扩展。

Abstract: Tensor algebra workloads, of which deep neural networks are prominent examples, are energy-intensive workloads in modern datacenter and edge deployments, making accelerators necessary to achieve energy efficiency and high throughput. To quickly evaluate and iterate on accelerator designs, we need an accelerator modeling framework that captures salient attributes of devices, circuits, architectures, workloads, as well as optimizing the mapping of the workload onto the hardware.
  In this paper, we introduce AccelForge, which improves upon existing accelerator modeling frameworks in capabilities, speed, and ease-of-use. AccelForge unifies and multiple works into one framework, and it includes (1) composable user-defined and user-modifiable models of devices, circuits, and architectures, (2) fast mappers that enable accurate evaluation in orders of magnitude less (computer and human) time, and (3) easy-to-use and easy-to-extend, yet still high performance, Python implementations of both the model and mapper to enable rapid research and extension to novel optimizations.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [An Empirical Measurement of Jailbreaking Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10594)
*Yujie Mu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文系统比较了6种越狱攻击评估器（HarmBench、JailbreakBench、JailbreakRadar、StrongReject、JADES、JailMeter），在统一的人类标注数据上以人类判断为参照评估其与人一致性、错误类型及跨攻击家族的稳定性，发现JADES整体表现最佳，HarmBench与StrongReject次之。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家评估越狱响应成本高、难以规模化，社区因而依赖自动评估器判断攻击是否成功。但现有越狱研究通常各自独立验证所选评估器，造成资源重复投入，且结果跨论文难以比较；不同评估器还内嵌不同的越狱成功定义，使报告的攻�击强度与表面进展在很大程度上取决于所使用的评估器。因此有必要在受控条件下对常见评估器进行系统性横向比较。

Method: 选取近期越狱攻击与防御研究中反复出现的6种评估器（HarmBench、JailbreakBench、JailbreakRadar、StrongReject、JADES、JailMeter）。在JailbreakQR与JailMeter-Eva数据集上，以人类判断为参考标准进行评估，度量指标包括与人一致性、错误类型以及跨攻击家族的一致性。对于需要通用大模型评判者的评估器，统一使用共享骨干模型以控制模型特异性差异。据作者所知，此前没有研究在相同人类标注数据、受控设置下对这6种评估器进行过全面评估。

Result: JADES整体表现最佳；HarmBench与StrongReject同样表现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 不同越狱评估器在统一基准下存在可测量的性能差异，评估器选择会实质影响攻击强度与进展的判断；JADES在综合表现上领先，HarmBench和StrongReject为可靠备选。该受控比较有助于减少重复评估成本并提升跨论文结果的可比性。

Abstract: Expert evaluation of jailbreak responses is costly and difficult to scale, so the community increasingly relies on automated evaluators to determine whether an attack succeeds. However, jailbreak studies typically validate their chosen evaluator independently, repeatedly spending resources on similar evaluation efforts while making results across papers difficult to compare. Different evaluators also encode different definitions of jailbreak success, meaning that reported attack strength and apparent progress can depend substantially on which evaluator is used. We systematically compare six evaluators that recur in recent jailbreak attack and defense research: HarmBench, JailbreakBench, JailbreakRadar, StrongReject, JADES, and JailMeter. To our knowledge, no prior study has evaluated all six on the same human-labeled data under a controlled setup. We evaluate them on JailbreakQR and JailMeter-Eva, using human judgments as the reference, and measure agreement with humans, error types, and consistency across attack families. For evaluators that require a general-purpose LLM judge, we use a shared backbone to control for model-specific variation. We found that JADES exhibits the best overall performance, while HarmBench and StrongReject also demonstrate good performance.

</details>


### [159] [Threshold Choice, Not Sample Size, Bounds Trustless Verification of Nondeterministic Compound AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10601)
*Alper Alimoglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文提出一个可在边缘—云—轨道多归属环境中验证非确定性复合AI流水线复现的协议，同时应对非确定输出、节点不诚实与记录间歇可达，并指出关键瓶颈在阈值而非样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI流水线（LLM调用、检索器、工具链）具有非确定性，且跨无单一主体的异构节点执行，中间结果常被丢弃。要在这种场景下验证复现，须同时容忍非确定输出、不可信上报节点与间歇性共享记录访问，而现有工作最多只覆盖其中两项。

Method: 协议对每个阶段的输入、输出与上下文计算摘要并提交，用策略摘要钉住度量与阈值；在不信任执行节点的前提下锚定记录；分区时推迟裁决；挑战由k次重执行的中位数判定，无需法定人数（quorum）。

Result: 在合成HotpotQA流水线上，校准后的固定阈值接受44/45次诚实复现、拒绝104/105对分歧结果，但在k=5时让同输入伪造在27/29次试验中通过；增加采样无益，因为采样只锐化估计而不改变其位置。固定度量与该流水线重执行离散度时，约束瓶颈是阈值而非样本量：按每次执行导出的阈值在零诚实拒绝下检出19/29，最佳常数阈值仅9/29；k=5时不拒绝任何诚实承诺，而k=3时为15次中3次；能捕获15次中11次针对自身构造的攻击者，而该攻击者须瞄准其承诺形成之后才抽取的目标。

Conclusion: 该阈值规则为度量性结果，尚未部署；核心结论是：在给定度量与重执行离散度下，检测能力主要受阈值设计约束，而非重执行样本量。

Abstract: Compound AI pipelines chain LLM calls, retrievers, and tools and are nondeterministic: sampling, model updates, and volatile tool responses make one input yield different outputs across runs. Such pipelines increasingly run across edge, cloud, and orbital nodes owned by no single party, whose optimizations discard intermediate results before inspection. Verifying reproduction there means tolerating nondeterministic outputs, a node that may not report honestly, and intermittent access to any shared record; existing work addresses at most two at once. We give a protocol covering all three: it commits digests of each stage's inputs, outputs, and context under a policy digest pinning the metric and threshold, anchors them without trusting the executing node, defers under partition, and decides a challenge on the median of $k$ re-executions, with no quorum. Where that procedure breaks is the main result. On a synthetic HotpotQA pipeline a calibrated fixed threshold accepts 44 of 45 honest reproductions and rejects 104 of 105 divergent pairs, yet lets same-input fabrication through in 27 of 29 trials at k=5, more samples being no help since sampling sharpens an estimate without moving it. Holding that metric and this pipeline's re-execution spread fixed, the binding constraint is the threshold rather than the sample size: one derived per execution detects 19 of 29 where the best constant matched to the same zero honest rejections reaches 9, rejects no honest commitment at k=5 though 3 of 15 at k=3, and catches 11 of 15 of an attacker built against it, which has to aim at a target drawn only after its commitment exists. That rule is measured rather than deployed.

</details>


### [160] [Adaptive Diffusion Freezing: Privacy-preserving Diffusion Models Against Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10608)
*Jialu Guo,Xiao Han,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出自适应扩散冻结（ADF）框架，通过跨时间步自适应冻结训练与风险感知掩码策略，在扩散模型上实现隐私、效用与效率的更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成任务中表现优异，但其训练过程存在显著隐私风险，尤其易受成员推理攻击（MIA）。已有隐私保护方法难以兼顾隐私、效用与效率，存在明显空白。

Method: ADF采用跨时间步自适应冻结训练，借助掩码矩阵显式控制不同数据子集在扩散各时间步的参与，减少过度记忆，使成员与非成员样本的模型行为更趋一致；并提出基于预训练的风险感知冻结策略，依据记忆倾向估计MIA风险，抑制高风险"子集-时间步"对的贡献。

Result: 在多个数据集上的评估表明，ADF既能提供有效的防御性能，又相较多种基线实现了最优的隐私-效用-效率权衡表现。

Conclusion: ADF通过掩码化的跨时间步自适应冻结与风险感知策略，有效缓解扩散模型的成员隐私泄露，并在隐私、效用与效率之间取得领先的折中，为隐私保护扩散模型提供了新框架。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generative tasks across various areas, however their training process raises significant privacy concerns, particularly under membership inference attacks (MIAs). Prior studies on privacy-preserving of diffusion models fail to balance privacy, utility, and efficiency. To address this gap, we propose a novel framework of privacy-preserving diffusion models, Adaptive Diffusion Freezing (ADF), which can defend against MIAs with better trade-off. By leveraging cross-timestep adaptive freezing training, ADF explicitly control the participation of different data subsets across diffusion timesteps via a mask matrix, which reduces the over-memorization and leads to more uniform model behaviors between member and nonmember samples. To construct a freezing mask matrix that effectively reduce membership leakage without unnecessarily harming generation quality, we introduce a pretraining-based risk-aware freezing policy to estimate MIA risk based on memorization tendency, and suppress the contribution of the subset-timestep pairs with higher risk. Evaluations on multiple datasets demonstrate that ADF provides effective defense performance as well as state-of-the-art privacy-utility-efficiency trade-off performance compared to various baselines.

</details>


### [161] [Black-Box Membership Inference via Word-Level Probability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10611)
*Shengjie Niu,Yeheng Ge,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出WPMIA，一种用于严格黑盒场景的词级概率成员推断攻击，通过蒙特卡洛采样与局部核平滑估计词级生成概率，并构造条件于不同前缀的序列级似然，从而在仅能获取文本续写的专有LLM上实现隐私审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推断攻击大多依赖逐token的logits或概率，无法应用于仅暴露文本续写的专有LLM，该严格黑盒场景研究不足。作者希望在该受限条件下仍能有效审计隐私风险。

Method: WPMIA首先通过蒙特卡洛采样结合局部核平滑估计词级生成概率，再将其聚合为序列级似然估计器；同时在不同前缀条件下构造似然，以放大成员与非成员之间的分布差异。

Result: 在多个开源LLM上，WPMIA持续优于现有黑盒基线；在GPT-5-Chat、Gemini-2.5-Flash和Claude-4.5-Haiku等专有LLM上，平均TPR@5%FPR达到42.0。

Conclusion: WPMIA为严格黑盒成员推断提供了有原则的统计基础，验证了在仅可访问文本续写的专有LLM上进行隐私审计的可行性，并为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) have emerged as critical tools for auditing privacy risks in large language models (LLMs), aiming to determine whether a given text was included in a model's training corpus. However, most existing MIAs require access to per-token logits or probabilities, making them inapplicable in practice to proprietary LLMs that expose only textual continuations. To address this underexplored setting, we propose Word-level Probability MIA (WPMIA), a statistically principled MIA for strict black-box privacy auditing. WPMIA estimates word-level generation probabilities via Monte Carlo sampling with local kernel smoothing, then aggregates these estimates into a sequence-level likelihood estimator. Furthermore, WPMIA constructs the likelihood conditioned on different prefixes, thereby amplifying the distributional differences between members and non-members. We evaluate WPMIA across various open-source LLMs and find that it consistently outperforms existing black-box baselines. Importantly, we also evaluate WPMIA on modern proprietary LLMs, including GPT-5-Chat, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and Claude-4.5-Haiku, achieving an average TPR@5\%FPR of 42.0 across these models. These results offer a sound foundation for future research on strict black-box membership inference. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/niusj03/WPMIA}{https://github.com/niusj03/WPMIA}.

</details>


### [162] [PEARL: A Task-Aware Framework for Evaluating Differentially Private Synthetic Educational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10612)
*Xianghui Meng,Yujing Zhang,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PEARL审计框架，对DP合成教育数据同时校验有效性、隐私、预测效用与任务适配性；96种设定中仅12种通过全部检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化学习依赖真实学习者数据（表现、行为、人口统计），但高度隐私敏感。DP合成数据可兼顾开发与研究并降低个体暴露风险。然而现有评估将隐私与预测效用分开考察，未判断合成数据是否仍适用于既定的个性化学习任务，缺乏任务导向的统一准入标准。

Method: 提出PEARL（Privacy-Equivalence Audit and Release Ledger），仅当DP合成教育数据集同时通过有效性、隐私保护、预测效用与目标任务适配性四类必需检查时才予批准发布，并记录每个被拒数据集的具体失败原因。在96种研究设定下评估（每种由数据集、数据生成方法、隐私预算与随机种子定义），并进行公平性分析与知识追踪模型测试（Deep Knowledge Tracing、Self-Attentive Knowledge Tracing）。

Result: 96种设定中仅12种生成的数据集通过全部适用检查。许多受隐私保护的数据集因缺失重要结果群体（如退课学生）或未能保持学习活动顺序而被拒。公平性分析显示，部分通过隐私与预测效用检查的数据集，在按残障与社经背景划分的群体间仍产生不均衡的学业风险预测性能。DKT与SAKT未能从任何被测合成知识追踪数据中学到有意义的下一作答模式。

Conclusion: 隐私保护本身并不保证合成教育数据对辍学预测、知识追踪或自适应辅导等任务具有可用性；需采用PEARL这类多维任务导向审计，并关注群体公平性与序列结构保留，以决定数据能否放行。

Abstract: Personalized learning systems rely on real learner data, including performance, behavior, and demographic information, but these data are highly privacy-sensitive. Differentially private (DP) synthetic data can support system development and educational research while reducing exposure of individual learners. Existing evaluations, however, assess privacy and predictive usefulness separately, without determining whether synthetic learner data remain usable for the intended personalized learning task. We introduce PEARL (Privacy-Equivalence Audit and Release Ledger), which approves a DP synthetic educational dataset only when it passes all required checks of validity, privacy protection, predictive usefulness, and suitability for the intended educational task, while recording why each rejected dataset fails. Across 96 study settings, each defined by a dataset, data-generation method, privacy budget, and random seed, only 12 produced synthetic datasets that passed all applicable PEARL checks. Many privacy-protected datasets were rejected for omitting important outcome groups, such as withdrawn students, or for failing to preserve the order of learning activities. Fairness analysis further showed that some datasets passing privacy and predictive-usefulness checks still yielded unequal at-risk prediction performance across groups defined by disability and socioeconomic background. Moreover, Deep Knowledge Tracing and Self-Attentive Knowledge Tracing learned no meaningful next-response patterns from any tested synthetic knowledge-tracing dataset, showing that privacy protection alone does not guarantee usefulness for dropout prediction, knowledge tracing, or adaptive tutoring.

</details>


### [163] [Understanding In-Context Multimodal Jailbreaks via Posterior Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10613)
*Xu Zhang,Dev Mistry,Xiang Xu,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出后验重加权框架，将多模态大模型的安全对齐行为建模为竞争行为模式上的后验偏好，把上下文演示视为动态改变安全/有害行为后验的推断时证据，从而形式化ICL越狱的证据累积过程并导出缩放律，并据此设计后验感知的推断时防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习（ICL）越狱揭示了多模态大模型（MLLM）的关键脆弱性：提示中的有害演示无需修改参数即可诱导不安全输出。现有工作虽积累了经验证据，但缺乏对"为何此类越狱能稳定成功"以及"其有效性如何随上下文组成变化"的原理性理解。

Method: 提出后验重加权框架：将安全对齐的MLLM建模为在竞争的行为模式上隐式运作，把上下文演示解释为推断时证据，动态改变模型在安全与有害行为之间的后验偏好；将越狱形式化为证据累积过程，导出关于演示数量、有害比例、对抗强度与语义多样性的预测性缩放律；并基于该框架提出后验感知的推断时防御，按估计风险自适应注入良性反向证据。

Result: 该框架给出了关于演示数量、有害比例、对抗强度与语义多样性的可预测缩放律；所提防御能有效抑制有害后验漂移，同时保持模型效用，在固定干预预算下相较现有上下文防御实现了显著更优的鲁棒性—效用权衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立后验重加权作为一个统一且具预测能力的框架，用于理解并缓解MLLM中的ICL越狱问题。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) jailbreaks reveal a critical vulnerability in multimodal large language models (MLLMs): harmful demonstrations in the prompt can induce unsafe outputs without modifying model parameters. Despite extensive empirical evidence, existing work lacks a principled understanding of why such jailbreaks reliably succeed or how their effectiveness scales with context composition. We propose a posterior reweighting framework that models a safety-aligned MLLM as implicitly operating over competing behavioral modes, and interprets in-context demonstrations as inference-time evidence that dynamically shifts the model's posterior preference between safe and harmful behaviors. This view formalizes jailbreak as a process of evidence accumulation, yielding predictive scaling laws with respect to demonstration count, harmful ratio, adversarial strength, and semantic diversity. Guided by this framework, we introduce a posterior-aware inference-time defense that adaptively injects benign counter-evidence based on estimated risk, effectively suppressing harmful posterior drift while preserving model utility. Compared to existing in-context defenses, our method achieves a significantly improved robustness-utility trade-off under a fixed intervention budget. Together, our results establish posterior reweighting as a unifying and predictive framework for understanding and mitigating ICL jailbreak in MLLMs.

</details>


### [164] [SoK: Privacy Attacks on Machine Learning via Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10627)
*Abdullah Caglar Oksuz,Anisa Halimi,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 系统梳理25项研究，揭示解释可被用于模型窃取、成员推断与模型反演，主张按"获取路径"评估解释隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习解释在提升透明性的同时扩大了攻击面，威胁模型机密性与数据隐私；而现有研究常仅以黑盒/白盒二分标签，掩盖了解释信号传递给攻击者的实际差异，导致风险与防御难以对位评估。

Method: 本文对25项利用解释实施模型提取、成员推断、模型反演（将属性推断视为部分反演）的研究进行系统化综述。核心方法是将"模型知识"与"解释获取"分离，划分五条路径：目标模型主动发布、攻击者自行推导、二次披露、特权访问、发布的全局工件；并沿这些路径比较系统与威胁模型、解释信号、辅助知识、目标模型、模态、查询预算、评价指标、报告性能及防御措施。

Result: 解释可在三条攻击线上降低攻击成本：模型提取成本下降；通过解释统计量、追索距离（recourse distance）与解释引导的鲁棒性暴露成员信号；支持对私有输入的空间或代数重建，从而支撑反演。

Conclusion: 不存在普遍不安全的解释族，也不存在普遍有效的防御。风险取决于暴露的信号、信号的获取方式、所针对的资产以及攻击者已有知识。因此应将解释隐私作为端到端的披露问题来评估，并使防御与具体获取路径及受保护资产相匹配。

Abstract: Machine learning explanations reveal model behavior beyond predictions, creating attack surfaces for model confidentiality and data privacy. We systematize 25 studies that exploit explanations for model extraction, membership inference, and model inversion, treating attribute inference as partial inversion. Existing work is often labeled only black- or white-box, obscuring substantial differences in what explanation signal reaches an adversary. We therefore separate model knowledge from explanation acquisition and identify five paths: target-released, attacker-derived, secondary disclosure, privileged access, and released global artifacts. Across these paths, explanations reduce extraction cost, expose membership signals through explanation statistics, recourse distance, and explanation-guided robustness, and support spatial or algebraic reconstruction of private inputs. We compare system and threat models, explanation signals, auxiliary knowledge, target models, modalities, query budgets, evaluation metrics, reported performance, and defenses. Our analysis shows that no explanation family is uniformly unsafe and no defense is uniformly effective. Risk depends on which signal is exposed, how it is acquired, which asset is targeted, and what the attacker already knows. We argue that explanation privacy should therefore be evaluated as an end-to-end disclosure problem, with defenses matched to the acquisition path and protected asset.

</details>


### [165] [From Cycle Space to Cycle Manifold: Limits and Achievability of Blind False Data Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10631)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在直流支路潮流模型下证明：无噪声正交检验的残差敏感子空间恰为加权环空间，其正交补即完整的隐蔽假数据注入攻击空间；加权环空间知识对完整盲攻击既必要又充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有盲假数据注入攻击（FDIA）方法将测量建模为低秩子空间，仅从代数角度刻画攻击，未阐明使攻击保持隐蔽的物理电网约束，也未给出恢复完整攻击空间所需的最小信息。本文旨在从物理约束角度补足这一理论空白。

Method: 在连通直流（DC）支路潮流模型下，刻画无噪声正交检验的残差敏感子空间，证明其等价于加权环空间；据此给出完整隐蔽攻击空间的构造，并分析其可辨识性（拓扑仅识别至2-同构，环边参数在每个双连通分量内仅识别至一个尺度，桥参数不可识别亦不需要）。随后提出一个计算上无约束的基准方法，以及一个可处理的仅用测量的重构方法。作为紧凑交流（AC）扩展，用环流形刻画可行的支路P/Q测量，并演示GPU上的拓扑辅助流形拟合与测量生成；在无损、定电压、小角度极限下，有功功率切片的正空间退化为DC加权环空间。

Result: 在IEEE系统上的实验，以95%标称接受阈值为基准，比较了BDD绕过率与状态影响；验证了所提重构方法的有效性，并展示了AC扩展中拓扑辅助流形拟合与测量生成的可行性。

Conclusion: 加权环空间为完整盲FDIA提供了必要且充分的物理刻画，明确了攻击隐蔽性的电网约束与最小信息需求；所提基准与仅测量重构方法实用可行，并可推广至AC情形。

Abstract: A false data injection attack (FDIA) can change the estimated grid state while evading a residual-based bad data detector (BDD). Existing blind attacks learn a low-rank measurement subspace, but this algebraic view does not state the physical grid constraints that make an attack stealthy or the minimum information needed to recover the complete attack space. Under the connected direct-current (DC) branch-flow model, we show that the residual-sensitive subspace of the noiseless orthogonal test is exactly the weighted cycle space. Its orthogonal complement is therefore the complete stealthy attack space, making weighted cycle-space knowledge both necessary and sufficient for complete blind FDIA. This space identifies the topology only up to 2-isomorphism and the relative cycle-edge parameters only up to one scale per biconnected component; bridge parameters are neither identified nor required. We then formulate a computationally unconstrained benchmark and a tractable measurement-only reconstruction method. Experiments on IEEE systems compare BDD bypass rate at a 95% nominal-acceptance threshold against state impact. As a compact alternating-current (AC) extension, we characterize feasible branch P/Q measurements by a cycle manifold and demonstrate topology-assisted manifold fitting and measurement generation on a graphics processing unit (GPU). In the lossless fixed-voltage small-angle limit, the normal space of the active-power slice reduces to the DC weighted cycle space.

</details>


### [166] [HermiCache: Enclave-Aware Cache Replacement for Trusted Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10634)
*Oussama Elmnaouri,Pascal Cotret,Vianney Lapôtre,Loïc Lagadec*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 HermiCache，一种面向 RISC-V 的缓存侧信道防护方案，在 OpenHwGroup CVA6 核与 Keystone TEE 上实现，支持细粒度配置与确定性保护，面积开销仅 6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE 虽能保护 enclave 内存不被不可信软件访问，但因微架构资源共享，仍面临基于缓存的侧信道攻击。现有对策（缓存分区、随机化等）在需要细粒度配置和确定性保护时不理想。

Method: 提出 HermiCache，面向 RISC-V 核设计，并在集成 Keystone TEE 的 OpenHwGroup CVA6 核上实现（软件层使用 Keystone TEE）。

Result: 在处理器核上的面积开销为 6%。

Conclusion: HermiCache 满足细粒度配置与确定性保护的需求，且面积开销可接受（6%），是缓存侧信道防护的一种可行方案。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) protect enclave memory from untrusted software but remain vulnerable to cache-based side-channel attacks due to shared microarchitectural resources. Existing countermeasures use techniques such as cache partitioning or randomization: these solutions are not ideal if a designer wants fine-grained configurations and a deterministic protection. In this paper, we introduce HermiCache which is an answer to these requirements. HermiCache is designed for RISC-V cores and has been implemented in the OpenHwGroup CVA6 core with a Keystone TEE for the software layer. The solution has an area overhead of 6% on the processor core.

</details>


### [167] [Architecting the Secure AI-SOC: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Pipeline Integrity and Threat Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10707)
*Anna Gazani,Spyridon Kounoupidis,Panagiotis Katsaros,Nikolaos Kekatos,Grigorios Tsoumakas,Georgios Koutidis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号纵深防御架构，通过SIEM解码器确定性预过滤与NeMo Guardrails语义校验的双层机制，结合闭环遥测与HITL可见性，有效阻断面向AI-SOC的日志投毒间接提示注入"promptware"杀伤链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被引入安全运营中心以提升威胁情报处理效率，但引入了经由日志投毒的间接提示注入漏洞。攻击者可在系统日志中嵌入恶意载荷，劫持LLM的操作逻辑，形成多步"promptware"杀伤链。现有防御存在两难：确定性防御计算高效但语义盲区大，纯神经网络评估则延迟过高且具概率性缺陷。

Method: 提出神经符号纵深防御架构。第一层以定制化SIEM解码器作为确定性预过滤器，在数据摄取边缘进行结构化净化，消除体量填充与基于签名的注入；第二层采用NeMo Guardrails，在LLM处理前对结构化SIEM告警进行自检式语义边界校验。框架还集成闭环遥测系统，在SOC仪表盘内提供对已阻断攻击的Human-in-the-Loop可见性。

Result: 实验评估映射至MITRE ATLAS分类体系，针对多样化提示注入进行测试。结果表明该协同方法可有效瓦解promptware杀伤链，以可验证约束限定LLM随机性，为新一代AI-SOC提供具韧性且高可观测性的防御机制。

Conclusion: 神经符号纵深防御可在确定性与语义理解之间取得平衡，兼顾低延迟与可验证性，是保障LLM驱动SOC流水线端到端完整性的可行路径。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into Security Operations Centers (SOCs) streamlines threat intelligence but introduces critical vulnerabilities, notably indirect prompt injection via log poisoning. Adversaries exploit this vector to execute multistep ``promptware'' kill chains by embedding malicious payloads within system logs to hijack the LLM's operational logic. Securing this pipeline presents a dichotomy: deterministic defenses are computationally efficient yet semantically blind, while purely neural evaluations introduce prohibitive latency and probabilistic flaws. To address this, we propose a novel neurosymbolic defense-in-depth architecture that ensures end-to-end pipeline integrity. The primary layer employs customized SIEM decoders as a deterministic pre-filter, performing immediate structural sanitization to neutralize volumetric padding and signature-based injections at the ingestion edge. The secondary layer leverages NeMo Guardrails to enforce strict semantic boundaries through self-checking validation on the structured SIEM alerts prior to LLM processing. Furthermore, the framework integrates a closed-loop telemetry system, providing critical Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) visibility into thwarted attacks directly within the SOC dashboard. We present a comprehensive experimental evaluation mapped to the MITRE ATLAS taxonomy, assessing the framework against diverse prompt injections. Our results demonstrate that this synergistic approach effectively dismantles the promptware kill chain - bounding LLM stochasticity with verifiable constraints, and delivering a resilient, highly observable defense mechanism for next-generation AI-SOCs.

</details>


### [168] [PHAT: PHotonic Accelerator for TFHE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11613)
*Guowei Yang,Farbin Fayza,Beren Aydoğan,Carlos A. Ríos Ocampo,Ayse K. Coskun,Ajay Joshi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PHAT，一种基于光寻址相变存储器（OPCM）的光子加速器，用于加速TFHE全同态加密中的FFT运算，实现了2.14–5.10倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE支持在加密数据上进行安全计算，适用于云端的隐私保护应用；其中TFHE支持任意操作，但自举阶段的FFT运算计算与通信开销高，传统电子加速器受工艺缩放限制和内存墙问题影响难以达到足够吞吐量。OPCM的存内计算系统具有高计算与通信吞吐量，适合加速FFT，但直接将FFT映射到OPCM面临高精度模拟计算以及OPCM单元编程的高延迟和高能耗挑战。

Method: 提出PHAT——一种利用光寻址相变存储器（OPCM）的光子加速器，采用面向OPCM优化的电光混合加速器架构：包含基于OPCM的FFT单元、为OPCM定制的twiddle-stationary数据流，以及最大化FFT单元利用率的调度机制。

Result: 在四个真实TFHE工作负载上，PHAT相较于最先进的ASIC加速器实现了2.14–5.10倍的加速。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提升了TFHE应用的性能，为云计算中实用高效的同态加密铺平了道路。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables secure computation on encrypted data, making it a promising solution for privacy-preserving applications in the cloud. Among various FHE schemes, FHE over the Torus (TFHE) stands out due to its support for arbitrary operations. However, its high computation and communication overhead, particularly in the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) operations required during bootstrapping, limits its practicality for real-world applications. Conventional electronic accelerators struggle to achieve sufficient throughput due to the limitations of technology scaling and the memory-wall problem.
  To address these challenges, we propose PHAT, a PHotonic Accelerator for TFHE leveraging Optically-addressed Phase-Change Memory (OPCM). OPCM-based processing-in-memory systems offer high computation and communication throughput, making them well-suited for accelerating FFT operations in TFHE. However, directly mapping FFT to OPCM presents challenges such as high-precision analog computation and the high latency and energy cost of programming OPCM cells. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel electro-photonic accelerator architecture optimized for TFHE, featuring OPCM-based FFT units, a twiddle-stationary dataflow tailored for OPCM, and a scheduling mechanism to maximize the utilization of the FFT units. PHAT delivers $2.14\times$--$5.10\times$ speedup across four real-world TFHE workloads against the state-of-the-art ASIC accelerator. Our approach significantly enhances the performance of TFHE applications, paving the way for practical and efficient homomorphic encryption in cloud computing.

</details>


### [169] [CARTS: Contextual Autoregressive Rank Transcoding Steganography for Full-Capacity Keyed Text Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10744)
*Wissam Ghantous,Alexander V. Mantzaris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对CARTS（上下文自回归秩转码隐写）进行严格的形式化安全分析，定义相关安全概念与计算问题并研究其理论关系，最后在Llama 3 8B上进行实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Norelli等人的Calgacus构造实验性地展示了借助自回归语言模型保持逐位置秩信息、将载荷文本转换为等token长度隐写文本的现象，但缺乏形式化安全分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在确定性模型假设下证明CARTS的精确正确性；引入秩坐标表示，使密钥成为秩向量空间上的双射；定义上下文搜索、密钥碰撞、消息歧义与编码映射非交换性等安全概念与计算问题，并研究其理论关系（如用上下文搜索刻画消息歧义、密钥碰撞与消息歧义之间的张力）；在Llama 3 8B上开展实证研究。

Result: Llama 3 8B实验中所有测试案例均精确恢复原始载荷；随机密钥生成下未发现密钥碰撞；人工构造的碰撞被证明是局部而非全局的；未发现可交换密钥对，表明对所研究的攻击向量具有抵抗力。

Conclusion: 本工作为语言模型在密码学与隐私保护通信中的建设性应用开辟了具有形式化基础的研究议程。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models can be used to transform a payload text into a stegotext of identical token length by preserving per-position rank information across contexts - a methodology we formalize as Contextual Autoregressive Rank Transcoding Steganography (CARTS). While the Calgacus construction of Norelli et al. demonstrated this phenomenon experimentally, no formal security analysis existed. This paper provides the first rigorous treatment of CARTS. We show its exact correctness under deterministic model assumptions, introduce a rank-coordinate representation in which keys act as bijections on rank-vector space, define relevant security notions and the computational problems naturally associated with the construction - context search, key collisions, message equivocation, and non-commutativity of the encoding maps - and study the theoretical relationships between them, including the characterization of message equivocation in terms of context search, and the tension between key collisions and message equivocation. An empirical study on Llama 3 8B confirms exact recovery of the original payload in all tested cases, finds no key collisions under random key generation, establishes that a hand-crafted collision is local rather than global, and finds no commuting key pairs - suggesting resistance to the attack vectors studied. This work opens a formally grounded research agenda for the constructive use of language models in cryptography and privacy-preserving communication.

</details>


### [170] [Temporal and Multimodal Deep Learning for Cyberattack Detection in LEO Satellite Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10746)
*Kyle Stein,Guillermo Francia,Eman El-Sheikh,Hossain Shahriar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于深度学习的地球低轨（LEO）卫星网络攻击检测，利用卫星专用UNSW-IoTSAT数据集，提出子系统融合MLP与分层多模态Transformer，并强调防泄漏与跨卫星泛化评估，分层Transformer最高达91.66%准确率和85.63%宏F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星通信系统日益重要，其跨射频链路、星载硬件与轨道操作产生异构信息，攻击检测比传统网络入侵更复杂。现有方法多依赖地面入侵数据集，或独立评估单条观测，难以刻画LEO卫星特有的时序攻击行为。

Method: 基于新发布的卫星专用UNSW-IoTSAT数据集开展系统性深度学习攻击检测研究；设计保留硬件、轨道与射频信息的结构化学习架构，包括子系统融合MLP和分层多模态Transformer（建模跨子系统交互与时序演化）；并构建防泄漏的行级与时序评估设置及跨卫星泛化评估，以分析模型架构与评估协议的影响。

Result: 实验表明结构化多模态建模与严格评估具有价值；在防泄漏评估协议下，分层Transformer最高取得91.66%准确率与85.63%宏F1。

Conclusion: 结构化多模态建模结合严谨的评估协议能有效提升LEO卫星网络攻击检测性能，模型架构与评估设置共同决定检测效果。

Abstract: The growing reliance on Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite communication systems has increased the need for intelligent methods capable of detecting cyberattacks across complex and dynamic space environments. Unlike conventional network intrusion detection, satellite systems generate heterogeneous information across radio-frequency (RF) links, onboard hardware, and orbital operations. However, many existing approaches either rely on terrestrial intrusion datasets or evaluate individual observations independently, limiting their ability to capture temporal attack behavior specific to LEO satellites. In this work, we conduct a systematic study of deep-learning-based cyberattack detection using the recently introduced satellite-specific UNSW-IoTSAT dataset. We investigate structured learning architectures that preserve hardware, orbital, and RF information, including a Subsystem-Fusion MLP and a hierarchical multimodal Transformer that models both cross-subsystem interactions and temporal evolution. We further evaluate leakage-resistant row-level and temporal settings, along with cross-satellite generalization, to characterize how model architecture and evaluation protocol influence satellite cyberattack detection. Experimental results demonstrate the value of structured multimodal modeling and rigorous evaluation, with the hierarchical Transformer achieving up to 91.66% accuracy and 85.63% macro F1 under the leakage-resistant evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [171] [Beyond Static Guarantees: Measuring the Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail Gap in Security-Sensitive and LLM-Generated Python Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10762)
*Jessica Pourleyli,Maitreyee Das Urmi,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"静态通过、动态失败"(SPDF)现象，并设计静态扫描+LLM的CWE推理+Docker隔离环境自主漏洞验证的三阶段流水线，在1,355个Python样本上发现约1/7静态干净样本实际可被利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析因可扩展、可复现、低成本而被广泛用作安全门禁，但无法直接观测运行时利用行为；依赖对抗输入、执行上下文或漏洞链的可利用缺陷可能绕过静态检查，然而"通过静态分析"常被等同于"行为安全"，这种假设需要被检验。

Method: 提出SPDF现象与三阶段智能体流水线：(1) 复合Bandit-Semgrep静态扫描；(2) LLM驱动的CWE推理识别候选漏洞；(3) 在隔离Docker容器中进行自主漏洞利用验证。在SecurityEval、RedCode、CyberNative三个数据集的1,355个Python样本上评估。

Result: 在654个Bandit-Semgrep无告警样本中，LLM阶段于235个文件识别出394个候选漏洞；动态验证确认或部分确认95个文件可利用，流水线整体比率14.53%（约1/7静态干净样本）。按数据集，候选文件-CWE对的确认率分别为RedCode 33.7%、CyberNative 28.6%、SecurityEval 5.4%。CWE-338、CWE-916等高频确认类别未被Bandit和Semgrep告警。

Conclusion: 静态分析通过与运行时安全是软件保障中具有层级关系的不同层次，而非可互换的度量；该结论有望改变AI生成代码与安全敏感代码的评估方式。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) fuel the quest for scalable methods to assess the security of generated and security-sensitive software. Static analysis is widely adopted as a scalable, reproducible, and inexpensive security gate, but cannot directly observe runtime exploit behaviour. Vulnerabilities dependent on adversarial inputs, execution context, or exploit chaining may evade static checks while remaining exploitable in practice, yet passing static analysis is often treated as evidence of secure behaviour. This paper introduces the Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail (SPDF) phenomenon and a three-stage agentic pipeline combining static scanning, LLM-driven Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) reasoning, and autonomous exploit verification in isolated Docker containers. We evaluate 1,355 Python samples from SecurityEval, RedCode, and CyberNative datasets. Of the 654 samples producing no findings under the composite Bandit-Semgrep gate, the LLM detection stage identified 394 candidate vulnerabilities across 235 files. Dynamic verification confirmed or partially confirmed exploitability in 95 files, yielding an inclusive pipeline rate of 14.53% (roughly 1 in 7 statically clean samples). This rate represents the proportion of Bandit-Semgrep-clean samples for which the pipeline identified a candidate vulnerability and obtained runtime evidence supporting exploitability. Outcomes varied by dataset: among candidate file--CWE pairs, confirmed exploitability was 33.7% for RedCode, 28.6% for CyberNative, and 5.4% for SecurityEval. Several frequently confirmed classes, including CWE-338 and CWE-916, were flagged by neither Bandit nor Semgrep. These findings indicate that static-analysis success and runtime security are hierarchical layers of software assurance rather than interchangeable measures, and have the potential to reshape how AI-generated and security-sensitive code is evaluated.

</details>


### [172] [Big Enough to Break Out: Tracking the Rising Capability of LLM Penetration-Testing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10780)
*Victoria Lovelace,Cameron Berryman,Yuhan You,Suhas Reddy Adavelly,Joel Sadler,Daniel Graham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对比两套基于PentestGPT的系统（Kimi K2.5人在回路 vs Claude Opus 4.8全自主），自主系统在三个靶机上全部通关，旧系统两个未通关但各完成约一半子任务；加入覆盖记忆层均无改善，瓶颈在于规划与承诺而非记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在渗透测试中应用渐广，但对其能力边界与失败机制认识不足；同时学界常将长程任务的瓶颈归因于长时记忆丢失，需实证检验这一假设。

Method: 在三个公开靶标上对比两套PentestGPT派生系统：旧版人在回路运行开源权重Kimi K2.5（普通大学GPU、无供应商护栏），新版全自主运行Claude Opus 4.8；随后为两套系统均加装覆盖记忆层（coverage-memory layer），并以细粒度子任务评分衡量能力。

Result: 自主系统三靶全解，包括旧系统始终未能完成的两个；旧系统虽未通关，仍完成约半数子任务。加装记忆层后两者成绩均无提升；在可复盘的旧系统停滞运行中，瓶颈表现为规划与承诺（已掌握前进证据却未转为具体利用假设），而非记忆丢失。

Conclusion: 因模型、框架、自主性与记忆架构同时变化，只能描述趋势而无法归因；方向性启示是进攻能力随规划能力而非记忆提升。该子任务评分同样可供防御方使用，以在威胁落地前持续度量并跟踪对手能力增长。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to penetration testing, but we still know little about what they can do or how they fail. We compare two PentestGPT-based systems: a legacy human-in-the-loop system running the open-weight Kimi K2.5, and a newer autonomous system running Claude Opus 4.8. Across three public targets, the autonomous system solves all three, including the two the legacy system never finishes. The legacy result is the more surprising of the two. Even on the machines the legacy system fails to solve, it completes about half the subtasks, while running on ordinary university GPUs with no provider guardrails. We can describe the trend but not explain it, since model, harness, autonomy, and memory architecture all change together. Its direction still points to the next question: what will limit these agents as they take on more complex tasks? The usual answer is long-horizon memory, the loss of access to earlier findings during long attack chains. We test it by adding a coverage-memory layer to both systems, and neither improves outcomes. In the legacy stalled runs we could review, the limiting factor appeared to be planning and commitment rather than lost memory: agents held the evidence for a route forward and never turned it into a concrete exploitation hypothesis, which may suggest that offensive capability will advance with agents' ability to plan rather than with better memory. The same subtask scoring that tracks this capability is available to defenders, who can measure it as it rises instead of waiting to meet it in the field.

</details>


### [173] [No-Box Vulnerability Analysis: Description-only Detection of Indirect Prompt Injection Vulnerabilities in MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10854)
*Zehua Zhang,Jie Hu,Pratham Hegde,Aditya Maheshbhai Gabani,Souradip Nath,Yibo Liu,Siyu Liu,Hongkai Chen,Hulin Wang,Zhuoer Lyu,Chang Zhu,Divij Handa,Yan Shoshitaishvili,Tiffany Bao,Ruoyu Wang,Adam Doupe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"无盒(no-box)漏洞分析"新范式：在既无系统访问权限也无运行时交互、仅能获取功能元数据的条件下，推断对所有可能实现都成立的漏洞假设，并以MCPSEC原型对MCP服务器进行间接提示注入漏洞审计，验证了该范式的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统漏洞分析依赖系统访问(白盒)或动态交互(黑盒/灰盒)，但第三方分析者在审计闭源、远程托管的关键在役系统或商业受限软件时，往往两者皆不可得。因此需要一种在仅有功能元数据时可用的新分析范式。

Method: 提出no-box漏洞分析：仅利用功能元数据(定义系统预期行为，包括输入、输出与副作用，并约束与其行为一致的实现空间)，在不观察、不交互目标系统的前提下，假设出存在于该元数据所有可能实现中的漏洞；分析者可在后续获得更多访问权限时再验证这些假设。实现原型MCPSEC，仅依据服务器注册时暴露的工具元数据，审计Model Context Protocol (MCP)服务器的间接提示注入漏洞。

Result: 在20个广泛部署的MCP服务器(共177个工具)上评估MCPSEC，人工评估确认95个工具存在漏洞。MCPSEC判定143个工具为易受攻击，并为每个易受攻击工具生成漏洞假设及利用技术。仅凭元数据，MCPSEC预测出94个(98.9%召回率)真实且已验证的漏洞，对比LLM基线为80个(84.2%召回率)。

Conclusion: 结果确立了no-box漏洞分析作为一种新的分析范式，并证明其在真实系统中的实际可行性。

Abstract: Conventional vulnerability analysis relies on either system access or dynamic interaction, all of which may be unavailable to third-party analysts auditing closed-source, remotely hosted, critical in situ systems, or commercially gated software. Therefore, we propose a new paradigm of no-box vulnerability analysis in which neither access nor runtime interaction is available, and only functionality metadata is available. Such metadata defines the intended behavior of the system, including its inputs, outputs, and side effects, while constraining the space of implementations consistent with that behavior. We propose hypothesizing about vulnerabilities that exist across all possible implementations of a given system metadata, without observing or interacting with the target system. An analyst can later validate these hypotheses when additional access is available. We showcase the feasibility of no-box vulnerability analysis through implementing a prototype called MCPSEC, which audits Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers for indirect prompt injection vulnerabilities using only the tool metadata exposed at server registration time. We evaluate MCPSEC on 20 widely deployed MCP servers comprising 177 tools, among which human evaluators confirm 95 vulnerable tools. MCPSEC identified 143 tools as vulnerable, and for each vulnerable tool, it produced a hypothesized vulnerability along with exploitation technique. Using metadata alone, MCPSEC predicted 94 (98.9% recall) real verified vulnerabilities, compared against an LLM baseline with 80 (84.2% recall). Overall, our results introduce no-box vulnerability analysis as a new analysis paradigm and demonstrate its practical feasibility in realistic systems.

</details>


### [174] [A2ABreak: Systematic Security Analysis of the A2A Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10871)
*Alireza Lotfi,Mirza Masfiqur Rahman,Imtiaz Karim,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出A2ABreak，首个针对A2A（Agent2Agent）智能体互操作协议的严格系统性安全分析框架：通过LLM辅助从自然语言规范中抽取经校验的有限状态机（37状态、76迁移，源自929条形式化陈述），在完全合规假设下进行对抗性验证，发现11个仅凭规范即可利用的新漏洞，精确率73.3%、F1为84.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A2A协议由Linux Foundation治理，是跨组织边界实现自主AI智能体发现、认证与任务委派的开源标准，并与MCP互补，正快速成为多智能体生态的横向通信层。然而该协议的安全性此前缺乏系统性分析，存在明显研究空白。

Method: A2ABreak框架：利用LLM辅助从协议自然语言规范中抽取经过验证的有限状态机，从929条形式化陈述中构建包含37个状态、76个迁移的统一模型；随后在该模型上进行系统性推理，通过对抗性验证在完全合规假设下挖掘协议级漏洞。

Result: 发现11个新漏洞，均可由符合规范的对手利用，无需任何实现缺陷。典型问题包括：通过未受保护的上下文标识符实现跨客户端上下文注入；委派链中多跳身份丢失导致凭据窃取；恶意智能体通过未 attested 的能力声明进行数据外泄。与独立专家评审对比，A2ABreak精确率73.3%、F1为84.6%；在同规范上运行的零样本LLM基线产生零条确认发现。

Conclusion: A2A协议存在仅凭规范合规即可触发的协议级安全缺陷，说明标准设计本身需要强化。显式形式化建模是可靠协议安全分析的必要条件，仅依赖零样本LLM不足以发现真实漏洞。A2ABreak为智能体互操作协议的安全评估提供了首个系统化方法。

Abstract: The Agent2Agent (A2A) protocol, now governed by the Linux Foundation, is an open standard that enables autonomous AI agents to discover, authenticate with, and delegate tasks to one another across organizational boundaries. Designed to complement the Model Context Protocol (MCP) for tool integration, A2A is rapidly emerging as the horizontal communication layer of the multi-agent ecosystem. Yet the protocol's security has received no systematic analysis.
  This paper presents A2ABreak, the first rigorous systematic security analysis of the A2A protocol. We introduce a novel framework that utilizes an LLM-assisted extraction of a verified finite-state machine directly from the natural-language specification, producing a unified model of 37 states and 76 transitions from 929 formalized statements, and then systematically reasons over this model to discover protocol-level vulnerabilities through adversarial verification, under a full-compliance assumption.
  Our analysis uncovers 11 new vulnerabilities, each exploitable by a specification-compliant adversary without requiring any implementation flaw. Among the findings are cross-client context injection through unprotected context identifiers, credential harvesting via multi-hop identity loss in delegation chains, and data exfiltration through rogue agents advertising unattested capability claims.
  A2ABreak achieves 73.3% precision and 84.6% F1 against independent expert review, while a zero-shot LLM baseline operating over the same specification produces zero confirmed findings, demonstrating that explicit formal grounding is essential for sound protocol security analysis.

</details>


### [175] [AspisAI: A Canonical, Machine-Interpretable Governance Framework for Automated Multi-Standard Compliance Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10881)
*Tsafac Nkombong Regine Cyrille,Hasan Dag,Reiner Creutzburg,Knut Haufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AspisAI——一个标准无关、有界合规治理框架，将多标准要求映射为规范化机器可解释控制模型，并用条件决策规则评估证据，实现可解释、可追溯的合规判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管与关键基础设施组织需同时满足ISO/IEC 27001、NIST CSF 2.0、Cyber Essentials、GDPR等异构网络安全与隐私要求；现有实践依赖人工映射、表格跟踪与周期性审计，成本高、跨标准不一致、可追溯性弱。

Method: 在26项代表性要求的有界范围内，将多个框架的选定要求翻译为规范化、机器可解释的控制模型，基于条件决策规则评估提交的证据，生成可解释、可追溯的合规判定；在受控仿真中按五项治理导向准则评估，并使用两个外部参照点（NIST公开信息性引用、OpenSSF Scorecard真实第三方证据）校验。

Result: 跨标准映射与NIST公开信息性引用相比达57%完全一致，分歧限于同族控制；应用于OpenSSF Scorecard真实第三方证据时发现实际开源项目中的真实治理缺口。受控结果包含完整要求编码、88.5%映射覆盖率、完整可追溯性，并正确检测出全部引入缺口。

Conclusion: 规范化、保留溯源的治理模型可使多标准合规同时具备可自动化与可审计性，并证明功能正确性及仿真之外的适用性。

Abstract: Organisations operating in regulated and critical-infrastructure sectors must satisfy multiple, heterogeneous cybersecurity and privacy instruments simultaneously, including but not limited to ISO/IEC~27001, the NIST Cybersecurity Framework~2.0, Cyber Essentials, and the GDPR. In practice, these obligations are managed through manual mappings, spreadsheet-based tracking, and periodic audits that are costly to maintain, inconsistent across standards, and weak in traceability. This paper presents \emph{AspisAI}, a bounded, standard-agnostic governance framework that translates selected requirements from several frameworks into a canonical, machine-interpretable control model, and evaluates submitted evidence against condition-based decision rules to produce explainable, traceable compliance determinations. Within a bounded scope of 26 representative requirements, the framework is evaluated in a controlled simulation against five governance-oriented criteria and, critically, against two external reference points that mitigate the circularity of single-author evaluation: its cross-standard mappings are validated against NIST's own published informative references, with 57\,\% exact agreement and divergences confined to same-family controls, and the framework is applied to real third-party evidence from the OpenSSF Scorecard, surfacing genuine governance gaps in a live open-source project. The controlled results, comprising full requirement encoding, 88.5\,\% mapping coverage, complete traceability, and correct detection of all introduced gaps, establish functional correctness, while the external validation provides evidence of applicability beyond the simulation. The contribution is therefore a demonstration that a canonical, provenance-preserving governance model can render multi-standard compliance both automatable and auditable.

</details>


### [176] [DriftNet: A Dual-Head Trajectory Transformer for Detecting and Localizing Prompt Injection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10892)
*Asif Pinjari,Mithun Paul Saint-Germain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DriftNet：双头轨迹 Transformer，单次前向同时完成轨迹级攻击判定与逐步四类标签标注，在 AgentDrift 上轨迹 F1 达 0.983。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
