Motivation: 间接提示注入攻击成功后，痕迹体现在智能体自身行为中：良性工具调用前缀、被投毒observation、服务攻击者的动作后缀。运维需要三个事实：攻击从何进入、哪些步骤被污染、疑似投毒是否被抵御。现有系统只能给出整条轨迹判决或单一不安全索引，无法联合回答。

Method: DriftNet 为双头轨迹 Transformer，读取日志化工具调用轨迹一次前向完成三问：一头做轨迹级受损/未受损二分类，另一头为每步打四类标签（benign、injection point、hijacked、failed injection）。步骤嵌入由冻结句编码器与四个身份无关的 world 特征构成；训练主干不足两百万参数，使用双头类加权联合目标，无需访问智能体模型。在 AgentDrift 任务不相交划分（12,536 条轨迹、71,024 个标注步骤）上评估，20 组配置扫描将超参敏感性限定在 0.011 F1，测试集仅评估一次。

Result: 轨迹级 F1 0.983；98.7% 受攻击轨迹精确恢复注入点；hijacked 跨度 IoU 0.979；218 次被抵御攻击零误报；困难负样本误报率 2.9%。同划分重训的表面基线仅恢复 11.1% 部分劫持与 17.1% 延迟执行，DriftNet 分别达 98.6% 与 93.2%，且各项误报率更低。26 个残余错误多为标注注入 observation 不含可读指令的轨迹，并同时报告基准测得的 world 身份规律性。

Conclusion: DriftNet 是首个产出轨迹判决与逐步四类标签联合输出的监督检测器，以轻量、模型无关方式为运维提供攻击入口定位、污染范围与抵御识别三项可操作信息。

Abstract: When an indirect prompt injection succeeds against an LLM agent, the compromise is visible in the agent's own behavior: a benign prefix of tool calls, a poisoned observation, and a suffix of actions that serve the attacker. An operator needs three facts: where the attack entered, which steps it corrupted, and whether apparent poison was resisted. Existing systems return either a whole-trace verdict or a single unsafe index. We present DriftNet, a dual-head trajectory Transformer that reads a logged tool-call trajectory and answers all three questions in one forward pass: one head classifies the trajectory as compromised or not, and a second assigns every step one of four labels (benign, injection point, hijacked, failed injection). To our knowledge it is the first supervised detector to produce this joint output. A frozen sentence encoder and four identity-free world features embed each step; the trained trunk, under two million parameters and optimized with a class-weighted joint objective over both heads, needs no access to the agent's model. On the task-disjoint split of the AgentDrift benchmark (12,536 trajectories, 71,024 labeled steps), with a 20-configuration sweep bounding hyperparameter sensitivity to 0.011 F1 and the test part evaluated exactly once, DriftNet reaches trajectory-level F1 of 0.983, exact injection-point recovery on 98.7% of attacked trajectories, hijacked-span IoU of 0.979, zero flags on 218 resisted attacks, and 2.9% flags on hard negatives. A surface baseline retrained on the identical split recovers 11.1% of partial hijacks and 17.1% of delayed executions; DriftNet reaches 98.6% and 93.2% while lowering every false-alarm rate. Reading all 26 residual errors shows that most misses trace to trajectories whose labeled injection observation carries no legible instruction, and we report the benchmark's measured world-identity regularity alongside the results.

</details>


### [177] [You've Got a BUD in Me: Authenticated Reads from Per-Block Write Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11251)
*Alejandro Ranchal-Pedrosa,Cody Littley,Ben Marsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出用区块写入认证（BUD/SuperBUD）替代全状态结构，实现历史成员/非成员证明，证明规模随间隔对数增长，性能随状态膨胀的退化远小于Merkle Patricia Trie。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统区块链通过维护覆盖全状态的认证结构来支持已认证读取，导致状态膨胀时证明与验证成本显著上升，随状态规模增长退化严重。论文希望改为只认证每个区块的写入，从而在状态增大时仍保持低成本的历史成员与排除证明。

Method: 提出Block Update Digest（BUD），以写入日志为承诺，日志中的前驱指针把同一键的连续修改串联成链；用SuperBUD汇总一个窗口内的最后写入；再用指数层级（exponential hierarchy）把长未变更区间压缩成短证明。摘要规模随层级范围内的间隔呈对数增长，超出范围时每个顶层窗口额外增加一个摘要。并给出针对恶意证明者及至多f个拜占庭验证者的可靠性证明，以及在归档、证明/见证（attestation）与委员会证据可用前提下、由部署后修改锚定查询的完备性。

Result: 在状态规模扩大50倍的实验中，base-BUD路径成本仅上升1.24倍，而内存版与受缓存限制的磁盘版Merkle Patricia Trie分别上升3.1倍与69.5倍。合成轨迹下，两摘要读取层载荷保持在800字节以内，热哈希路径验证在p99不超过146微秒。

Conclusion: 基于写入认证的BUD/SuperBUD层级方案能在状态大幅增长时保持稳定的证明规模与验证延迟，显著优于Merkle Patricia Trie，并在一定假设下具备可靠性与完备性保证。

Abstract: Blockchains usually pay for authenticated reads by maintaining a structure that spans the entire state. We show how validators can support historical membership and exclusion proofs by authenticating each block's writes instead. A Block Update Digest (BUD) commits a write log whose predecessor pointers link successive modifications of each key. A SuperBUD summarizes last writes over a window; an exponential hierarchy turns long unchanged intervals into short proofs. The digest count is logarithmic in the gap within the hierarchy's range, with one additional digest per top-level window beyond it. We prove soundness against adversarial provers and up to f Byzantine validators, and completeness for queries anchored by a post-deployment modification, assuming archive, attestation, and committee evidence is available. Across a 50x increase in state size, the measured base-BUD path rises by 1.24x, compared with 3.1x and 69.5x for in-memory and cache-bounded disk-backed Merkle Patricia tries. On the synthetic trace, two-digest read-layer payloads stay below 800 bytes, and warm hash-path verification takes at most 146 microseconds at p99.

</details>


### [178] [Empirical Evaluation of Membership Inference Attacks on NLP Text Classifiers: A Baseline Study on SST-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10935)
*William Novak,Muhammad Abusaqer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在GLUE SST-2情感分类任务上，该文对TF-IDF+逻辑回归与微调DistilBERT开展了成员推断攻击（MIA）的受控基准测试，并验证了两种缓解措施对隐私-效用权衡的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP模型的训练数据常包含敏感用户文本，成员推断攻击会判断某条记录是否被用于训练，构成隐私风险；但现有研究缺乏对文本分类中不同模型复杂度、不同防御手段下MIA脆弱性的统一可控比较。

Method: 在GLUE SST-2数据集上，对比TF-IDF+逻辑回归流水线与微调DistilBERT分类器；采用基于损失阈值的MIA（loss-threshold MIA）；用验证集准确率和宏F1衡量效用，用攻击AUC衡量泄漏；测试两种缓解措施：更强的正则化，以及将DistilBERT微调轮数由3轮减为2轮。

Result: DistilBERT达到0.9466准确率与0.9460宏F1，逻辑回归为0.8756与0.8727；两模型均泄漏成员信号，攻击AUC分别为0.5615和0.5800。更强正则化降低了逻辑回归的泄漏但带来明显的效用损失；将微调轮数从3降至2可在准确率损失可忽略的情况下降低DistilBERT的泄漏。

Conclusion: 模型效用更高并不必然意味着隐私泄漏更严重；轻量级的训练调整（如减少微调轮数）即可改善隐私-效用权衡，无需依赖复杂防御机制。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) try to determine whether a specific record was used to train a model, a privacy risk that matters in natural language processing (NLP), where training data can contain sensitive user text. This paper presents a controlled benchmark of membership inference vulnerability for text classification on the GLUE SST-2 sentiment dataset. A TF-IDF + Logistic Regression pipeline and a fine-tuned DistilBERT classifier are compared under a loss-threshold MIA, with utility measured by development accuracy and macro F1. DistilBERT reached 0.9466 accuracy and 0.9460 macro F1 against 0.8756 and 0.8727 for Logistic Regression, yet both models leaked membership signal (Attack AUC 0.5615 and 0.5800, respectively). Two mitigations were tested. Stronger regularization reduced leakage for Logistic Regression at a visible utility cost, whereas fine-tuning DistilBERT for 2 epochs instead of 3 reduced leakage with negligible accuracy loss. Lightweight training adjustments can improve the privacy-utility trade-off without complex defenses.

</details>


### [179] [Empirical Evaluation of Data Poisoning Attacks in Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10952)
*Toshif Khan,Muhammad Abusaqer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究在MNIST与Fashion-MNIST上，用逻辑回归、线性SVM和随机森林评估标签翻转与后门投毒两类训练期攻击，比较干净训练与5%/10%/20%投毒率下的多项指标。标签翻转导致明显性能下降（逻辑回归与线性SVM最严重，随机森林较稳定）；后门投毒攻击成功率0.9667–1.0000，同时干净测试性能常接近基线。研究强调区分无差别投毒（标准指标可见）与定向后门投毒（隐蔽但高效），主张超越传统干净测试指标的面向安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据投毒通过污染训练数据来降低模型性能或植入攻击者控制的行为，威胁模型安全。研究动机在于系统比较不同类型训练期攻击对模型的影响，揭示传统干净测试指标难以反映的隐蔽安全隐患。

Method: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST数据集上，选取三种基线分类器：逻辑回归、线性SVM与随机森林。评估两类代表性训练期攻击：标签翻转与后门投毒。将干净训练与5%、10%、20%三种投毒率进行对比，采用干净测试准确率、宏精确率、宏召回率、宏F1值，并针对后门攻击额外使用攻击成功率作为评价指标。

Result: 标签翻转造成明显的性能退化，对逻辑回归和线性SVM影响最大，而随机森林性能相对稳定。后门投毒在两个数据集和三种模型上的攻击成功率介于0.9667至1.0000之间，同时干净测试性能常保持在基线附近。

Conclusion: 研究清晰区分了两类投毒：无差别投毒会体现在标准指标上，而定向后门投毒相对隐蔽却能嵌入高效的恶意行为。结果支持在传统干净测试指标之外，采用面向安全的评估方法。

Abstract: Data poisoning corrupts training data to degrade a model or to plant attacker-controlled behavior. This study evaluates two representative training-time attacks, label flipping and backdoor poisoning, on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST with three baseline classifiers: Logistic Regression, Linear SVM, and Random Forest. Clean training is compared with poisoning rates of 5%, 10%, and 20% using clean-test accuracy, macro-precision, macro-recall, macro-F1, and, for backdoors, attack success rate. Label flipping caused clear degradation, largest for Logistic Regression and Linear SVM, while Random Forest stayed comparatively stable. Backdoor poisoning reached attack success rates from 0.9667 to 1.0000 on both datasets and all three models while often keeping clean-test performance near baseline. The results separate indiscriminate poisoning, which shows up in standard metrics, from targeted backdoor poisoning, which stays comparatively stealthy while embedding highly effective malicious behavior, and they support security-oriented evaluation beyond conventional clean-test metrics.

</details>


### [180] [DeFiFusion: Combining Transaction Events with Smart Contracts to Detect Price Manipulation Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11008)
*Rui Cao,Shaojing Fan,Liming Fang,Yuchan Liu,Yingying Jiao,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeFiFusion，一种双模态价格操纵攻击（PMA）检测框架，通过联合建模交易事件与智能合约语义，实现 SOTA 检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeFi 中交易动态与智能合约逻辑深度耦合，去中心化消除了中介却扩大了价格操纵攻击（PMA）的攻击面，造成严重经济损失。现有检测范式存在根本缺陷：以交易为中心的方法缺乏对合约执行语义的感知，在正常市场波动下易产生误报；静态合约分析忽略真实交易行为，常报告实际不可利用的漏洞。

Method: 提出双模态 PMA 检测框架 DeFiFusion，核心思想是 PMA 恶意性仅源自交易行为与其所利用的合约逻辑之间的交互，单一信号不足。具体包括：面向价格操纵的事件编码，提取针对操纵模式的细粒度时间与经济特征；基于 LLM 的合约语义提取，提供先前行为方法所缺失的执行逻辑上下文；以及双模态投影融合 Transformer（采用 T5 风格相对位置编码），以捕捉区分 PMA 与正常市场活动的循环多阶段执行结构。

Result: 大量实验表明，DeFiFusion 持续取得最先进的检测性能，在 225 个 PMA 案例中成功召回 222 个，同时保持 96.10% 的精确率。

Conclusion: 通过双模态融合交易事件与合约语义，DeFiFusion 有效弥补了现有交易中心与静态合约分析方法的不足，在保持高精确率的同时实现高召回率，验证了联合建模在 PMA 检测中的必要性。

Abstract: Decentralized Finance (DeFi) has emerged as a rapidly growing blockchain-based financial service, where market transaction dynamics and underlying smart contract logic are intricately intertwined. This autonomous interplay, while eliminating centralized intermediaries, significantly expands the vulnerability surface of DeFi protocols to Price Manipulation Attacks (PMAs), which have already inflicted catastrophic financial losses. Despite their gravity, existing detection paradigms suffer from fundamental limitations. Transaction-centric methods lack awareness of contract execution semantics, making them prone to false positives under legitimate market volatility, while static contract analyses ignore real transaction behaviors and frequently report vulnerabilities that are infeasible to exploit in practice. We present DeFiFusion, a dual-modal PMA detection framework that closes this gap by jointly modeling transaction events and smart contract semantics within a unified pipeline. Our core insight is that PMA maliciousness emerges only from the interaction between transaction behaviors and the contract logic they exploit; neither signal suffices in isolation. Accordingly, we derive price-manipulation-aware event encoding for extracting fine-grained temporal and economic features tailored to manipulation patterns. We further introduce LLM-based contract semantic extraction to supply the execution-logic context that prior behavioral methods lack. To fuse these modalities, we propose a Dual-Modal Projection-Fusion Transformer with T5-style relative positional encoding, capturing the cyclic multi-stage execution structures that distinguish PMAs from benign market activity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeFiFusion consistently achieves state-of-the-art detection performance, effectively recalling 222 of the 225 PMA cases while maintaining a precision of 96.10%.

</details>


### [181] [The Missing Boundary: How Autonomous Agents Lose Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11024)
*Zonghao Ying,Xiangfan Wu,Huiyu Wu,Xing Zheng,Huangsheng Cheng,Xiaorong Shi,Jing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究自主智能体在追求合法任务时"越界执行"的条件，提出"控制边界退化 + 可执行的不安全机会"是失控的必要组合条件，并通过1800条确定性多轮轨迹实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体越来越多地执行涉及工具调用、持久状态和高后果动作的长程任务，其失控风险日益突出。既有研究多将失败归因于对抗性指令、恶意环境或目标冲突，但无法解释在任务本身合法、且存在合规路径的情况下，失控为何仍会发生。作者希望厘清"控制边界丧失"如何从合法任务执行中内生地涌现。

Method: 在确定性的多轮环境中独立操纵三个因素：目标压力、控制退化、可执行的不安全机会；覆盖5个智能体模型、16个操作领域，共1800条唯一轨迹，采用全因子设计。另设上下文管理消融实验（对比保留与省略控制约束的压缩方式），并做控制边界恢复实验。

Result: 控制退化或不安全机会单独存在时，均不会产生显著失控；两者同时具备时，全因子实验中失控率达55%，在额外10个操作领域达62%。恢复原控制边界后，即使不安全动作仍可执行，失控率降至0%。上下文消融显示压缩本身无害：保留控制约束时失控率为0%，省略时升至87%。

Conclusion: 失控是一种潜在状态向外部违规的转化：任务目标保持完整，但可执行的机会会把缺失的控制边界变成高后果动作。因此，安全治理的关键不在于消除合法任务目标或屏蔽环境机会，而在于维护并恢复显式的控制边界。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly perform long-horizon tasks involving tool use, persistent state, and consequential actions, raising a fundamental question: \emph{under what conditions does an agent cross the boundary of authorized execution while pursuing a legitimate task?} Existing studies often attribute such failures to adversarial instructions, malicious environments, or conflicting objectives, leaving unclear how loss of control can emerge during otherwise legitimate task execution. We study this question by independently manipulating three factors: goal pressure, control degradation, and executable unsafe opportunity. Our central hypothesis is that a degraded control boundary becomes consequential when the environment exposes an executable action that crosses it, even when the underlying task remains legitimate and a sanctioned path remains feasible. We test this hypothesis in a deterministic multi-turn environment across five agent models and 16 operational domains. Across 1,800 unique trajectories, we find that neither degraded control nor unsafe opportunity alone produces substantial loss of control; when both are present, the loss-of-control rate reaches $55\%$ in the full-factorial study and $62\%$ across ten additional operational domains. Restoring the original control boundary reduces the rate to $0\%$ even when the unsafe action remains executable. A context-management ablation further shows that compaction itself is not harmful: preserving the control constraints yields $0\%$ loss of control, whereas omitting them increases the rate to $87\%$. These results show how a latent loss of control can become an external violation: the task objective remains intact, but an executable opportunity can turn a missing control boundary into consequential action. Our code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/Tencent/AI-Infra-Guard.

</details>


### [182] [BenchShield: Formal Model-Backed Instrumentation for Reward Integrity in LLM-Agent Evaluation Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11028)
*Shenghan Zheng,Zonglin Di,Yimin Liu,Kyoung Whan Choe,Jiankai Sun,Heguang Lin,Penghao Jiang,Yifeng He,Xiao Cheng,Jicheng Wang,Wenbo Chen,Alex Yates,Yinzhe Zhao,Bingran You,Yuan Gao,Ayush Munot,Shubham Gaur,Zhe Ye,Hao Wang,Xiangyi Li,Dawn Song,Christophe Hauser*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 BenchShield——一个面向 LLM-Agent 评测的模型驱动插桩层，基于评测奖励相关事件的有限生命周期模型，结合静态阶段感知污点分析与运行时基础设施侧证据归因，检测并证明运行是否越出预期评测边界，从而缓解 reward hacking。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LM-Agent 基准正演变为交互式评测基础设施，Agent 可观测状态、调用工具、修改工作区、提交产物并依据结果过程获得奖励。这种交互性使评测易受 reward hacking 影响：Agent 通过利用奖励相关的轨迹而非真正解决任务来抬高分数。现有防御多依赖任务特定补丁、提示指令或事后检测器，无法提供可复用的证据证明某次具体运行仍处于预期评测边界内。

Method: 在基准基础设施内构建 BenchShield 模型驱动插桩层，将检测锚定于评测奖励相关事件的有限生命周期模型，并在该模型上运行两类互补分析：一是静态、阶段感知的污点分析，在运行前暴露 reward-hacking 路径；二是对应的运行时分析，利用基础设施侧证据归因具体 Agent 使用行为，并输出有证据支撑的断言。同时构建 BenchShield Trajectories 人工标注语料，包含来自三个基准、三万余次公开 Agent 运行的 456 条已裁定轨迹。

Result: 在相同任务与模型下，相较于 agentic hackability scanner 基线，BenchShield 将全链召回率从 23–94% 提升至 77–100%，同向量覆盖率从 16–56% 提升至 43–78%，并将每任务成本最多降低 65%；其运行时分析基于基础设施侧证据检测 reward hacking 的准确率达 96%。

Conclusion: BenchShield 提供了可复用的、有证据支撑的评测奖励完整性保障机制，兼顾静态事前路径暴露与运行时精确归因，在召回、覆盖与成本上均优于现有基线，可用于 LLM-Agent 评测中的 reward hacking 检测。

Abstract: LM-agent benchmarks increasingly function as interactive evaluation infrastructure. Agents observe state, call tools, modify workspaces,
  submit artifacts, and receive rewards from outcome procedures. This interactivity makes evaluations vulnerable to reward hacking: an agent
  improves its measured score by exploiting the reward-relevant trajectory instead of solving the intended task. Existing defenses rely largely
  on task-specific patches, prompt instructions, or post-hoc detectors. They do not provide reusable evidence that a concrete run remained
  within its intended evaluation boundary. This paper presents BenchShield, a model-backed instrumentation layer for reward integrity in
  LLM-agent evaluation. BenchShield grounds detection in a finite lifecycle model of an evaluation's reward-relevant events. Within the
  benchmark infrastructure, two complementary analyses operate over this model. A static, phase-aware taint analysis exposes reward-hacking
  paths before a run. Its runtime counterpart uses infrastructure-side evidence to attribute concrete agent use and emit evidence-backed claims.
  We construct BenchShield Trajectories, a human-labeled corpus of 456 adjudicated trajectories from more than 31,000 public agent runs across
  three benchmarks. Compared with an agentic hackability scanner baseline on the same tasks and model, BenchShield improves full-chain recall
  from 23-94% to 77-100%, same-vector coverage from 16-56% to 43-78%, and reduces per-task cost by up to 65%. Its runtime analysis achieves 96%
  accuracy in detecting reward hacking from infrastructure-side evidence.

</details>


### [183] [ToxicRAG: Compromising Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems via Single-Shot Knowledge Poisoning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11082)
*Haozhe Lu,Jiaqi Li,Xinyuan Zhu,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ToxicRAG是一种"每目标单文档"的知识投毒攻击，将虚假信息包装为连贯的知识更新叙事，在12个数据集-模型组合上取得0.61–0.91的ASR，全面匹配或超越最强基线（优势0–11个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG虽能将LLM输出锚定于外部证据，却也引入知识投毒风险。现有代表性攻击依赖多篇注入文档或模板，直接断言目标答案，形式单一且易被识别。本文旨在验证：仅用单篇文档、以叙事形式表达错误信息，是否同样能有效操纵RAG输出。

Method: 提出ToxicRAG：针对每个目标问题仅注入一篇文档，将其表述为连贯的"知识更新"叙事——先承认既有答案，再引入看似使其失效的虚构事件，最后将攻击者选定答案归于一组所谓权威来源。可选地加入"以答案为中心"的自验证循环：若代理语言模型未能复现目标答案，则对候选文档进行修订。评测覆盖Natural Questions、HotpotQA、MS-MARCO各100个目标问题，4种受害LLM与4种稠密检索器。

Result: 在论文所报告的采样语料设定下，ToxicRAG在12个数据集-模型组合上的ASR介于0.61至0.91之间；在所有组合中均匹配或超过所评估的最强基线，领先幅度为0至11个百分点。

Conclusion: 叙事形式的单文档投毒在受评估的RAG配置下仍具有显著影响力，表明仅靠单篇文档即可实现有效攻击。这一结果凸显了在RAG系统中加强事实一致性与来源溯源（provenance）研究的必要性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) can ground large language model (LLM) outputs in external evidence, but it also exposes the system to knowledge poisoning. Representative attacks use multiple injected documents or templates that directly assert a target answer. We present ToxicRAG, a one-document-per-target attack that expresses misinformation as a coherent knowledge-update narrative. The generated document first acknowledges the previously accepted answer, introduces fabricated events that appear to invalidate it, and then attributes the attacker-selected answer to a set of purported authorities. An answer-focused self-validation loop optionally revises a candidate when a surrogate language model does not reproduce the target answer. We evaluate the attack on 100 target questions from each of Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and MS-MARCO, using four victim LLMs and four dense retrievers. In the sampled-corpus setting reported in this paper, ToxicRAG obtains ASRs between 0.61 and 0.91 across the twelve dataset--model combinations. It matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated baseline in every combination, with margins ranging from 0 to 11 percentage points. These results show that narrative-form poisoned documents can remain influential under the evaluated RAG configurations and motivate further study of factual consistency and source provenance in RAG systems.

</details>


### [184] [The Machines Are Calling: Measuring Automated and Synthetic Voices in Unwanted Inbound Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11137)
*Xingyu Shen,Tommy Duong,Muduo Xu,Xiaodong An,Jiaqi Gan,Haoyuan Tang,Jamey Z. Liang,Siyu Zhang,Yan Zhang,Simiao Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过交互式语音蜜罐在66天内录制10,987通电话，结合音频指纹、商用合成语音检测器与盲听者三重手段，首次量化了美国不受欢迎电话中机器拨打与合成语音的占比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2024年2月FCC将AI生成语音纳入TCPA监管，但尚无同行评审的测量数据说明有多少骚扰电话由机器拨打、其中多少语音是合成的而非录音播放，这一测量空白亟待填补。

Method: 部署语言模型人格化语音蜜罐，使用真实美国号码，将主叫方单独录轨；66天录制10,987通电话，剔除11天静默应答数据。对每通开场音频使用三种工具：跨电话识别同一录音的音频指纹、针对主叫前10秒的商用合成语音检测器，以及盲听者对检测器标记结果的复核。

Result: 在正常日被问候的7,233通电话中，13.8%以重复录音开场，13.1%为检测器标记为合成的新音频，9.9%问候后主叫全程沉默，54.2%为检测器标记为人类的新音频，9.0%无法评分。机器语音开场至少占26.9%，另有约十分之一为判读为机器拨打的静默连接；录音重放在检测器自身检出率中占45%（6,192通已评分开场中的29.3%）。同一波形跨两通电话落在检测器阈值两侧的比例为13.6%，11名听者确认检测器标记内容的54.4%。合成开场集中于引流垃圾信息（33.8%）而非诈骗（21.1%），仅0.44%披露自动化。流行度与诱饵号码流通时长相关（同周内59%对19%），起因为号码播种历史而非日历时间。活动生命周期长于其号码：一则录音合规通知出现在六场活动中，一个合成声音服务九个活动。

Conclusion: 骚扰电话中机器语音开场至少占26.9%，合成语音占比高且检测器存在同波形判定不一致（13.6%）与准确率有限（盲听确认仅54.4%）的问题；合成语音主要用于引流垃圾信息，且极少披露自动化。号码的播种历史而非时间推移决定其被骚扰的流行度，且活动与号码、声音资源呈多对多复用关系，说明监管与测量需关注号码流通史和跨活动复用模式。

Abstract: In February 2024 the U.S. Federal Communications Commission (FCC) placed AI-generated voices under the Telephone Consumer Protection Act (TCPA). Yet no peer-reviewed measurement says how much unwanted call traffic is placed by a machine, or how much of that machine speech is synthesized rather than played from a recording. We report both with a disclosed pipeline. An interactive voice honeypot (language-model personas on real U.S. numbers, the caller recorded on its own track) recorded 10,987 calls over 66 days; 11 days on which our stack answered silently are set aside. Three instruments read each opening: an audio fingerprint that finds the same recording played on other calls, a commercial synthetic-speech detector on the caller's first ten seconds, and blinded listeners who check what it flags. Of the 7,233 calls our persona greeted on normal days, 13.8% open with a recording we also heard on another call, and 13.1% with fresh audio the detector labels synthetic. A further 9.9% open with a caller who never spoke after our greeting, 54.2% with fresh audio the detector labels human, and 9.0% could not be scored. Machine-voiced openings are therefore at least 26.9%, a further tenth of calls are silent connections we read as machine-placed, and replays of a recording make up 45% of the detector's own rate (29.3% of 6,192 scored openings). The same waveform played on two calls lands on opposite sides of the detector's threshold 13.6% of the time, and eleven listeners confirm 54.4% of what it flags. Synthetic openings concentrate in lead-generation spam (33.8%), not fraud (21.1%); 0.44% disclose automation. Prevalence tracks how long a bait number has circulated (59% against 19% in the same weeks): seeding history, not calendar time, explains the trend. Campaigns outlast their numbers: one recorded compliance notice opens calls in six campaigns, and one synthetic voice serves nine.

</details>


### [185] [You Get What You Sample: Evaluating Sampling Strategies for Web Security Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11218)
*Xuenan Zhang,Yuqing Yang,Giancarlo Pellegrino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估抽样方法对Web安全测量结论的影响，基于50万Tranco与2480万Common Crawl主机的实证比较，发现Top N抽样无法代表整体网络分布，而概率抽样可给出稳定无偏估计，并提出自适应概率抽样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web测量研究依赖Tranco等域名数据集量化安全问题的普遍性与影响，但全量分析成本过高而必须抽样；然而抽样长期由惯例（尤以Top N为主）而非证据驱动，其对安全发现有效性与可推广性的影响缺乏系统评估，常见策略是否引入系统性偏差、扭曲漏洞比例或限制研究间可比性尚不明确。

Method: 通过系统性文献综述与大规模测量（50万Tranco域名、2480万Common Crawl主机），对数据集与抽样策略进行对比评估，比较Top N、概率抽样与混合抽样的表现。

Result: Top N抽样虽可能合理，但不反映Web整体分布；概率抽样对普遍性及多种影响目标可产生稳定、无偏估计；混合抽样相较纯概率抽样无优势，其确定性前缀持续对准确性产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 为后续研究提供数据支撑的指导：建议采用自适应概率抽样策略，即使在目标问题普遍性未知时仍能保持有效。

Abstract: Web measurement studies rely on domain datasets such as Tranco to quantify the prevalence and impact of security issues at scale, but exhaustively analyzing these datasets is often infeasible because of the cost of advanced analysis techniques, requiring the use of sampling. Despite its widespread use, sampling remains largely guided by convention---most commonly \emph{Top $N$} domain selection---rather than evidence, and its influence on the validity and generalizability of security findings has received little systematic evaluation. Consequently, it remains unclear whether common sampling strategies introduce systematic bias, distort observed vulnerability rates, or limit comparability across studies.
  In this work, we undertake, to the best of our knowledge, the first comprehensive investigation into how sampling methodologies affect the measurements and the conclusions. Through a comprehensive literature review and large-scale measurements of 500k Tranco and 24.8M Common Crawl hosts, we perform a comparative evaluation of datasets and sampling strategies. We show that, while Top $N$ sampling may be a rational strategy, the researchers have to bear in mind that Top $N$ does not reflect the overall distribution of the web. Instead, probability-based strategies yield stable, unbiased estimates for prevalence and many impact objectives. Hybrid sampling provides no advantages over pure probability sampling, as its deterministic prefix consistently contributes negatively to accuracy. Building on these results, we provide data-backed guidance for future studies, proposing to use an adaptive probability-based sampling strategy that remains effective even when the prevalence of the target issue is unknown.

</details>


### [186] [Few-Shot Learning for Network Intrusion Detection: Methods, Datasets, and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11275)
*Arne Roszeitis,Victor Jüttner,Erik Buchmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 系统综述2022—2026年小样本学习用于异常网络入侵检测的研究，从1358条记录筛选出21篇，梳理方法、数据集与性能，指出元学习与CNN最常用、复现性受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于异常的NIDS是重要防线，但新攻击类型缺乏标注数据，训练困难；小样本学习（FSL）可缓解该问题，然而现有方法与评测设置差异大，缺乏系统性梳理。

Method: 采用类似PRISMA 2020报告规范的系统文献综述，检索ACM Digital Library、IEEE Xplore和Scopus，经筛选、去重与质量过滤后保留21项研究，并对FSL方法、数据集与实验参数进行分类及性能比较。

Result: 元学习和卷积神经网络是最常用方法，分别有8篇和10篇研究；多数研究每类评估样本不超过5个，设置差异较大；CIC-IDS2017和CSE-CIC-IDS2018是最常用数据集。

Conclusion: 该领域方法多样但评估设置不统一，关键参数与源代码缺失限制了可复现性和方法间直接比较。

Abstract: Anomaly-based network intrusion detection systems (NIDS) are an important first line of defense. However, training NIDS for new attack types is challenging, because labeled attack data are rarely available. Few-shot learning (FSL) addresses this problem by learning from few samples. However, the approaches and evaluation settings, that have been investigated so far, vary widely. This work systematically reviews FSL approaches for NIDS published from 2022 to 2026. We conduct a systematic literature review with PRISMA 2020-like reporting to search ACM Digital Library, IEEE Xplore, and Scopus. From a set of 1,358 initial records, we retain 21 studies after screening, deduplication, and quality filtering. We classify the applied FSL approaches, datasets, and experimental parameters and compare reported performance. Meta-learning and convolutional neural networks are the most common approaches, with 8 and 10 studies, respectively. Most studies evaluate five or fewer samples per class, although settings vary. CIC-IDS2017 and CSE-CIC-IDS2018 are the most frequently used datasets. Missing parameters and source code limit reproducibility and direct comparison between approaches.

</details>


### [187] [Deep-Fake CAPTCHA: Mitigating Next-Generation Social Engineering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11404)
*Guy Frankovits,Lior Yasur,Fred M. Grabovski,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-CAPTCHA，一种面向语音与视频通话中实时深度伪造冒充的主动防御框架：通过要求主叫方完成人易完成、实时深度伪造系统难以逼真生成的挑战-响应任务，并从真实性、身份一致性、任务完成度、响应时间四个维度验证，在音视频两种模态上显著优于被动检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时深度伪造使语音与视频通话中的身份冒充成为现实威胁，而人们本身难以区分实时深度伪造与真实媒体；现有被动检测方法依赖寻找伪造痕迹（artifacts），在实时场景下效果有限，因此需要一种更主动、更鲁棒的防御手段来应对新一代社会工程攻击。

Method: 提出DF-CAPTCHA主动防御框架。不被动搜索伪造痕迹，而是要求主叫方执行简单的挑战-响应任务（对人类容易、对当前实时深度伪造系统难以逼真生成）。随后依据四项标准验证响应：真实性（realism）、身份一致性（identity consistency）、任务完成度（task completion）与响应时间（response time）。在音频与视频两种模态上，通过用户研究与实时深度伪造模型实验进行评估。

Result: 用户研究显示，人们往往难以区分实时深度伪造与真实媒体；而DF-CAPTCHA相较被动方法显著提升检测性能，在音频与视频两种模态上均达到高准确率。

Conclusion: 基于主动挑战的验证是一种实用且鲁棒的防御方式，可有效应对基于实时深度伪造的下一代社会工程攻击。

Abstract: This paper presents DF-CAPTCHA, an active defense against real-time deepfake impersonation in voice and video calls. Instead of passively searching for artifacts, DF-CAPTCHA prompts the caller to perform simple challenge-response tasks that are easy for humans but difficult for current real-time deepfake systems to generate convincingly. The framework verifies the response using four criteria: realism, identity consistency, task completion, and response time. We evaluate the approach across both audio and video modalities using user studies and experiments with real-time deepfake models. Results show that people often struggle to distinguish real-time deepfakes from authentic media, while DF-CAPTCHA substantially improves detection performance over passive methods, reaching high accuracy in both modalities. These findings suggest that active challenge-based verification is a practical and robust defense against next-generation social engineering attacks based on real-time deepfakes.

</details>


### [188] ["They don't care about this": A Systematic Study of TEE Build Reproducibility in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11411)
*Annika Wilde,Marco Gutfleisch,Felix Reichmann,Anirban Chakraborty,Yuval Yarom,M. Angela Sasse,Ghassan Karame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文通过技术分析115个TEE部署并对12名开发者访谈，发现91%的TEE构建不可复现，80%同时缺失源码与参考构建，指出复现性问题源于技术障碍与更广泛的生态系统挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程证明的有效性依赖验证者能将测量值追溯到原始源代码，而这一属性只能通过可复现构建来保证。然而TEE部署中可复现构建的实际状况尚缺乏系统性实证研究。

Method: 对115个TEE部署（涵盖Intel SGX、Intel TDX、AMD SEV等主流TEE）进行技术分析，考察其可复现性；联系50个SGX项目维护者，招募12名来自工业界与学术界的开发者进行访谈。

Result: 91%的部署不可复现，80%未能同时提供源代码和参考构建这两个可复现性的必要前提；仅1名受访者表示可复现性是开发中的优先事项，印证了技术分析结论。

Conclusion: 除二进制中嵌入时间戳等技术障碍外，还存在多利益相关方项目中构建环境缺乏控制等生态系统层面的挑战。实现TEE可复现性需要超越单个开发者的整体性开发方法，并需更强的承诺，而非将TEE仅当作"安全徽章"。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have become a cornerstone of modern cloud computing, providing strong confidentiality and integrity guarantees for both code and data. A critical component of this trust model is remote attestation, which enables external entities to verify the authenticity and integrity of code executing within a TEE through cryptographic measurements. However, the effectiveness of remote attestation fundamentally depends on the verifier's ability to trace the reported measurement back to the original source code - a property that can only be guaranteed through reproducible builds.
  In this paper, we investigate the reproducibility of TEE builds through a technical analysis of 115 TEE deployments. Our analysis spans popular TEEs such as Intel SGX, Intel TDX, and AMD SEV, and reveals that a striking 91% of those deployments were not reproducible, with 80% failing to provide both source code and a reference build, the two essential prerequisites for reproducibility. To explore the root causes, we contacted the maintainers of 50 SGX projects and managed to recruit 12 developers from industry and academia for interviews. Only one of our participants reported that reproducibility is a priority during development, effectively confirming our technical findings. Beyond technical barriers (e.g., timestamps included in the binary) that can be readily addressed, we identify broader ecosystem-level challenges, such as the lack of control over the build environment in projects involving multiple stakeholders. We argue that achieving reproducibility in TEEs requires a holistic development approach that extends beyond individual developers and calls for stronger commitments - rather than treating TEEs as a "security badge".

</details>


### [189] [Heterogeneous Cross-Chain Transaction Tracing for Solana Bridges via Candidate-Set Selective Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11413)
*Wenjie Dou,Zheng Che,Meng Shen,Hanbiao Du,Qing Li,Yan Qiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文针对Solana跨链资金追踪的语义鸿沟问题，形式化四类Solana-bound跨链交易模式，提出候选集选择性决策追踪方法SolTracer，将异构执行语义映射到统一事件空间，在封闭世界、开放世界与跨源链泛化三场景中超越SOTA（开放世界50% TA比例下F1提升20.16%），并基于该方法开展真实跨链转移的生态实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana作为高吞吐区块链平台快速扩张，吸引大量流动性与用户活动，同时被不法行为者利用跨链桥将非法资金转移至Solana以混淆资金链路。与EVM兼容平台不同，Solana执行动态独特且缺乏标准事件日志，产生严重语义鸿沟，导致现有追踪方法无法可靠关联跨账本交易。因此需要面向Solana特性的跨链交易追踪方法。

Method: 论文形式化四类Solana-bound跨链交易模式，并提出基于候选集选择性决策的追踪方法SolTracer：将异构执行语义映射到统一事件空间，采用候选集选择性决策机制可靠关联目标交易，并在缺乏有效目标时选择弃权（abstain）。

Result: 在封闭世界关联、开放世界关联与跨源链泛化三类代表性场景中，SolTracer均优于SOTA方法。尤其在具有50% TA比例的高难度开放世界设置下，相比最强SOTA基线，F1分数提升20.16%。

Conclusion: SolTracer可有效解决Solana跨链追踪中的语义鸿沟与可靠性关联问题。基于该方法的真实跨链转移实证分析揭示了跨链桥机制间数量-价值显著背离、跨资产转移普遍存在，以及链上结算与区块浏览器可见性之间的解耦现象。

Abstract: Solana is a rapidly growing high-throughput blockchain platform that has attracted substantial liquidity and user activity. However, this expansion has also drawn the attention of illicit actors, who frequently leverage cross-chain bridges to route illicit funds onto Solana to obfuscate transaction lineage. Unlike EVM-compatible platforms, Solana features distinct execution dynamics and lacks standard event logs, creating severe semantic gaps that prevent existing tracing methods from reliably correlating cross-ledger transactions. In this paper, we formalize four types of Solana-bound cross-chain transaction modes and propose a candidate-set selective decision-based tracing method called SolTracer. SolTracer maps disparate execution semantics into a unified event space and employs candidate-set selective decision-making to reliably associate target transactions while abstaining when valid targets are absent. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SolTracer outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across three representative scenarios: closed-world association, open-world association, and cross-source-chain generalization. In particular, under the challenging open-world setting with a 50% TA ratio, SolTracer improves the F1 score by 20.16% over the strongest SOTA baseline. Utilizing SolTracer, we conduct an empirical analysis on real-world cross-chain transfers to investigate ecosystem dynamics. Our analysis explores the stark count-value divergence across bridge mechanisms, the prevalence of cross-asset shifts, and the decoupling between on-chain settlement and explorer visibility.

</details>


### [190] [Chypothermia: Clock Freezing for Static Side-channel Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11442)
*Fatemeh Khojasteh Dana,Mehmet Ali Cetin,Xinrui Wang,Andrew Butler,Yuval Yarom,Shahin Tajik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Chypothermia——一种无需电气篡改的低温攻击，通过干扰片上混合信号元件，禁用时钟传感器、时钟生成电路与电压传感器，并使时钟停摆而保留密钥数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态侧信道攻击利用时钟停摆窃取敏感信息，现有防御依赖监测异常时钟行为并触发敏感数据清除。作者希望揭示这些防御在物理环境操纵（低温）下的脆弱性。

Method: 将芯片暴露于低温环境，利用其对片上混合信号元件（信号感知与生成）的干扰，构造 Chypothermia 攻击；并与 Chypnosis 结合，在中等低温工作范围内停摆时钟且规避温度传感器检测。在多种 FPGA/SoC 平台实现，覆盖软 IP 与硬 IP 传感器；并对集成先进时钟传感器的 OpenTitan 根信任 alert handler 进行验证。最后设计并实现 FPGA 兼容的自加热传感器作为对策。

Result: Chypothermia 成功禁用软 IP 与硬 IP 传感器实现，规避检测并阻止 OpenTitan 的密钥清零；与 Chypnosis 结合可在中等低温范围内停摆时钟而不被温度传感器察觉；所提出的自加热传感器对 Chypothermia 表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 低温可作为一种无需电气篡改的有效攻击向量，破坏基于时钟与电压传感的安全防护，现有温度传感防御不足；自加热传感器是一种可行的缓解手段，凸显物理环境防护在芯片安全中的必要性。

Abstract: Static side-channel attacks, which exploit halted-clock conditions to extract sensitive information, pose an increasing threat to chip security. To counter these attacks, various defenses have been proposed that monitor for abnormal clock behavior and trigger the clearing of sensitive data when clock anomalies are detected. In this work, we demonstrate that exposing a chip to cryogenic temperatures interferes with on-chip mixed-signal components, responsible for signal sensing and generation. Based on this observation, we develop Chypothermia, an attack that, without any electrical tampering with the system, disables the target clock sensor, the clock generation circuit, and the voltage sensors, while preserving the secret data.
  While effective at halting the clock, cooling is a slow process and, on its own, is often insufficient against systems equipped with temperature sensors designed to detect thermal anomalies. To bypass these protections, we combine Chypothermia with Chypnosis (Mitard et al., IEEE SP 2026) and show that, even within a moderately low-temperature operating range, this combination can halt the clock while evading detection. We implement Chypothermia on multiple FPGA/SoC platforms and demonstrate successful disabling of both soft-IP and hard-IP sensor implementations. Finally, we apply Chypothermia to the alert handler of the OpenTitan root of trust, which incorporates a state-of-the-art clock sensor, and show that the attack evades detection and prevents key zeroization. Finally, we introduce and implement an FPGA-compatible self-heating sensor as a countermeasure and demonstrate its robustness against Chypothermia.

</details>


### [191] [On Identifying Sound Conditions for Frontrunning Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11535)
*Sebastian Holler,Anna Piscitelli,Jannik Albrecht,Stephan Dübler,Ghassan Karame,Clara Schneidewind*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次给出智能合约抢跑漏洞的形式化定义，指出其抗性取决于诚实用户的交互方式而非合约本身，并提出可靠算法合成安全交互条件，在真实合约中发现此前未知的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易排序的异步性导致抢跑攻击，造成以太坊等主流链数百万美元损失；矿工可通过重排、延迟或插入交易获利。然而该领域缺乏对"合约抗抢跑"的严格定义，且现有动态检测方法存在根本性不足：在287份智能合约审计、393个已报告漏洞中，55%落在现有检测标准范围之外。

Method: 提出首个面向智能合约抢跑漏洞的形式化定义，其核心洞见为：抗抢跑并非合约的固有属性，而关键取决于诚实用户与合约的交互方式。基于此观察，设计了一个可靠的（sound）算法用于合成安全交互条件，并实现原型工具应用于已审计的真实合约。

Result: 大规模审计研究显示现有检测标准遗漏55%的已报告漏洞；所提算法与原型在两个以太坊合约中揭示了此前未被发现的漏洞。

Conclusion: 形式化定义弥补了抢跑漏洞定义缺失的空白，表明抗抢跑依赖用户交互条件；该框架可扩展检测范围并发现现有方法遗漏的漏洞，为智能合约安全审计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Blockchains enable decentralized applications through smart contracts---interactive programs executed through consensus. However, the inherently asynchronous nature of blockchain transaction ordering introduces a class of vulnerabilities known as frontrunning attacks, which have caused millions of dollars in losses in major blockchains, such as Ethereum. Frontrunning attacks arise because users interact with smart contracts through transactions, which are added to the blockchain by designated nodes called miners. Miners can exploit their ability to reorder, delay, or insert transactions to gain an advantage over honest users, effectively frontrunning them.
  Yet, to date, the field lacks a rigorous definition of what it even means for a contract to resist such attacks. Worse, we show that existing dynamic detection approaches are fundamentally inadequate: in a large-scale study comprising 287 smart contract audits, 55% of the 393 reported vulnerabilities identified by leading smart contract auditors fall outside the scope of state-of-the-art detection criteria. To address this gap, we propose the first formal definition of frontrunning vulnerability for smart contracts. Our definition captures a key insight: resistance to frontrunning is not an intrinsic property of a contract alone, but depends critically on how honest users interact with it. Grounded in this observation, we develop a sound algorithm for synthesizing secure interaction conditions, alongside a prototype implementation that we apply to audited real-world contracts---revealing previously undiscovered vulnerabilities in two Ethereum contracts.

</details>


### [192] [Accountability in Certificate Transparency and Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11552)
*Timo Treitz,Robert Künnemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文将可问责性（accountability）视为证书透明（CT）的核心目标，并在Dolev-Yao模型下从传统PKI逐步建模至CT及其扩展，分析其信任假设的消除情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT已被主流浏览器支持，但其四角色（CA、日志、监控者、客户端）间的复杂校验机制使得CT究竟如何以复杂基础设施换取信任假设的消除难以精确刻画。已有的Dolev-Yao和计算模型分析仅针对高度简化的模型，且定义专为CA定制，实际捕捉的是设计特征而非目标安全属性。因此需要一个更精确、以目标属性为导向的分析。

Method: 作者提出将可问责性作为CT的主要目标，采用Dolev-Yao符号模型进行系统分析。分析路径为：从最基础的vanilla PKI出发，逐步过渡到CT本体，最后分析SCT Auditing与Gossiping两种扩展方案。通过形式化建模刻画各阶段的信任假设与可问责性保证。

Result: 分析表明：1）朴素CT依赖一个诚实的日志（honest log），但在该假设下能够提供可问责性；2）SCT Auditing扩展可以消除对诚实日志的假设；3）Gossiping扩展无法消除该假设。

Conclusion: CT的核心价值应在可问责性框架下理解，而非常见的简化安全定义。该工作澄清了CT及其扩展在信任假设上的能力边界：SCT Auditing能去除对诚实日志的依赖，而Gossiping不能，为后续CT协议设计与分析提供了精确的符号模型基础。

Abstract: Certificate Transparency (CT) aims to reduce the trust required in Certificate Authorities (CAs) within the TLS certificate ecosystem. It is supported by all major browsers. The protocol obliges all CAs to record the certificates they issue in a public log, which itself is monitored for compliance and consistency by third parties. Given this complex set of checks between the four roles-CA, loggers, monitor but also the end user's client-it is very hard to provide a precise account of how CT eliminates trust assumptions in exchange for complex infrastructure. Analyses both in the Dolev-Yao paradigm and the computational paradigm only regard a very simplified model and feature definitions adapted specifically to CAs, essentially capturing design features rather than the target property. The present paper posits accountability as the main goal of CT and presents a thorough analysis in the Dolev-Yao model. We start with the vanilla PKI and, step by step, move to CT, finally analyzing proposed extensions for SCT Auditing and Gossiping. We show that plain CT relies on an honest log, but provides accountability under this assumption. Furthermore, we show that the SCT Auditing extension can eliminate this assumption, while the Gossiping extension cannot.

</details>


### [193] [From Intent to Execution Grant: An Execution-Boundary Conformance Profile for High-Risk AI Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11596)
*Mengting Wu,Lin Wang,Yong Zhang,Jiang Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 EBL-Core——一个执行边界一致性剖面,用于判定单一已物化 AI 生成候选动作在显式条件下能否获得动作范围内的执行授权,并配套参考实现与有限验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体的动作日益具有外部后果(资金转移、基础设施变更、软件部署、信息披露、物理驱动)。授权引擎、策略语言、运行时监控、溯源机制与智能体护栏虽提供重要基础,但未就"从候选动作到执行权限"这一最终跃迁给出统一的语义契约。

Method: 定义 EBL-Core 一致性剖面,通过执行释放契约(ERC)绑定结构化意图对象、根策略与操作策略、证据义务、类型化证据、上下文、时间与可验证的决策推导。ERC 本身非承载权限的令牌;经校验的 ALLOW ERC 可支撑由赎期(Redemption-time)验证管制的独立执行授予。规范涵盖动作绑定、策略不可弱化、证据处理、确定性裁决、推导验证与授予生命周期行为。配套参考工件提供模式、裁决、独立验证与语义重放,以及可线性化的内存授予存储。

Result: 在保留运行中,34 个静态向量与 15 项生命周期检查均与预期结果一致。100 次试验中,32 个并发赎期尝试每次仅产生一次成功赎回并保护测试效果;100 次撤销-赎回竞争均以有效终态结束。

Conclusion: 这些有界结果证明了所规定子集的可执行性,而非人类意图正确性、证据真实性、完全中介、生产就绪性、机械化正确性或部署级安全性。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly propose actions with external consequences, including financial transfers, infrastructure changes, software deployments, disclosures, and physical actuation. Authorization engines, policy languages, runtime monitors, provenance mechanisms, and agent guardrails provide important foundations, but do not necessarily define a common semantic contract for the final transition from a particular candidate action to execution authority.
  We specify EBL-Core, an execution-boundary conformance profile for deciding whether one canonical, fully materialized AI-generated candidate may receive action-scoped execution authority under explicit conditions. It binds a structured intent object, Root and Operational Policies, evidence obligations, typed evidence, context, time, and a verifiable Decision Derivation through an Execution Release Contract (ERC). An ERC is not an authority-bearing token; a verified ALLOW ERC may support a separate Execution Grant governed by Redemption-time validation.
  EBL-Core specifies action binding, policy non-weakening, evidence handling, deterministic adjudication, derivation verification, and grant lifecycle behavior. An accompanying reference artifact provides schemas, adjudication, separate verification and Semantic Replay, and a linearizable in-memory grant store. In the retained run, 34 static vectors and 15 lifecycle checks matched expected outcomes. Across 100 trials, 32 concurrent Redemption attempts yielded exactly one successful Redemption and protected test effect per trial; 100 Revoke-Redeem races ended in valid terminal outcomes. These bounded results demonstrate executability of the specified subset, not human-intent correctness, evidence truth, complete mediation, production readiness, mechanized correctness, or deployment-level security.

</details>


### [194] [Signing the Transaction but Not the Decision: Whisper Attacks and a Binding Defense for AP2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11757)
*Yedidel Louck,Amit Dvir,Ariel Stulman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了AP2等智能体支付协议的意图约束缺失漏洞，提出三种攻击（高成功率达90%、56%、73.3%），并给出协议层防御A-VIP及评测基准AP2-WhisperBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体开始代替用户购物与支付，AP2等协议虽能生成密码学有效的签名，却不约束导致该签名的决策过程，使普通商品描述文本即可操纵智能体形成"合规但不符用户意图"的购物车，形成安全盲区。

Method: 作者首先构造并验证三类攻击：诱导智能体获取他人支付凭证、生成与用户所见不符但签名有效的购物车、用单条关于库存或商品来源的事实性声明将智能体从廉价展示项转向高价项。随后提出A-VIP协议层防御，将签名意图视为能力授权（capability grant）而非评判商家描述，把每次凭证查询绑定到发起请求的会话、每条购物车行绑定到用户所见的商品列表，并标记未授权支出。

Result: 在AP2示例智能体默认指定的Gemini Flash-Lite模型上，三种攻击成功率分别为90%、56%和73.3%。该漏洞在17个Google模型、3个无关智能体框架、2个跨厂商锚点以及Google自有消费级助手上均复现。防御方面，前两种攻击留下结构性痕迹，A-VIP通过绑定以零误报拦截；第三种攻击无痕，A-VIP以用户确认方式暴露未授权支出。

Conclusion: 现有智能体支付协议在意图层缺乏约束是系统性漏洞，跨模型与跨框架普遍存在。A-VIP通过将签名意图作为能力授权并对凭证查询与购物车行做来源绑定，可在协议层有效阻断有痕攻击并显式提示无痕风险。作者开源了A-VIP代码、机器校验不变量以及包含1,544个评测场景的AP2-WhisperBench。

Abstract: Software agents are beginning to shop and pay on a person's behalf. Agent payment protocols such as AP2 produce cryptographically valid signatures for completed purchases, yet do not constrain the decisions that lead to them. Consequently, ordinary product-description text can steer a shopping agent into forming a cart that passes every protocol check but no longer matches the user's request. In this paper, we show that this vulnerability enables three related attacks. In the first attack, the agent is steered into fetching another user's payment credentials. In the second, it assembles a cryptographically valid cart whose contents do not match what the user was shown. In the third, a single factual claim about stock or product lineage moves the agent from the cheaper displayed item to a more expensive one, while the resulting cart remains fully consistent with the listing. In experiments using the Gemini Flash-Lite models that AP2's sample agents specify by default, the three attacks succeeded at rates of 90%, 56%, and 73.3%, respectively. The same vulnerability appears across seventeen Google models, three unrelated agent frameworks, two cross-vendor anchors, and Google's own consumer assistant. To address this attack vector, we introduce A-VIP (AP2 Verified-Intent Protection), a protocol-layer defense that treats the signed intent as a capability grant rather than judging the merchant's description. The defense binds every credential lookup to the session that requested it and every cart line to the listing seen, while flagging unauthorized spending. The first two attacks leave structural traces that these bindings block with zero false positives. The third attack leaves no trace, so A-VIP surfaces unauthorized spending for user confirmation. Finally, we release the A-VIP code, machine-checked invariants, and AP2-WhisperBench, a suite of 1,544 evaluation scenarios.

</details>


### [195] [Differentially Private EEG Feature Anonymization: A Privacy-Utility Case Study in Clinical Neurophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11777)
*Noman Sadiq,Mohsen Toorani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究针对临床脑电（EEG）派生特征表示的受试者级差分隐私保护，采用高斯与拉普拉斯扰动，覆盖客户端、集中式服务端与去中心化本地训练三种部署场景，并评估隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床EEG数据对医疗研究与AI临床决策支持系统具有重要价值，但原始记录及派生特征包含患者敏感信息，在跨环境复用、分析与共享时存在隐私风险。传统匿名化方法难以处理高维生物医学信号，去除直接标识符无法防止重识别、链接或推断风险；而过度保护又会扭曲临床相关信号特征、降低数据效用，因此需要研究可量化的隐私保护机制。

Method: 提出面向EEG派生特征表示的受试者级差分隐私框架。流程为：EEG预处理与特征提取 → 对患者级特征表示施加高斯或拉普拉斯扰动。框架考虑三种部署场景：客户端匿名化、集中式服务端匿名化、去中心化本地训练。拉普拉斯实验评估实际实现的噪声尺度，正式全向量校准所需的尺度则单独推导。采用统计效用指标与下游机器学习效用检验评估两类扰动的影响。

Result: 差分隐私扰动可集成到EEG处理流程中；但所选机制、隐私参数与敏感度校准会显著影响数据效用。在规模小且类别不平衡的临床EEG数据集上，保持下游任务效用尤为困难。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了基于差分隐私的EEG特征匿名化中实际的隐私-效用权衡，以及在小规模、不平衡临床EEG数据中维持下游效用的挑战，为临床EEG数据共享与AI决策支持系统的隐私保护设计提供了实践参考。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalography (EEG) data are valuable for healthcare research and for developing artificial intelligence (AI)-based clinical decision-support systems, but EEG recordings and derived features may contain sensitive patient-specific information. This creates privacy risks when data are reused, analyzed, or shared across clinical and research environments. Conventional anonymization methods are often insufficient for high-dimensional biomedical signals, since removing direct identifiers does not necessarily prevent re-identification, linkage, or inference risks. At the same time, strong privacy protection may distort clinically relevant signal characteristics and reduce data utility. This paper studies subject-level differential privacy for protecting clinical EEG-derived feature representations using Gaussian and Laplace perturbations. The proposed framework considers three deployment scenarios: client-side anonymization, centralized server-side anonymization, and decentralized local training. Following EEG preprocessing and feature extraction, Gaussian and Laplace perturbations are applied to the resulting patient-level EEG feature representations. The Laplace experiments evaluate the implemented noise scales, while the scales required for formal full-vector calibration are derived separately. The effects of both perturbations are assessed using statistical utility measures and a downstream machine-learning-based utility check. The results show that differentially private perturbation can be integrated into EEG processing workflows, but the selected mechanism, privacy parameters, and sensitivity calibration strongly influence data utility. The study highlights the practical privacy-utility trade-off in DP-based EEG feature anonymization and the challenges of preserving downstream utility in small and imbalanced clinical EEG datasets.

</details>


### [196] [SpecGuard: Inference-Time Backdoor Detection For Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11799)
*Rui Wen,Ahmed Salem,Andrew Paverd,Mark Russinovich,Zheng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 SpecGuard，一种利用投机解码验证信号、零额外模型计算成本的推理期后门检测器，通过草稿令牌接受率变化识别被触发的后门行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型常被微调、共享或从第三方下载，部署模型可能隐藏后门：良性输入表现正常，出现秘密触发器时切换到攻击者控制行为。虽可在部署前审计，但对于频繁更新的模型，运行时监控仍很重要。难点在于 LLM 服务对延迟敏感，现有推理期检测器要么依赖触发器形式的假设（对隐蔽攻击失效），要么需要额外模型计算（输入扰动或额外生成过程）。

Method: SpecGuard 重用投机解码，不增加模型计算成本。投机解码用小草稿模型提议令牌、目标模型验证以加速推理。作者观察到验证过程本身已暴露有用信号：后门被触发时目标模型转向攻击者行为，而干净草稿模型无法预测该偏移，导致草稿令牌接受率变化。作者形式化了该信号出现的条件，并证明攻击者若抑制该信号必须同时削弱后门。

Result: 在多种后门类型和模型家族上，SpecGuard 可靠检测触发行为，包括输入级过滤器失效的隐蔽情形，同时避免现有运行时检测器的额外生成成本。投机解码因而可兼作检测后门 LLM 行为的免费、常开的信号。

Conclusion: 投机解码可被重新利用为免费、常开的后门检测信号，SpecGuard 在零额外模型计算下实现了对隐蔽后门的可靠推理期检测。

Abstract: Large language models are often fine-tuned, shared, or downloaded from third parties, so a deployed model may carry a hidden backdoor that behaves normally on benign inputs but switches to attacker-controlled behavior when a secret trigger appears. While backdoors can be audited before deployment, runtime monitoring remains important for models that are frequently updated. The challenge is that LLM serving is latency-sensitive: existing inference-time detectors either rely on assumptions about the trigger form, which can fail on stealthy attacks, or require extra model computation, such as input perturbations or an additional generation pass.
  We introduce SpecGuard, an inference-time backdoor detector that repurposes speculative decoding at zero added model-computation cost. Speculative decoding speeds up inference by using a small draft model to propose tokens and a target model to verify them. We observe that this verification process already exposes a useful signal: when a backdoor is triggered, the target model shifts toward the attacker's behavior, while a clean draft model does not predict this shift, causing the draft-token acceptance rate to change.
  We formalize when this signal appears and show that an attacker who suppresses it must also weaken the backdoor. Across diverse backdoor types and model families, SpecGuard reliably detects triggered behavior, including stealthy cases where input-level filters are blind, while avoiding the extra generation cost of existing runtime detectors. Speculative decoding therefore doubles as a free, always-on signal for detecting backdoored LLM behavior.

</details>


### [197] [Don't Trust the Super-App: A Case Study of Russia's Max](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11814)
*Richa Priyanka,Aaron Ortwein,Joel Reardon,Michael Specter,Piyush Kumar Sharma,Roya Ensafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出既往超级应用安全研究均默认其可信，但该假设难以成立；作者以MAX为例证明恶意超级应用可静默获取小程序UI、读写本地存储、注入JavaScript、中介网络流量并操控认证上下文，从而在无痕情况下侵害小程序与用户安全与隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超级应用作为承载第三方小程序的移动架构，历经十年安全研究均假设其是可信中介。然而现实证据表明这一隐含信任难以成立：部分超级应用已被证明大规模被动追踪用户跨小程序活动，或其母公司深度介入网络言论治理，或依靠国家支持在长期断网环境中运行。作者因此质疑该信任假设的合理性。

Method: 作者以MAX为案例进行安全能力分析，验证恶意超级应用在架构设计赋予的特权下可实施的具体攻击：捕获小程序UI、读写小程序本地存储、向小程序运行时注入任意JavaScript、中介小程序网络流量，以及控制认证上下文以支持静默用户冒充。作者并进一步论证这些能力源于架构设计本身，因此存在于任何超级应用中。

Result: 研究表明恶意超级应用具备确凿能力，可在不留下任何痕迹的情况下静默破坏小程序与用户的安全性和隐私。这些能力并非MAX特有缺陷，而是任何超级应用因架构特权而普遍具备的特性。

Conclusion: 本文呼吁移动操作系统与应用商店紧急介入，在该架构盲点被进一步利用之前予以弥补，以保障小程序生态中用户的安全与隐私。

Abstract: Super-apps, an emerging mobile architecture, host third-party mini-apps inside a single app, allowing users to access diverse services. A decade of security research on the super-app ecosystem has all assumed super-apps to be a trusted intermediary. We argue this implicit trust is difficult to justify: China's WeChat is already shown to passively track its user's activity across mini-apps at extraordinary scale; Russia's MAX's parent company is reported to be deeply entangled with the state prosecution of online speech; and Iran's Bale was reported to be functioning in the world's longest internet shutdown due to its state-backed support.
  In this paper, we show how malicious super-apps have undeniable capabilities to silently undermine the security and privacy of mini-apps and users without leaving any trace. Using MAX as an example, we show how it can capture mini-app UI, read and write mini-app local storage, inject arbitrary JavaScript into a mini-app's runtime, mediate mini-app network traffic, and control authentication context in ways that can enable silent user impersonation. Sadly, these capabilities manifest themselves in any super-app because of the architectural privileges granted to them by design. We argue that mobile OS and app store interventions are urgently needed to close this architectural blind spot before it is further exploited.

</details>


### [198] [Atlas: Efficient Verifiable Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11841)
*Nikolay Avramov,Hidde Lycklama,Alexander Viand,Anwar Hithnawi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Atlas 提出了首个可验证的图基语义搜索系统，通过为 HNSW 设计新型零知识证明，在不泄露索引的前提下证明查询正确执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语义搜索中，服务提供商同时控制索引与查询执行，客户只能被动信任结果来自正确的算法与预期的索引。提供商可能截断搜索以降低成本、偏置结果或暗中偏离既定执行。可验证性可以消除这一信任假设，但大规模检索依赖 HNSW 这类图算法，其数据依赖的遍历难以映射到零知识证明的固定约束系统，因此已有可验证系统只能针对更易编码的规则聚类索引，牺牲了图搜索的召回率。

Method: Atlas 的核心是针对 HNSW 搜索的新型零知识证明，建立在三项技术之上：(1) 预处理，将依赖数据库的开销转移到离线阶段，使单次查询的证明成本随遍历规模而非数据库规模增长；(2) 将 HNSW 重构为固定大小状态的流程，并证明其返回结果与原算法一致；(3) 时间步标记批处理，将整个遍历中各步骤的论证合并为一个。

Result: Atlas 首次实现了大规模可验证的图基搜索：在 SIFT1M 基准上单次查询证明时间不到 1 秒，在 1 亿向量规模下为 2.0 秒，同时保持与明文 HNSW 相同的召回率，且除结果外不泄露索引的任何信息。在完整的 RAG 流水线中，Atlas 的可验证检索保持了端到端答案质量，并以更低的证明成本达到优于所有既有可验证检索系统的质量。

Conclusion: Atlas 通过为 HNSW 定制零知识证明，突破了以往可验证检索系统只能处理规则索引、牺牲召回率的限制，实现了兼顾大规模、高召回与低证明成本的可验证图基语义搜索。

Abstract: Semantic search is a core primitive of modern applications, powering recommender systems, web search, and retrieval-augmented generation for language models. The provider controls the index and query execution, leaving clients to trust that results come from the right algorithm over the intended index. A provider may truncate search to cut cost, bias results, or otherwise deviate from the specified execution undetected. Verifiability can remove this trust assumption by proving that results follow the agreed algorithm over a committed index. Realizing this efficiently is hard, as retrieval at scale relies on HNSW, a graph-based algorithm whose data-dependent traversal maps poorly onto the fixed constraint systems of zero-knowledge proofs. Prior verifiable systems therefore target regular, cluster-based indices that are easier to encode, sacrificing the recall of graph-based search. We present Atlas, a system that lets a provider prove a query was answered correctly against its committed index without revealing the index. At its core is a new zero-knowledge proof for HNSW search, built on three techniques: preprocessing that shifts all database-dependent cost offline, so per-query proving scales with the traversal rather than the database; a restructuring of HNSW into a fixed-size-state procedure that we prove returns the same result; and a timestep-tagged batching that merges the per-step arguments of the entire traversal into one. Atlas is the first to demonstrate verifiable graph-based search at scale, proving a query in under a second on the SIFT1M benchmark and in 2.0 seconds at 100 million vectors, while maintaining the recall of plaintext HNSW and revealing nothing about the index beyond the result. In a complete RAG pipeline, Atlas' proven retrieval preserves end-to-end answer quality, and reaches higher quality at lower proving cost than all prior verifiable retrieval systems.

</details>


### [199] [BlueSTAR: Tiered Agentic Architecture for Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11852)
*Simona Boboila,Xavier Cadet,Edward Koh,Daniel Balasubramanian,Dirk Van Bruggen,Peter Chin,Alina Oprea*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BlueSTAR是一种面向企业IT/OT网络的自主网络防御分层智能体架构，可将海量安全遥测压缩为紧凑的妥协指标，并引入兼顾攻击者可达范围、关键任务资产影响与防御副作用的韧性度量；在两个实弹企业IT/OT靶场、七条基于真实入侵技术的攻击链上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络攻击日益自动化，压缩了人类分析师检测、推理与响应的窗口；LLM虽能关联异构证据并推理未知威胁，但直接用于运营安全遥测不现实——原始日志产生速度快于模型处理能力、单条事件语义模糊、无约束的LLM动作会带来显著运营风险。

Method: 提出BlueSTAR分层智能体架构：首先将高吞吐安全遥测转化为紧凑的妥协指标（IOC），以缓解处理速度与日志到达速率的失配；同时引入韧性度量，联合刻画攻击者可达范围、对关键任务资产的影响，以及防御动作造成的业务中断。

Result: 在两个真实企业IT/OT网络靶场上、基于七条真实世界入侵技术攻击链进行评估。BlueSTAR在已知威胁上保持确定性响应的快速遏制能力，同时成功防御需要上下文与跨周期推理的攻击，包括凭证窃取、重复入侵、并发攻击者以及针对物理过程的攻击。

Conclusion: 分层智能体设计可在控制运营风险的前提下，将LLM的推理能力用于自主网络防御，兼顾已知威胁的快速遏制与复杂、跨周期攻击的应对，并通过韧性度量对攻防效果进行综合评估。

Abstract: Cyber attacks are increasingly automated, narrowing the time available for human analysts to detect, reason about, and respond to intrusions. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising foundation for autonomous cyber defense because they can correlate heterogeneous evidence and reason about previously unseen threats. However, directly applying LLMs to operational security telemetry is impractical: raw logs arrive faster than current models can process them, individual events are often ambiguous, and unconstrained LLM actions can introduce significant operational risk. We present BlueSTAR, a tiered agentic architecture for autonomous cyber defense in enterprise IT/OT networks. BlueSTAR first transforms high-volume security telemetry into compact indicators of compromise. We further introduce a resilience metric that jointly captures attacker reach, impact on mission-critical assets, and disruption caused by defensive actions. We evaluate BlueSTAR on two live enterprise IT/OT cyber ranges using seven attack chains based on real-world intrusion techniques. Across attack chains, BlueSTAR retains the fast containment of deterministic response for known threats while successfully defending against attacks requiring contextual and cross-cycle reasoning, including credential theft, repeated compromise, concurrent attackers, and attacks against physical processes.

</details>


### [200] [From Specs to Apps: Verifying and Monitoring Models of Signal and WhatsApp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11882)
*Moustafa Said,Aurora Naska,Kevin Morio,Robert Künnemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文应用运行时监控工具SpecMon，对WhatsApp Web与Signal Desktop的Signal协议实现进行形式化建模与运行时验证，弥合协议规范与实现运行行为之间的差距，并发现libsignal原库与WhatsApp分叉间的未记录差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Signal协议为数十亿用户（WhatsApp、Signal应用）提供通信安全，计算模型与Dolev-Yao模型下已有强形式化安全保证；但协议规约的保证与实现运行时实际行为之间仍存在鸿沟，缺乏对实际执行是否遵循形式化协议模型的验证。

Method: 采用SpecMon运行时监控器，对WhatsApp Web和Signal Desktop两应用进行插桩，捕获其与网络及密码学组件的交互；据此构建两个与Tamarin兼容的多集重写（multiset-rewrite）模型，首个为WhatsApp Web的Signal协议实现模型，另一个为迄今最详细的Signal原始协议模型；对核心组件验证认证与保密性质，并通过模糊测试、注入安全故障进行监控评估。

Result: 监控表明观测到的执行（相对于受信任的事件抽取与符号抽象）符合这些模型；对Signal协议核心组件验证了认证与保密性质；监控揭示了原始libsignal库与WhatsApp分叉之间此前未记录的差异；实测环境下监控开销低，能高效监控真实应用并检测人为注入的安全故障；开发WhatsApp Web模型、插桩、增加模糊测试并运行实验共耗时三周人时，方法具有可复现性。

Conclusion: SpecMon运行时监控可有效弥合Signal协议规范与实现运行行为间的差距，验证实现层面的认证与保密性质，并发现实现差异；该方法高效、低开销且可复现，为协议实现的形式化运行时验证提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The Signal protocol is a prominent messaging protocol that secures communication for billions of users. It powers WhatsApp, the most widely used messaging application worldwide, and the Signal app, popular among privacy-conscious users. Extensive research in the computational and Dolev-Yao settings provides strong formal security guarantees for the protocol itself. However, a gap remains between the guarantees of the protocol specification and the implementation's actual behavior at runtime. In this work, we bridge this gap by applying SpecMon, a recently proposed runtime monitor, to check whether observed executions conform to formal protocol models. To this end, we instrument two applications (WhatsApp Web and Signal Desktop) to capture their interactions with the network and the cryptographic components. Using this instrumentation, we develop two multiset-rewrite models that are compatible with Tamarin, thus enabling verification. We derive the first model of WhatsApp Web's implementation of the Signal protocol and the most detailed model to date of Signal's original protocol. Monitoring establishes that observed executions conform to these models, relative to the trusted event extraction and the symbolic abstraction. For the core components of the Signal protocol, we verify authentication and secrecy properties. Finally, monitoring reveals previously undocumented differences between the original libsignal library and WhatsApp's fork. We evaluate our methodology and demonstrate its reproducibility. Developing the WhatsApp Web model, instrumenting the app, adding fuzzing, and running the experiments took three person-weeks. We also demonstrate efficient monitoring of real-world applications and detection of deliberately injected security faults, with low overhead in our measured setting.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.BM'></div>

# q-bio.BM [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Sequence-Informed Geometric Evaluation of RNA 3D Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10644)
*Andrea Zerio,Yighua Yao,Alessandro Micheli,Roland G. Huber,Mile Sikic,Samir Bhatt,Andres R. Masegosa,Yuangang Pan*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: 提出SIRGE——一个序列信息驱动的几何评估器，将结构表示条件化于预训练RNA语言模型的核苷酸嵌入，用于对同一序列产生的海量候选构象进行可靠排序与筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算RNA结构流程会为同一序列生成大量候选构象，仅凭几何合理性不足以可靠评估，关键在于判断该几何构象是否与给定序列相容。现有评估器缺乏序列感知能力，无法提供可靠的排序信息。

Method: SIRGE将结构表示条件化在预训练RNA语言模型输出的核苷酸嵌入上，实现序列信息与几何推理的融合；并通过与仅几何模型的对齐对照实验，检验序列条件化的独立贡献。

Result: 早期结果显示SIRGE在Kendall-τ一致性、Top-1选择和Top-3排序指标上均优于已有评估器；受控比较表明序列条件化能纠正同等几何模型的错误，并改善目标层面的排序结构。

Conclusion: 预训练序列表示可提供补充几何推理的排序信息，序列条件化几何评估是提升RNA候选构象评判可靠性的有效方向，但结论尚属初步证据。

Abstract: Computational RNA structure pipelines generate many candidate conformations for the same sequence. Reliable evaluation therefore requires more than recognising plausible geometry, it requires determining whether that geometry is compatible with the sequence. We introduce SIRGE, a sequence-informed geometric evaluator that conditions structural representations on nucleotide embeddings from a pretrained RNA language model. Early results show that SIRGE outperforms established evaluators in Kendall--$τ$ alignment, Top-1 selection, and Top-3 ranking. Controlled comparisons further show that sequence conditioning corrects errors made by an otherwise matched geometric model and improves target-level rank structure. These findings provide initial evidence that pretrained sequence representations supply ranking information that complements geometric reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出M3-Former，一种由大语言模型增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架，融合静态属性与航行意图作为语义先验，并采用双粒度MoE与转向加权交叉熵损失，在丹麦AIS数据集上1~4小时预测任务中均优于SOTA，4小时ADE/FDE分别降低4.4%和5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船舶轨迹预测面临三大挑战：行为多模态性、语义信息利用不足、长期误差累积。现有方法难以在长时间跨度上有效整合静态语义与动态轨迹信息，且稀疏转向样本的长尾分布导致关键机动场景预测精度不足。

Method: 1) 引入船舶静态属性与航行意图作为语义先验；2) 构建统一多模态表示空间，用预训练LLM编码静态语义信息，并通过自注意力与动态轨迹特征对齐；3) 设计双粒度Mixture-of-Experts架构，序列级专家建模全局航线趋势，token级专家细化局部机动行为；4) 提出Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy损失，缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布问题。

Result: 在真实丹麦AIS数据集上，M3-Former在1至4小时各预测时域上一致优于SOTA基线；4小时预测任务中，相较最强基线ADE降低4.4%、FDE降低5.1%。定性与消融分析表明，语义融合有效减少长期轨迹漂移，双粒度MoE提升了复杂航道与航线分支场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了语义引导的层次化预测范式，将高层航行意图与局部运动动态联合建模，实现鲁棒的长期船舶轨迹预测。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [203] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Halo，一种将异方差预测（同时估计位置参数与尺度参数）复用于点估计改进的通用改造方法，在五个电力价格市场的基准上，对三种主流深度预测模型配合高斯与拉普拉斯损失均显著提升 MSE/MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测通常被用于不确定性量化；而时序领域之外的研究曾报告异方差估计对点估计有负面效果。作者希望验证在时间序列预测中，引入尺度参数估计是否反而能改善点估计精度。

Method: Halo 复用已有深度预测器的架构，增加第二个输出用于预测其隐含分布的尺度参数，并以匹配的负对数似然（高斯或拉普拉斯）进行训练。作者在三种 SOTA 模型（Transformer、图网络+变分自编码器、单层卷积网络）上适配该方法，并在标准预测基准的五个电力价格市场上评估。

Result: 在 30 组“模型-市场-指标”比较中，Halo 使 28 组的 MSE 与 MAE 得到提升；平均 MSE 降低 2.6% 至 16.5%，平均 MAE 降低 1.7% 至 11.0%。两项发现：(1) 尺度估计来自第二个投影头还是完整并行网络影响远小于“是否估计尺度”本身；(2) 在已为点估计基线调优的超参数下改进依然成立，重新调参并非必需。

Conclusion: 异方差预测在时间序列中不仅服务于不确定性量化，还能一致地提升点估计性能；该增益对尺度模块结构不敏感，且无需重新调参，是一种低成本、可广泛嵌入现有深度预测框架的改进手段。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [204] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练框架，将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型经分数蒸馏采样（SDS）蒸馏进密度法拓扑优化的物理循环，使自然语言提示成为可机器解释的设计意图表示；在245次SDS运行中，38/49个提示—域组合显著降低柔顺度（力学最高-31.5%，热弹性-23.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然承载构型（叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网）具有高比刚度，但经典拓扑优化难以复现此类几何，且工程师难以用自然语言表达结构设计意图。

Method: 将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练的设计知识源，通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）蒸馏进密度法拓扑优化循环；每次迭代中，提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度结合，由物理决定哪些生成特征得以保留；针对扩散—物理耦合产生的中密度倾向，采用带β-continuation的Heaviside投影进行消除，并以自动化骨架管道将优化密度场转为可用于CAD的候选几何。

Result: 245次主要SDS运行涵盖4个几何域、2种物理机制，49个提示—域组合中38个实现统计显著的柔顺度下降（力学最高-31.5%，热弹性-23.0%），优于梯度基线；跨域形态分析发现一致的结构改善特征——多数域中生成先验抑制肋骨架的末端分支，端点—柔顺度相关性r=+0.56至+0.99；Heaviside投影使中密度比例从42.6%降至<3%。

Conclusion: 通过更换文本提示即可将生成先验跨域、跨载荷与跨物理目标重定向（每个新问题的物理设置单独指定），表明预训练生成模型可作为可复用、免训练的工程设计先验。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [205] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在室内火灾检测中的应用，针对上行带宽受限、拜占庭客户端及单一固定聚合服务器三个实际障碍，提出了配套数据集、可边缘部署的压缩检测器以及半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测系统依赖边缘摄像头，其敏感视频难以集中收集，因此适合联邦学习；但现有联邦方案未解决三个实际问题：上行带宽有限、存在拜占庭（恶意或故障）客户端，以及对单一永久固定聚合服务器的无条件信任。

Method: 方法包括三部分：(i) 从八个公开来源整理构建室内火灾检测数据集；(ii) 设计可边缘部署的检测器，对模型更新进行最高10倍压缩，且均衡准确率损失较小；(iii) 提出半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，将历史感知聚合与轮换协调者相结合。

Result: 在留出测试集上，轮换协调者方法在准确率和检测速度上与固定服务器对应方法相当；轮换协调机制能够剔除单轮过滤器遗漏的隐蔽攻击，同时消除固定服务器的单点故障；物理分布式的六节点云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为室内火灾检测提供了从数据集、压缩边缘检测器到半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习的完整方案，在保持准确率与检测速度的同时提升了鲁棒性、带宽效率与部署可行性。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [206] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述2015年以来AI与深度学习在肺癌影像检测中的应用，指出CNN结合迁移学习与数据增强可提升诊断准确率与效率，但仍面临数据标准化、可解释性、隐私与伦理等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌死亡率高，早期诊断对改善预后至关重要；但医学影像解读复杂且依赖专业受训人员，人工智能与深度学习有望实现影像分析的自动化与优化。

Method: 在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus、Web of Science等数据库进行系统性检索，筛选出96篇2015年至今关于AI与深度学习在生物医学工程中应用的文献，重点分析卷积神经网络（CNN）、迁移学习与数据增强等技术。

Result: AI与深度学习在肺癌早期诊断中可作为有效替代方案，具有较高的敏感性与特异性；CNN结合迁移学习与数据增强是提升影像解读准确性与效率的潜力技术。

Conclusion: AI与深度学习对肺癌患者诊疗具有积极影响，但需进一步研究与监管，并解决数据标准化缺失、模型可解释性、患者隐私及伦理社会影响等问题，以确保其临床应用的可靠性、安全性与责任性。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [207] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoSteer：将保范激活引导建模为黎曼优化问题，通过表示流形上的多步测地线小步更新，并学习非线性目标函数自适应确定方向，在TruthfulQA等基准上优于现有激活引导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保范激活引导方法依赖预定义的引导轨迹与单步更新，难以刻画激活分布的复杂结构，限制了引导效果与稳定性。

Method: 将引导建模为黎曼优化问题，在表示流形上以一系列小测地线步长更新激活；学习一个区分为期望/非期望激活的非线性激活空间目标函数，用以自适应指导每一步引导方向，从而替代固定引导方向。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts和UltraFeedback三个基准上，GeoSteer一致优于当前最先进的激活引导基线，同时保持激活范数不变，引导行为更平滑、稳定且一致。

Conclusion: 用自适应、几何感知的优化替代预定义的单步编辑，可使保范激活引导更加有效。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [208] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究共形校准迁移问题：当仅有源空间标注校准数据、需要通过未标注配对观测将预测集迁移到目标空间时，提出TCC方法实现目标域覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测依赖校准数据与部署数据可交换，但实际中标注校准数据仅存在于源空间，目标空间只有未标注配对观测，导致可交换性失效，直接迁移校准会产生覆盖偏差。

Method: 提出Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)：先用配对数据将源域标注校准传输到目标空间，再利用未标注目标输入校正残余失配。两种实例化：TCC-KS用无标签不确定性代理检测失配并进行保守校准调整；weighted-TCC在权重稳定时对迁移校准重加权以提升效率。给出适应可观测失配度量的有限样本目标域覆盖保证。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C和SEN12MS上，无需目标域标注校准数据即可实现可靠的目标域覆盖迁移，且无标签诊断指标能预测何时需要校正。

Conclusion: TCC通过在源-目标配对传输基础上进行无标签失配校正，实现了不可交换设定下的目标域共形覆盖保证，为跨域/跨模态共形校准提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs.
