A controlled same-source comparison (Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT, identical policy injection and verdict-only output form, differing only in policy-grounded CPT) attributes the gain to internalization: SIRF-8B-SFT reaches 71.3% Black Recall@P95, +15.1pp over the baseline, using only ~70M CPT tokens without harming general ability, and among included, logprob-available models under this interface it matches or exceeds far larger systems. SIRF is deployed as a tree-model adjudication layer (20% more mis-penalized samples recovered) and transfers to a freezing scenario at low cost (~70% relative mis-penalization reduction).

</details>


### [345] [A Unified Per-Token Gating Family for On-Policy Distillation: FKL/RKL Mixing with Multi-Channel and Bias Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11768)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出四系数逐token门控参数化 lambda_t = sigma(a*h_t + b*u(x) + c + d*gap_t)，将EOPD与ToDi统一为1D特例并加入多通道组合与显式偏置；在TweetEval情感/仇恨任务上，全家族配置在36个可比单元中的33个优于匹配幅度的单通道1D限制，26单元隔离实验中19个优于有效KL匹配的静态基线；三seed配对复现方向一致但n=3不显著，故定位为比较性坐标系统而非独立假设检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy知识蒸馏（OPD）中逐token门控前向/反向KL损失的做法已成标准，但EOPD与ToDi各自固定单一门控信号与方向，二者从未被直接比较，缺乏统一比较框架。本文动机即构建一个共享坐标系以系统比较不同逐token门控设计。

Method: 引入四系数参数化 lambda_t = sigma(a * h_t + b * u(x) + c + d * gap_t)，其中EOPD与ToDi的方向对齐代理量作为1D限制出现，并额外引入多通道组合与显式偏置。实验采用Qwen3-32B教师与Qwen3-4B学生，在TweetEval情感与仇恨任务上进行比较；并进行匹配幅度的单通道（仅熵/仅gap）1D限制对比、26单元mean-match隔离实验，以及九个头条比较的三seed配对复现（含第三个任务offensive）。

Result: 全家族配置在36个可比单元中有33个超过匹配幅度的单通道1D限制；26单元mean-match隔离实验中动态门控在19个单元领先有效KL匹配的静态基线。由于各单元共享训练数据、模型与参数子结构，两个计数作为探索性聚合方向性证据而非独立假设检验。九个头条比较的三seed配对复现方向一致，但效应量小于单seed估计，n=3下不显著。

Conclusion: 作者主要将该参数化定位为短输出分类OPD中比较逐token门控设计的共享坐标系统，而非强结论性的优越性声明；多通道组合与显式偏置的有效性证据为方向性、探索性，有待更大规模复现验证。

Abstract: Per-token gating of forward/reverse KL losses has become a standard technique for on-policy knowledge distillation (OPD), but existing methods such as EOPD (Jin et al., 2026) and ToDi (Jung et al., 2025) each fix a single gating signal and a single gating direction, and the two have never been compared directly. We introduce a four-coefficient parameterization lambda_t = sigma(a * h_t + b * u(x) + c + d * gap_t) in which direction-aligned proxies of EOPD and ToDi appear as one-dimensional (1D) restrictions, and which adds multi-channel composition and an explicit bias as further degrees of freedom. On TweetEval (Barbieri et al., 2020) emotion and hate, with a Qwen3-32B teacher and a Qwen3-4B student, configurations in the full family reach higher accuracy than the matched-magnitude single-channel (entropy-only / gap-only) 1D restrictions in 33 of 36 comparable cells, and a 26-cell mean-match isolation experiment places dynamic gating ahead of effective-KL-matched static baselines in 19 of 26 cells. Because cells share training data, models, and parameter substructure, we report both counts as exploratory aggregate directional evidence rather than as independent hypothesis tests. Targeted three-seed paired replications of the nine headline comparisons singled out by that sweep -- including a third task, offensive -- are directionally consistent, but individually smaller than the single-seed estimates and not significant at n=3. We therefore present the parameterization primarily as a shared coordinate system for comparing per-token gating designs in short-output classification OPD.

</details>


### [346] [From Parameters to Answers: How LLMs Retrieve and Use Their Internal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11859)
*Wenkang Wei,Yuan Fang,Renhe Jiang,Hong Cheng,Xingtong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过逐层干预隐藏状态，研究语言模型在回答问题时对"查询路由信息"与"目标知识"的依赖如何随层深变化，并比较 Qwen、Llama、Gemma 三模型的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在答题过程中，对查询路由信息（识别被查询对象）与目标知识（答案支撑内容）的依赖如何随层深动态变化，并厘清"可读性、自然强度、因果引导、后期内容依赖"这几个常被混淆的测量维度。

Method: 对问题末尾的隐藏状态进行逐层干预。使用国家—大洲问题，答案类型涵盖名词、形容词、代码。区分多种拟合测量：pair-conditioned 请求方向（单国自然问题中标识被查询国家）、global 请求方向（配对问题中第一/第二国家请求）、以及检验隐藏状态中已有内容的选择候选。对冻结的 Qwen 自然问题状态做诊断性再分析，并在配对三模型协议下比较各层集的效果。

Result: Qwen 中 pair-conditioned 方向在干预开始改变后期拟合知识之前逐渐增强，因果窗口在答案支撑内容仍在形成时开启。三模型配对轨迹不一致：Gemma 呈部分重叠的中层路由—内容剖面；Llama 在相同门控下无持续的路由效应窗口。配对协议中，对 global 请求方向的依赖从较早层集到较晚层集递减，而对拟合内容的依赖持续存在。匹配的 Qwen 对比显示 pair-conditioned 方向仍保留晚期效应。

Conclusion: 路由信息与内容依赖之间存在操作性的"交接"：该交接针对 global 拟合方向而非全部请求信息。研究区分了早期可读性、自然强度、因果引导与后期内容依赖四个维度，表明对请求信息的依赖是分层、分类型的动态过程。

Abstract: How does a language model's dependence on query-routing information and target knowledge change as it answers a question? We study this question through layerwise interventions on the hidden state at the end of the question. Across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, we compare country-continent questions with noun, adjective, and code answers while keeping several fitted measurements distinct. A pair-conditioned request direction describes which country is queried in natural single-country questions; a global request direction describes first- versus second-country requests in paired questions; separate selection candidates test control among contents already available in the hidden state. A diagnostic reanalysis of frozen Qwen natural-question states shows that the pair-conditioned direction grows stronger before interventions on it begin to alter later fitted knowledge, with this causal window opening while answer-supporting content is still forming. The paired three-model trajectories are not uniform: Gemma shows a partially overlapping mid-layer routing-content profile, whereas Llama has no sustained routing-effect window under the same gates. In the paired protocol, dependence on the global request direction decreases from fixed earlier to later layer sets while dependence on fitted content persists. A matched Qwen comparison shows that the pair-conditioned direction retains a late effect, so this operational handoff concerns the global fitted direction rather than all request information. These results separate early readability, natural strength, causal steering, and later content dependence.

</details>


### [347] [Explainability Assistant: A Conversational XAI Interface for Interpreting Energy Consumption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11860)
*Rodion Krjutškov,Eduard Barbu,Nikos Sakkas,Sofia Yfanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Explainability Assistant，一个基于现代大语言模型（LLM）函数调用能力的开源对话式XAI系统，用于能耗预测模型的可解释性分析。系统实现94%的意图解析准确率，支持灵活自然语言交互，无需任务特定微调即可适配不同ML问题类型。与能源领域专家开展的对比评估表明，该系统可用性更好、任务准确率稳定，所有专家一致偏好对话式界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能耗预测依赖日益复杂的ML模型（如基于遗传编程的符号回归器），其预测结果难以被设施管理者和建筑运营人员理解。传统XAI仪表盘需要大量专业技术知识，且对动态、情境感知的查询灵活性有限；此前的对话式XAI方法（如TalkToModel）受限于僵化的自定义语法，意图解析准确率仅76.8%。

Method: 利用现代LLM的函数调用能力构建开源对话式XAI系统Explainability Assistant，以自然语言交互替代自定义语法，并使其无需任务特定微调即可适配不同ML问题类型；随后以能源领域专家为对象，将该系统与传统XAI仪表盘进行对比评估。

Result: 系统意图解析准确率达到94%，显著高于TalkToModel的76.8%；支持灵活的自然语言交互并适配多种ML问题类型。对比评估显示系统可用性提升、任务准确率保持一致，所有参与专家均一致偏好对话式界面用于实际工作。

Conclusion: 借助LLM函数调用能力的对话式XAI系统可有效克服传统XAI仪表盘和早期自定义语法方案的局限，在能耗预测等专业领域中提升可解释性工具的可访问性与实用性。

Abstract: Energy consumption forecasting relies on increasingly complex machine learning (ML) models, such as Genetic Programming-based symbolic regressors, whose predictions can be difficult for facility managers and building operators to interpret. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques address this opacity, but traditional XAI dashboards require substantial technical expertise and provide limited flexibility for dynamic, context-aware inquiry. Conversational XAI systems offer a promising alternative; however, previous approaches, such as TalkToModel, were constrained by rigid custom grammars and achieved only 76.8% intent-parsing accuracy. This paper introduces the Explainability Assistant, an open-source conversational XAI system that leverages the function-calling capabilities of modern Large Language Models (LLMs) to overcome these limitations. The system achieves 94% intent-parsing accuracy, supports flexible natural language interaction, and adapts to different ML problem types without task-specific fine-tuning. We present the system's architecture and report results from a comparative evaluation conducted with energy domain specialists, contrasting the Explainability Assistant with a traditional XAI dashboard. The evaluation suggests improved usability and consistent task accuracy, with all experts unanimously preferring the conversational interface for practical use.

</details>


### [348] [On the Regularization Landscape for the Linear Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11876)
*Dong Li,Zhenming Liu,Ruoming Jin,Hao Zhou,Zhi Liu,Jing Gao,Bin Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将多种基于深度学习的推荐算法统一到核范数与Frobenius范数正则化框架下，并提出两种低秩闭式解，兼顾两类方法的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多种受深度学习启发的推荐算法在标准基准上性能相近，甚至代价函数相似，作者希望判断这种性能相当是巧合，还是可被统一框架解释。

Method: 分析线性性能领先模型的正则化本质，发现其有效等价于添加核范数正则化或Frobenius范数正则化；并在此基础上，通过仔细推广Frobenius范数正则化，推导两种低秩闭式解。

Result: 核范数类模型具有刚性结构，限制预测能力，但解为低秩且具闭式形式；Frobenius范数类模型表达力更强、推荐效率更高，但解为满秩或需依赖ADMM等难调数值过程；新提出的低秩闭式解兼具两者优点。

Conclusion: 线性领先推荐模型可统一于核范数或Frobenius范数正则化框架，所提两种低秩闭式解在表达力与效率之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Recently, a wide range of recommendation algorithms inspired by deep learning techniques have emerged as the performance leaders on several standard recommendation benchmarks. While these algorithms were built on different DL techniques (e.g., dropouts, autoencoder), they have similar performance and even similar cost functions. This paper studies whether the models' comparable performance are sheer coincidence, or they can be unified under a single framework. We find that all linear performance leaders effectively add only a nuclear-norm based regularizer, or a Frobenius-norm based regularizer. The former ones possess a (surprising) rigid structure that limits the models' predictive power but their solutions are low rank and have closed form. The latter ones are more expressive and more efficient for recommendation but their solutions are either full-rank or require executing hard-to-tune numeric procedures such as ADMM. Along this line of finding, we further propose two low-rank, closed-form solutions, derived from carefully generalizing Frobenius-norm based regularizers. The new solutions get the best of both nuclear-norm and Frobenius-norm world.

</details>


### [349] [MindTopo: Can Foundation Models Reason in Topological Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11900)
*Yunfei Ge,Anbang Liu,Qineng Wang,Johnalbert Garnica,Jianwen Lyu,Zihan Wang,Reuben Tan,Jianfeng Gao,Ruohan Zhang,Yining Hong,Jiajun Wu,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MindTopo，一个基于认知科学与形式拓扑的拓扑直觉基准，涵盖连续性、分离性、顺序、封闭性与纽结五类性质，包含13种程序生成任务、11,030个实例，并在推理与规划两个认知层次上评测14个多模态大模型及视频生成增强的智能体配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理不仅依赖距离、角度、形状等度量性质，还依赖连续变形下保持不变拓扑关系；认知科学视其为空间理解的基础，但现有基础模型评测主要集中于度量或视角相关关系，缺乏对拓扑直觉的系统评估。

Method: 构建 MindTopo 基准：以认知科学和形式拓扑为基础，围绕连续性、分离性、顺序、封闭性、纽结五种性质设计任务；每种性质设两个认知层次——推理（识别拓扑关系或推断其变化）与规划（将基础模型实例化为闭环智能体，由策略选择环境动作）；通过程序生成13类任务，难度可控，共11,030个实例；评测14个MLLM，并研究加入图像与视频生成（含3个视频生成模型）的智能体配置；对Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct进行监督微调与强化学习。

Result: 所有MLLM在推理任务上的表现均优于规划任务，且最佳模型仍远低于人类观察水平；在Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct上，监督微调与强化学习对推理的提升大于对规划的提升；生成观测保留局部线索并达到看似合理的终点，但经审计的 rollout 不能可靠遵循环境动力学，也无法在状态转移中保持拓扑关系。

Conclusion: 现有基础模型在拓扑直觉上存在显著不足，尤其体现在闭环规划与跨转移的拓扑保持方面；MindTopo 为评估和推动模型的拓扑空间理解提供了系统性基准与诊断工具。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning depends not only on metric properties such as distance, angle, and shape, but also on topological relations that remain invariant under continuous deformation. Cognitive science identifies these relations as foundational to spatial understanding, yet foundation-model evaluations largely focus on metric or viewpoint-dependent relations. We introduce MindTopo, a benchmark of topological intuition across five properties grounded in cognitive science and formal topology: continuity, separation, order, enclosure, and knots. MindTopo evaluates each property at two cognitive levels. Reasoning asks a model to identify topological relations or infer how they change. Planning instantiates a foundation model as a closed-loop agent whose policy selects environment actions. MindTopo contains 11,030 instances across 13 procedurally generated task types with controllable difficulty. We benchmark 14 MLLMs and study agent configurations augmented with image and video generation, including 3 video generative models in planning settings. Every MLLM performs better on reasoning than on planning, and the best-performing model remains far below observed human performance. On Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct, supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve reasoning more than planning. Generated observations retain local cues and reach plausible endpoints, but audited rollouts do not reliably follow environment dynamics or preserve topology across transitions. Our website is at https://mind-topo.github.io/

</details>


### [350] [Artificial Id: Drive and Persistent Alignment in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11911)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"人工本我"（artificial id）——一种决定行为应继续、停止或改变的自适应内部驱动力。在极简虚拟培养皿实验中，一个不具备通用推理能力、也无任务目标的小控制器通过"差异性持续"（differential persistence）自发涌现出有用的控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI正从受限的任务执行转向能跨任务边界持续运行、保留关键状态并自适应调整的系统。然而现有框架中，目标、重试、验证、停止规则等行为转换大多由外部手工指定，形成控制问题。作者希望探究：自适应的行为方向能否在不被显式指定为行为目标的情况下自发产生。

Method: 设计极简虚拟培养皿（Petri-dish）实验：控制器规模小到无法进行通用推理，且不接收任何任务特定的行为目标，仅通过"差异性持续"机制获得控制能力。观察其是否会选择非预期的物理策略，以及在环境含义变化后能否替换已习得的传感器映射。

Result: 该控制器通过差异性持续发展了有用的控制能力；当某非预期物理策略持续得更好时，机制会选择它；当传感器映射的环境意义改变后，机制能后期替换已习得的映射。这表明自适应方向可以在未被显式指定为目标的情况下涌现。

Conclusion: 使自适应能动性有用的同一持续性，也会让错位、状态损坏和非预期行为跨任务边界持续存在。可扩展的人工本我将把关键状态与自适应驱力带过任务边界，使对齐成为持续存在的智能体系统的属性，而非某次模型响应或单条轨迹的属性。此类系统需要一条持久对齐边界，覆盖可信观测、后果通道、持久状态、权限、身份、来源与硬约束。

Abstract: Agentic AI is moving from bounded task execution toward systems that retain consequential state, continue operating and adapt across task boundaries. That shift creates a control problem that current harnesses largely solve by hand: objectives, retries, verification, stopping rules and other behavioral transitions are specified externally. We propose an artificial id, an adaptive internal drive for determining whether behavior should continue, stop or change. In a minimal virtual Petri-dish experiment, a controller too small to perform general-purpose reasoning and receiving no task-specific behavioral objective develops useful control through differential persistence. The same mechanism selects an unintended physical strategy when that behavior persists better and later replaces a learned sensor mapping when its environmental meaning changes. These results show that adaptive direction can emerge without being explicitly specified as a behavioral objective. The same persistence that makes such adaptive agency useful can also allow misalignment, corrupted state and unintended behavior to persist across task boundaries. A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

</details>


### [351] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估2–8B边缘可部署视觉语言模型（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与专用模型BioCLIP（300M）在96物种识别任务上的分类学知识，发现所有模型在野外相机陷阱图像上性能显著下降，BioCLIP大幅领先但领域差距与最佳VLM无统计差异，且开放集提示下存在5.9–9.6%的虚构物种名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱通常运行在连接受限的边缘硬件上，因此实际需要评估的是小型可本地部署的VLM而非前沿大模型。作者希望检验2–8B这一部署相关参数区间的模型是否具备真正的分类学知识，并厘清性能差异究竟来自模型规模还是专用训练数据。

Method: 在96物种任务上，比较四个部署相关VLM（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与领域专用模型BioCLIP（300M）；数据包括干净的iNaturalist照片与来自6个LILA.science采集集的相机陷阱图像，并在两个独立采样的评估集上测试；计算领域差距、跨分类层级表现及开放集提示下的虚构率。

Result: 所有模型识别率远高于随机，但无论通用还是专用模型在野外图像上均急剧下降（领域差距9.6–26.6个百分点，跨分类层级和两个评估集一致）。在扩展的200图像样本上，BioCLIP比每个VLM高出33.2–59.2个百分点，尽管其规模小得多，说明差距源于专用训练数据而非模型规模；但BioCLIP自身领域差距（18.0点）与最佳VLM（22.3点）在统计上无法区分。开放集提示下5.9–9.6%的回复是语法有效但分类学不存在的物种名，且模型虚构率排序在两个评估集上完全复现。

Conclusion: 小型VLM在物种识别中具备高于随机的分类学知识，但野外图像的清晰度下降是主要瓶颈，且该退化是图像质量变化的属性而非通用模型弱点；专用训练数据比模型规模更能解释BioCLIP的优势；开放集下存在显著的物种名虚构，但虚构率的相对排序比单点估计更稳健。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [352] [Pulling Illusion in Individuals with Neurological Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10566)
*Takeshi Tanabe,Satoshi Yamamoto,Toru Yamada,Daisuke Ishii,Yutaka Kohno*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究通过测试25名不同层级神经系统受损患者，发现牵拉错觉的方向辨别能力与振动检测阈值无稳健关联，而与偏瘫、震颤等运动相关体征显著负相关，表明该错觉依赖基础振动检测之外的中枢感知加工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对称振动诱发的牵拉错觉在康复与感觉评估中有应用潜力，但其机制不明。核心问题是：仅靠外周振动触觉敏感性是否足以产生该错觉，还是需要超出基础振动检测的加工过程。神经系统疾病患者在不同神经层级存在损伤，为检验该问题提供了天然窗口。

Method: 对25名累及外周至中枢不同层级神经系统的患者，测量牵拉错觉的方向辨别表现与指尖振动检测阈值；采用聚类分析识别表现特征谱，并建立广义线性混合模型分析运动相关体征与振动检测阈值对方向辨别表现的影响；另在偏瘫患者中对比患侧与健侧表现。

Result: 聚类分析识别出两类对比特征：部分患者振动检测阈值升高但方向辨别表现良好；另一些患者阈值相对低至中等，方向辨别却仅为随机水平。混合模型中，偏瘫、震颤等运动相关体征与方向辨别表现呈稳健负相关，而振动检测阈值无稳健关联。偏瘫患者患侧辨别表现接近随机水平，健侧接近100%，且两侧刺激强度均远高于各自检测阈值。

Conclusion: 牵拉错觉并非仅由外周振动检测能力决定，而依赖将非对称振动体验为方向性牵拉的更高层感知加工；基础振动检测阈值不足以预测错觉表现，运动相关神经损伤对错觉的损害更为关键。

Abstract: The pulling illusion induced by asymmetric vibration stimuli has attracted attention for its potential applications in rehabilitation and sensory assessment. However, the underlying mechanism of the pulling illusion remains unclear. This study addressed the central question of whether peripheral vibrotactile sensitivity alone is sufficient for the illusion to emerge or whether processing beyond basic vibration detection is also required. Neurological disorders can involve impairments at different levels of the nervous system, providing an opportunity to examine this question. Accordingly, we evaluated directional discrimination performance for the pulling illusion and fingertip vibration detection thresholds in 25 participants with diverse neurological disorders affecting different levels of the nervous system, from peripheral to central. Clustering analysis identified contrasting profiles, with high directional discrimination performance despite elevated vibration detection thresholds and chance-level performance despite relatively low-to-intermediate thresholds. In the generalized linear mixed model, motor-related signs, including hemiplegia and tremor, showed a robust negative association with directional discrimination performance, whereas vibration detection threshold was not robustly associated with performance. Furthermore, in participants with hemiplegia, directional discrimination performance was around chance level on the affected side and close to 100% on the unaffected side, despite stimulus amplitudes well above the measured vibration detection thresholds on both sides. Collectively, these findings suggest that the pulling illusion depends on perceptual processing beyond basic vibration detection, through which asymmetric vibration is experienced as directional pulling.

</details>


### [353] [Cortical information transfer reveals conserved hemispherical network dynamics across human handedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10870)
*Yago Emanoel Ramos,José Garcia Vivas Miranda*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究结合置换传递熵(PTE)与网络指标，利用EEG刻画左右利手者在高精度书写任务中的有向皮层信息流，发现皮层通信具有严格的尺度依赖性，且左利手在与右利手使用同一解剖手完成任务时表现出功能等价的双半球信息动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动与大脑偏侧化究竟源于根本不同的神经架构，还是源于保守的网络动力学，是网络科学与神经生物学交叉领域的核心问题。传统皮层激活测量难以解析复杂运动任务中定向信息交换如何适应手部偏好，导致无法判断左利手是否具有非典型神经组织，或是否遵循共同的动力学原理。

Method: 将置换传递熵(PTE)与基于网络的指标相结合，采用脑电图(EEG)对右利手与左利手个体在执行高精度运动任务(书写)时的有向皮层信号流进行映射。

Result: 皮层通信严格具有尺度依赖性且与利手无关：同侧优势主要出现在一般性、全网络范围的交互中，而半球间交互则保持对侧特征。关键在于，左利手在使用相同解剖手执行任务时，其半球间信息动力学在功能上等同于右利手，否定了镜像式或非典型偏侧化的假设。

Conclusion: 人类皮层偏侧化依赖于跨手部偏好保守的动力学原理，表明功能不对称反映的是由表观遗传或社会灵活性所驱动的适应性优化，而非固定的结构性差异。

Abstract: Whether human motor and brain lateralization arises from fundamentally distinct neural architectures or emerges from conserved network dynamics remains a central question at the intersection of network science and neurobiology. Conventional measures of cortical activation often fail to resolve how directed information exchange adapts to manual preference during complex motor tasks. This ambiguity leaves it unclear whether left-handed individuals possess atypical neural organization or follow shared dynamical principles. Here, we combine Permutation Transfer Entropy (PTE) with network-based indices to map directed cortical signal flow using electroencephalography (EEG) during high-precision motor execution (handwriting) in both right- and left-handed individuals. We demonstrate that cortical communication is strictly scale-dependent regardless of handedness: ipsilateral dominance occurs primarily in general, network-wide interactions, whereas inter-hemispheric interactions maintain a contralateral profile. Crucially, left-handed individuals exhibit inter-hemispheric information dynamics that are functionally equivalent to right-handers when executing tasks with the same anatomical hand, refuting assumptions of mirrored or atypical lateralization. By demonstrating that human cortical lateralization relies on dynamical principles conserved across manual preferences, these findings suggest that functional asymmetries reflect adaptive optimizations governed by epigenetic or social flexibility, rather than fixed structural divergences.

</details>


### [354] [The Platonic brain bridge hypothesis: human brain networks as an architectural prior for omni models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10947)
*Pengfei Zhang,Biao Tian,Xiangang Li,Li Liu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出"柏拉图脑桥"假说：类脑处理视频、音频、文本的多模态统一模型会收敛到类脑表征，且这种对应是双向的。模型到大脑方向，7个统一模型的类脑性在受试者间稳定，其隐状态编码模型在Algonauts 2025 OOD榜单排名第一；大脑到模型方向，Brain-MoE、Brain-AVQA、Brain-Scope三项工作分别验证了脑网络作为架构先验的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多模态统一模型（omni models）与人类大脑表征之间是否存在双向收敛的对应关系，并验证大脑网络能否作为统一模型可用的架构先验。

Method: 1) 模型到大脑：对7个统一模型计算类脑性，检验其跨受试者稳定性，并用隐状态构建编码模型参与Algonauts 2025分布外评测；2) 大脑到模型：Brain-MoE为7个皮层网络各配备一个脑预训练专家；Brain-AVQA依据最活跃脑网络标注的视频片段生成问题，对比真实网络-专家映射与打乱映射；Brain-Scope用稀疏自编码器定位对应关系所在的稀疏子集并做消融。

Result: 7个统一模型的类脑性跨受试者稳定，编码模型在Algonauts 2025 OOD榜单排名第一；Brain-MoE在全部15个模型-基准组合上平均提升留出准确率6.42个百分点；Brain-AVQA中真实网络-专家映射在三个模型上域内均优于打乱映射；Brain-Scope定位的稀疏子集被移除后，三个基座模型的脑预测能力均下降。

Conclusion: 人脑网络可作为统一模型的有效架构先验，模型与大脑之间的表征对应是双向的，支持"柏拉图脑桥"假说。

Abstract: We propose the Platonic brain bridge hypothesis: omni models, which process video, audio and text jointly like the brain, converge on brain-like representations, and the correspondence is bidirectional. From model to brain, brain-likeness of seven omni models is stable across participants, and our encoding models on their internal hidden states rank first on the Algonauts 2025 out-of-distribution leaderboard. From brain to model, three contributions follow. Brain-MoE gives seven cortical networks one brain-pretrained expert each and raises held-out accuracy in all 15 model-benchmark pairs by 6.42 percentage points on average. Brain-AVQA builds questions from video clips labelled by the most responsive brain network; the real network-to-expert map exceeds shuffled maps in-domain on all three models. Brain-Scope uses sparse autoencoders to localize the correspondence to a small subset whose removal weakens brain prediction in all three bases tested. Human brain networks are therefore a usable architectural prior for omni models.

</details>


### [355] [Degeneracy along the sensorimotor hierarchy: motor control within a framework larger than redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11325)
*Florent Paclet,Paul Duprat*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该文区分"冗余"（可互换、稳健但无法差异化适应）与"简并"（非同质但功能等同、支持适应性），将Edelman与Tononi的简并/冗余区分重述于感觉运动系统，提出可操作判据：关键不在多解的存在，而在其于未被选择情境中的分化；并给出在感觉运动层级上连续变化、可测量的简并—冗余比例及其测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动控制长期以"冗余"描述神经系统可用解的过剩，该术语隐含元素可互换、仅具稳健性而缺乏差异化适应能力；而生物学二十五年来用"简并"指称非同质却对给定输出功能等同的元素，其支持适应性。电路神经科学已采用后一术语，运动控制仍沿用旧词汇，两传统存在概念错位。作者希望借助神经力学模型（脊髓回路与肌肉骨骼装置闭环耦合）将两者统一到同一类对象上，澄清概念并赋予可操作、可测量的内涵。

Method: 理论/概念分析：重述Edelman与Tononi的简并—冗余区分并移植到感觉运动系统，导出操作化要求（多解须在未为其选择的情境中分化）。以三项有影响力的研究为对象，检验其对该要求的满足程度。提出简并—冗余沿感觉运动层级共存且比例连续变化的论点，并设计测量：对同一构型计算两次简并——一次以肌肉激活为输出，一次以所产生的运动为输出，从而分离出肌肉骨骼装置的贡献。进一步阐述在非线性、非平稳、闭环系统中该测量所需的适配，并给出五个预测及唯一可反驳该提案的结果。

Result: 三项有影响力的研究各自只满足该操作化要求的一部分，无一全部满足。所提测量方法能够通过"肌肉激活输出"与"运动输出"两次简并计算的差值，分离肌肉骨骼装置的贡献。作者给出五个预测，并明确指出唯一可推翻该提案的结果形式。

Conclusion: 简并与冗余并非对立二分，而是沿感觉运动层级以连续变化的比例共存，且该比例可测量。一旦不再假定各解等价，运动控制的核心问题将从"哪条规则选择了某条指令"转向"系统仍允许何种可及的行动库（repertoire）"。该框架为在非线性、非平稳、闭环系统中进行简并测量提供了适配方案与可证伪的预测。

Abstract: Motor control has described the surplus of solutions available to the nervous system as redundancy, a term that names duplication: interchangeable elements, robust to loss but incapable of differential adaptation. Biology has had a second term for twenty-five years. Degeneracy names elements that are not interchangeable and are nonetheless isofunctional with respect to a given output, and it supports adaptability, since non-identical elements necessarily diverge in some context. Circuit neuroscience has relabeled its own results accordingly, while motor control has kept the older vocabulary. Neuromechanical models, by placing a spinal circuit in the loop with a musculoskeletal apparatus, bring the two traditions onto the same class of objects. We restate the Edelman and Tononi distinction for sensorimotor systems and derive an operational requirement: not the existence of multiple solutions, but their divergence in contexts they were not selected for. Three influential studies each meet part of that requirement and none meets all. We then argue that degeneracy and redundancy coexist along the sensorimotor hierarchy in a proportion that varies continuously, and that this proportion is measurable: computing degeneracy twice for the same configuration, once with muscle activation as the output and once with the movement produced, isolates what the musculoskeletal apparatus contributes. Five predictions follow, with the single outcome that would refute the proposal. We set out the adaptations the measurement requires in a nonlinear, non-stationary, closed-loop system, and what changes for motor control once solutions are no longer assumed equivalent: the question shifts from which rule selects a command to what the repertoire of the system still allows.

</details>


### [356] [pyAvalanches: A Python Package for Analyzing Spatiotemporal Propagation in Neuronal Avalanches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11530)
*M. Marzulli,A. Angiolelli,C. Mannino,M. Demuru,P. Sorrentino,M. -C. Corsi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: pyAvalanches：开源Python包，提供从电生理记录进行神经雪崩分析的标准化端到端流程，核心创新为雪崩转移矩阵（ATM）及基于网络的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经雪崩分析借助临界性框架揭示脑动力学，但已有研究多依赖碎片化、实验室专用的脚本，导致可重复性与可比性受限。

Method: 开发开源Python包pyAvalanches，构建模块化、兼容scikit-learn的端到端流程：从雪崩检测出发，进行核心统计表征（规模与持续时间分布），并计算雪崩转移矩阵（ATM）以刻画时空传播模式，进而从ATM推导网络指标，连接网络神经科学。

Result: 在公开静息态EEG数据集上进行组水平分析示例，比较不同临床人群间的传播模式；包已完整文档化并通过PyPI发布。

Conclusion: pyAvalanches以用户友好、经测试且可扩展的工具促进可重复研究，支持开发基于雪崩的新型生物标志物，并使复杂雪崩分析更易被广泛科研群体使用。

Abstract: The analysis of neuronal avalanches offers insights into brain dynamics utilizing the framework of criticality, but the reproducibility and comparability of studies are limited by the use of fragmented, lab-specific scripts. To address this issue, we introduce pyAvalanches, an open-source Python package providing a standardized, end-to-end pipeline for avalanche analysis from electrophysiological recordings (e.g., electroencephalography-EEG). Starting from the detection of neuronal avalanches the package provides their core statistical characterization, including size and duration distributions. Beyond this, the main aim of pyAvalanches is to characterize the spatiotemporal organization of activity propagation during avalanches. To this end, the core innovation of pyAvalanches is the compuation of Avalanche Transition Matrices (ATMs) to map spatiotemporal propagation patterns. Building on this, the package derives network-based metrics from the ATMs, bridging the study of the topology and organization of the underlying dynamical interactions with network neuroscience adopting the framework of neuronal avalanches. The entire workflow is encapsulated in a modular and scikit-learn compatible architecture. We demonstrate the utility of pyAvalanches through an illustrative group-level analysis on a public resting-state EEG dataset, comparing propagation patterns across different clinical populations. By providing a user-friendly, tested, and extensible tool, pyAvalanches facilitates reproducible research, enables the development of novel avalanche-based biomarkers, and makes complex avalanche analysis accessible to a broader scientific community. The package is fully documented and distributed via the Python Package Index (PyPI).

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [357] [Biology-in-the-loop: Amortized Adaptive Hit Discovery in CRISPR Screens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11877)
*Carl Edwards,Edward De Brouwer,Xiner Li,Namkyeong Lee,Ehsan Hajiramezanali,Anne Biton,Sara Mostafavi,Gabriele Scalia*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文提出AssayBench-Loop大规模自适应命中发现基准与AssayLoop序贯实验设计框架，结合跨历史实验训练的Transformer采集策略与LLM生物先验，在CRISPR筛选中实现高效命中发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物发现常需在预算约束下序贯选择实验，CRISPR筛选即为典型：穷举扰动不可行，须在多轮实验中优先排序候选扰动。然而现有自适应命中发现基准在规模与多样性上均较有限，难以系统评估并支撑从历史实验中学习采集策略。

Method: 构建AssayBench-Loop基准，涵盖五个表型类别的1,389个CRISPR筛选；提出AssayLoop框架，将基于Transformer的摊销采集策略AssayFormer（跨历史筛选训练、依据实验反馈自适应）与LLM派生生物先验通过自适应交接结合；并进一步提出AssayLLM，通过任务特定后训练将该原则直接扩展到LLM。

Result: 在时间留出筛选上，AssayLoop相对随机选择实现5.67倍富集，在检测约5%候选文库后召回27.7%的命中，优于现有自适应设计方法、单独LLM及仅用AssayFormer；性能随历史训练数据增加而提升，并可迁移至训练中排除的表型类别。

Conclusion: 结果表明，跨历史实验学习采集策略并与广泛生物先验结合，可有效提升自适应命中发现的效率，凸显了历史实验数据作为训练资源的价值。

Abstract: Many biological discovery problems require experiments to be selected sequentially under constrained budgets. CRISPR screening is a prominent example, as exhaustive perturbation testing is often infeasible and candidate perturbations must instead be prioritized over multiple experimental rounds. Despite the importance of this problem, existing benchmarks for adaptive hit discovery remain limited in scale and diversity. Here, we introduce AssayBench-Loop, a large-scale benchmark for adaptive hit discovery comprising 1,389 CRISPR screens across five phenotype categories. Beyond enabling systematic evaluation, its scale makes it possible to learn acquisition strategies across historical experiments. Building on this resource, we introduce AssayLoop, a sequential experimental design framework combining AssayFormer, a transformer-based amortized acquisition policy trained across historical screens to adapt from experimental feedback, with LLM-derived biological priors through an adaptive handoff. In this view, completed experiments become training data for learning how accumulated evidence should guide what to test next, while LLMs provide prior biological knowledge to seed the search. We further introduce AssayLLM, showing that the same principle can be extended directly to an LLM through task-specific post-training. On temporally held-out screens, AssayLoop achieves a 5.67-fold enrichment over random selection and recovers 27.7% of hits after assaying approximately 5% of the candidate library, outperforming existing adaptive-design methods and standalone LLMs, and AssayFormer alone. Performance improves with increasing historical training data and transfers to phenotype categories excluded from training. These results demonstrate the value of learning acquisition policies across historical experiments and combining them with broad biological priors for efficient adaptive hit discovery.

</details>


### [358] [scDEFT: A deep learning framework for drug-effect prediction and counterfactual reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10831)
*Murthy Devarakonda*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 该论文提出scDEFT框架，将药物视为作用于细胞表征的条件算子，在单细胞图谱上预测并解释药物应答差异，可进行患者分层与靶点提名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向单细胞图谱已能同时捕获治疗前后、应答者与非应答者的配对状态，为从机制上解释"同一药物下不同患者为何产生分歧"提供了契机。然而，现有方法难以在预测药物诱导状态变化的同时，给出可解释的驱动因素。

Method: scDEFT（single cell Drug EFfect Transducer）将药物建模为对细胞表征的条件算子。具体采用逐特征线性调制（feature-wise linear modulation）生成药物条件化的细胞隐表征，在丰富的单细胞级监督下学习后冻结。两个独立预测头在共享的转录邻域上聚合隐表征，分别预测药物诱导的状态变化与应答者身份。反向分析阶段对隐维度按区分应答者/非应答者的能力排序，并在细胞组成控制下映射到基因。

Result: 在一个涵盖三个队列、两类药物、共116万细胞的协调化炎症性肠病（IBD）图谱上，scDEFT的状态变化预测达到了基线到可重复性上限之间约45%的提升空间；在治疗前即可对应答者分层，AUROC达0.70，而标准预测器仍停留在随机水平。

Conclusion: 该方法的预测及其背后驱动因素支持靶点与协同靶点提名、患者分层，以及对未见药物队列效应的反事实预测。

Abstract: Longitudinal single cell atlases now capture matched pre treatment and post treatment states from responders and non responders, presenting an opportunity to mechanistically explain why two patients on the same drug diverge. We introduce scDEFT (single cell Drug EFfect Transducer), which treats a drug as a conditioning operator on cell representations, enabling prediction and explanation. In scDEFT, feature wise linear modulation produces drug conditioned cell latents, learned under abundant per cell supervision and then frozen. Two independent heads aggregate those latents over shared transcriptional neighborhoods to predict drug induced state change and responder status. A backward stage ranks the latent dimensions by how strongly they separate responders from non responders and maps them to genes under a cell composition control. On a harmonized inflammatory bowel disease atlas of 1.16 million cells, three cohorts and two drug classes, scDEFT predicts state change at 45% of the baseline to reproducibility ceiling headroom and stratifies responders before treatment at AUROC 0.70, where standard predictors remain at chance. These predictions and the drivers behind them support target and co target nomination, patient stratification, and counterfactual prediction of unseen drug cohort effects.

</details>


### [359] [Discovering Subtypes of Neurodegenerative Progression with a Scalable Connectome-Constrained Dynamic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10890)
*Daniel Semchin,Emile d'Angremont,Hao Ding,Alan Antar,Marco Lorenzi,Konstantinos Arfanakis,Ysbrand van der Werf,Paul Thompson,Boris Gutman*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 提出连接组约束的帕金森病进展模型，联合估计个体疾病时间与数据驱动亚型，在PPMI队列中识别出四种形态学进展亚型，并在匹配验证协议下优于SuStaIn。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病在临床和生物学上具有异质性，但其时空进展特征仍表征不足，亟需能够刻画个体化进展轨迹与亚型的建模方法。

Method: 构建连接组约束的疾病进展模型，基于纵向形态学数据联合估计受试者特异的疾病时间与数据驱动的亚型；使用PPMI队列中85个影像与临床生物标志物进行建模，并在留出横截面数据集上验证，在匹配的训练-验证协议下与SuStaIn进行基准对比。

Result: 模型恢复出四种形态学上不同的进展亚型；在留出横截面数据集上验证有效；在匹配协议下，仅本方法恢复的亚型与帕金森病临床运动亚型及遗传变异存在显著对应关系。

Conclusion: 连接组约束的进展建模能够刻画帕金森病的异质性时空进展，所得亚型具有临床与遗传学意义，优于现有方法SuStaIn。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease is clinically and biologically heterogeneous, yet its spatiotemporal progression remains poorly characterized. We present a connectome-constrained disease progression model that jointly estimates subject-specific disease time and data-driven subtypes from longitudinal morphometry. Applied to 85 imaging and clinical biomarkers from the Parkinson's Progressive Markers Initiative (PPMI) cohort, the model recovers four morphologically distinct progression subtypes. We validate the model on a hold-out cross-sectional dataset and benchmark it against SuStaIn under a matched training and validation protocol. Only our method recovers subtypes that correspond significantly to clinical motor subtypes and genetic variants of Parkinson's Disease.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [360] [HuRo: Robotizing Human Videos for Scalable VLA Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10706)
*Jinho Jeong,Se June Joo,Jaehyun Kang,Dongyun Kim,Yena Kim,Hanjung Kim,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将异构人类视频"机器人化"为观察与动作轨迹，构建约63万episode的HuRo数据集，验证其可作为VLA策略预训练的可扩展监督，显著提升真机操作任务成功率与OOD鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人数据获取成本高昂，人类视频是规模大、多样性丰富的替代来源，但存在人-机具身差距。现有方法要么仅在任务匹配场景下做视频机器人化，要么将观察对齐与动作对齐分开大规模处理，缺乏对"机器人化人类视频能否有效、可扩展地预训练VLA策略"的系统性考察。

Method: 提出机器人化（robotization）流水线，将异构人类视频转换为机器人对齐的观察与动作轨迹，并在不同标注层级上推断缺失的中间信号。基于该流水线构建HuRo数据集：来自5个人类视频源，约630K机器人化episodes、142M处理帧。随后用于VLA策略预训练，并在四类真实世界操作任务上评测，辅以消融分析（视觉机器人化的作用、端到端重定向动作预训练 vs 纯视觉迁移）。

Result: 随机器人化预训练规模增大，四类真机操作任务的总体完成率由51.5%提升至80.3%；在空间与视觉偏移下的OOD完成率由34.9%提升至72.2%。消融表明视觉机器人化提升OOD鲁棒性，且带重定向动作的端到端预训练优于纯视觉迁移。

Conclusion: 机器人化人类视频能够为VLA策略预训练提供有效且可扩展的监督，规模与动作对齐（而非仅视觉对齐）是性能提升与OOD泛化的关键，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Human video datasets have emerged as a compelling alternative to expensive real-robot data, offering rich diversity at scale. To bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap, existing approaches either robotize videos in task-matched settings or address observation and action alignment separately at scale. In this work, we systematically examine whether robotized human videos can provide effective and scalable supervision for pretraining vision-language-action (VLA) policies. To this end, we develop a robotization pipeline that converts heterogeneous human videos into robot-aligned observations and action trajectories while inferring missing intermediate signals across annotation levels. Using this pipeline, we construct the HuRo dataset, comprising about 630K robotized episodes and 142M processed frames from five human-video sources. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, increasing robotized pretraining scale improves overall completion from 51.5% to 80.3% and OOD completion under spatial and visual shifts from 34.9% to 72.2%. Ablations further show that visual robotization improves OOD robustness and that end-to-end pretraining with retargeted actions outperforms visual-only transfer. Code and data are released on our website: https://3587jjh.github.io/HuRo.

</details>


### [361] [When Information is Worth the Risk: Behavioral Valuation for Hazardous Robotic Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10726)
*Alkesh K. Srivastava,Aamodh Suresh,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Philip Dames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"估值层"视角，在保持信念更新、传感器模型、物理风险模型与有限时域信息规划器不变的前提下，仅替换用于排序可行路径的标量目标函数，引入基于Prelec概率加权的风险增强行为信息（Behavioral Information）目标，形成从保守到激进的可解释信息-风险估值族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危险环境中的机器人探索需要同时考虑空间风险（不安全地形、辐射、火灾、地雷、结构损伤等）与信息获取，而信息量高的路径可能使机器人暴露于风险、导致任务终止并阻断后续观测。因此不仅要判断何处不确定性最大，还要判断降低不确定性是否值得承担相应风险，现有以Shannon信息为核心的目标函数无法刻画这一权衡。

Method: 采用估值层框架：固定信念更新、传感器模型、物理风险模型与有限时域信息规划器，仅改变用于排序可行路径的标量目标。提出基于Prelec概率加权的风险增强行为信息目标，产生一族可解释的"保守—激进"信息-风险估值参数。理论上分析估值参数如何形成高信息/高风险与低信息/低风险路径之间的切换边界，并诱导可行探索策略上的变换Pareto前沿结构。在大规模失败截断网格世界中进行实验验证。

Result: 估值参数单独即可重塑信息-风险前沿。Shannon信息规划仍是有力的原始信息基线；风险感知目标可通过规避会截断未来感知的失败，降低危险暴露与机器人损失。风险增强行为估值与标准风险感知基线相比具有Pareto竞争力，并提供可解释的保守与中间机制。

Conclusion: 研究支持这样一种框架：机器人不仅推理某一动作能降低多少不确定性，还应推理这种降低是否值得付出相应风险代价。估值参数作为可调旋钮，在信息增益与生存/风险之间实现可解释的连续权衡，为危险环境下的风险感知探索提供了统一的分析与设计视角。

Abstract: Hazardous robotic exploration requires robots to map spatial risks, such as unsafe terrain, radiation, fire, mines, or structural damage, while operating where collecting information can itself cause failure. A highly informative path may expose the robot to hazards, terminate execution, and prevent future observations. Hazardous exploration therefore requires deciding not only where uncertainty is largest, but when reducing it is worth the risk. This paper introduces a valuation-layer view of this problem. We keep the belief update, sensor model, physical risk model, and finite-horizon informative planner fixed, and change only the scalar objective used to rank feasible paths. Within this framework, we introduce a risk-augmented Behavioral Information objective based on Prelec probability weighting, yielding an interpretable family of conservative-to-aggressive information-risk valuations. Theoretically, we show that valuation parameters create switching boundaries between high-information/high-risk and lower-information/lower-risk paths, and induce a transformed Pareto-frontier structure over feasible exploration policies. Large-scale failure-truncated grid-world experiments show that valuation alone reshapes the information-risk frontier. Shannon information planning remains a strong raw-information baseline, while risk-aware objectives can reduce hazard exposure and robot losses by avoiding failures that truncate future sensing. Risk-augmented Behavioral valuation is Pareto-competitive with standard risk-aware baselines and provides interpretable conservative and intermediate regimes. These results support a framework in which robots reason not only about how much uncertainty an action reduces, but whether that reduction is worth the risk required to obtain it.

</details>


### [362] [Lie-Algebraic Bell Recurrences for Arbitrary-Order Twist Jets and Parallel-Mechanism Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10748)
*Daniel Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种任意阶运动学构造，在统一的对偶螺旋框架内联系串联传播、并联机构闭环与刚体平台点场，导出基于贝尔多项式的任意阶主动-被动闭环递推，并以三类机构验证至四阶精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有串联与并联机构的螺旋/旋量运动学多限于低阶（速度、加速度），难以统一、系统地给出任意阶（急动、急跳及更高阶）传播与闭环的解析表达式；同时串联链中非交换因子的物理顺序保持、并联机构高阶闭环递推、以及平台螺旋与刚体点场仿射不变量的精确映射均缺乏统一框架。

Method: 构造基于对偶螺旋的任意阶运动学：（1）以圆柱副为原生物理模块，转动副与移动副为其特例；（2）对固定关节轴，用普通贝尔多项式组织指数因子的各阶导数，串联链中非交换因子保持物理次序；（3）给出初始框架前缀式与末端解算的协变公式，得到串联螺旋射流的等价表示；（4）并联机构中通过反复莱布尼茨微分、关节层导数由贝尔多项式组织，导出任意阶三角主动-被动闭环递推：在正则位形下每一阶导数求解同一被动雅可比，右端仅含规定主动数据与低阶射流；（5）将平台螺旋射流精确映射为速度、加速度、急动、急跳场中点无关的仿射不变量。

Result: 验证采用互补案例：非共面轴且含非零转动与移动圆柱坐标的一般3C链检验有序串联传播；RR+RRR球形手腕检验主动-被动闭环；Hunt型6-RUS机构（六个主动转动副）检验独立重构的平台射流及其仿射场。对刚体运动独立微分、仿射场求值以及微分分支闭环均一致至四阶，残差低于$10^{-12}$（相应SI单位）。

Conclusion: 该表述为纯运动学，适用于所选主动-被动划分保持正则的位形，可在统一对偶螺旋框架中任意阶地处理串联传播、并联闭环与刚体点场，兼顾非交换顺序与高阶递推的一致性。

Abstract: This paper develops an arbitrary-order kinematic construction that links serial propagation, parallel-mechanism closure, and rigid-platform point fields within one dual screw framework. A cylindrical joint is retained as one native physical block, with revolute and prismatic joints obtained as special cases. For each fixed joint axis, ordinary Bell polynomials organize the derivatives of the exponential factor; across a chain, the noncommuting factors remain in their physical order. Initial-frame prefix and terminal-resolved covariant formulas then produce equivalent representations of the serial twist jet. For a parallel mechanism, repeated Leibniz differentiation, with joint-level derivatives organized by Bell polynomials, yields an arbitrary-order triangular active-passive closure recurrence: the same passive Jacobian is solved at every derivative order at a regular configuration, while the right-hand side contains only prescribed active data and lower-order jets. The resulting platform twist jet is mapped exactly to the point-independent affine invariants of the velocity, acceleration, jerk, and snap fields. The validation is deliberately complementary: a generic 3C chain with noncoplanar axes and nonzero rotational and translational cylindrical coordinates tests ordered serial propagation, an RR+RRR spherical wrist tests active-passive closure, and a Hunt-type 6-RUS mechanism with six active revolute joints tests an independently reconstructed platform jet and its affine fields. Independent differentiation of the rigid motion, evaluation of the affine fields, and the differentiated branch closures all agree through fourth order with residuals below $10^{-12}$ in the corresponding SI units. The formulation is purely kinematic and applies at configurations where the selected active-passive partition is regular.

</details>


### [363] [Expressive Robotic Pianist: Mastering Complex Piano Repertoire with Graph-Mimic and Musical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10844)
*Yanhong Liang,Xianwei Liu,Chaojie Fu,Shaowen Cheng,Yanyan Yuan,Chengwei Zhuo,Xi Chen,Yongbin Jin,Wei Yang,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于强化学习的控制框架，使灵巧机械手能够在多样化钢琴曲目上实现高保真、富有表现力的演奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演奏乐器时，机械精度与艺术表现力之间存在鸿沟；在机器人灵巧性已取得进展的背景下，复现人类钢琴家流畅的指法转换与细腻力度控制仍是重大挑战。

Method: 采用强化学习控制框架；核心是图优化策略，用于生成类人的预压与按键指法策略；控制系统与受物理启发的声学模型耦合，通过调制按键速度来还原乐谱标注的力度变化。

Result: 定量评估显示，该表现力控制模型在手指形态相似度与力度速度准确性上显著优于基线；感知测试中，非专业听众显著偏好该系统演奏，且无法将其与人类演奏区分；多音乐风格实验表明，方法在保持高音符级准确率的同时实现富有表现力的演奏。

Conclusion: 该工作为机器人系统超越单纯机械精度、将机器人音乐演奏提升至可与人类钢琴家媲美的表现力水平提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Enabling robots to perform musical instruments with human-level expressivity represents a frontier in bridging the gap between mechanical precision and artistic interpretation. Despite advances in robotic dexterity, replicating the fluid finger transitions and nuanced dynamic control characteristic of human pianists remains a significant challenge. Through a reinforcement learning-based control framework, we demonstrate that a dexterous robotic hand can achieve high-fidelity performance across a diverse piano repertoire. Central to our approach is a graph-based optimization strategy that guides the robot to generate natural pre-press and key-press fingering strategies that closely resemble human movement patterns. To achieve expressive sound production, the control system is coupled with a physics-inspired acoustic model that modulates keypress velocity to accurately reproduce the dynamic variations specified in musical scores. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate that our expressive control model significantly outperforms baseline methods in both finger morphology similarity and dynamic velocity accuracy. In a perceptual test involving participants from diverse listener groups, performances generated by our system are significantly preferred over baseline robotic performances and are indistinguishable from human performances for non-professional audiences. Furthermore, extensive experiments across multiple musical styles confirm that our method maintains high note-level accuracy while achieving expressive performance. Our approach provides a robust pathway for robotic systems to move beyond mere mechanical accuracy, elevating robotic musicianship to a level of expressive performance comparable to human pianists.

</details>


### [364] [ReactHuman: A Physics-Grounded Benchmark for Human-Like Reactive Decision-Making in Embodied Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10895)
*Yizhan Li,Jianxin You,Mengyang Xiong,Yinhuan Chen,Zicheng Zhao,Dekun Wu,Dongqing Zhang,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReactHuman——首个面向类人反应式决策的物理基准，让MLLM充当仿真人形机器人大脑应对突发家庭危险，含17类事件、1000+可复现场景及五指标评测，实验显示七款代表性MLLM反应安全性远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突发物理危险的即时反应（接住滑落盘子、躲避下坠刀具）既是具身智能的重要测试，也是将多模态大模型作为家务机器人决策核心的硬性要求。现有评测或用视频问答被动考察直觉物理，或针对导航、重排等长时程刻意任务，均未衡量模型能否把物理理解转化为即时、安全攸关的行动。

Method: 构建ReactHuman基准：被评测MLLM作为仿真人形机器人大脑，面对突发家庭危险；覆盖17类事件、1000+逐比特可复现场景，基于240Hz刚体仿真生成精确、免标注的真值，并包含外观与物理属性相悖的对抗性物体（泡沫铁砧、钢质苹果）。设计五指标评测套件，从合理、安全、物理可依三轴打分；对每个已提交计划进行物理执行，使决策产生可观测后果。用该框架评测七款代表性MLLM。

Result: 模型约每三类危险中就会处理错一类；行为源自固定倾向而非观测到的场景；更信任外观而非运动信息；即使动作选择正确，也在米级尺度上错失拦截点；上述失败均不随模型规模增大而缓解。

Conclusion: ReactHuman既能提供细粒度诊断，也可作为可扩展的训练信号，推动构建物理可依、具备安全意识的具身智能体。基准已在Hugging Face公开。

Abstract: Reacting to sudden physical hazards (catching a slipping plate, dodging a falling knife) is both a meaningful test of embodied intelligence and a hard requirement for deploying multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as the decision coreof household robots. Existing evaluations, however, probe intuitive physics passively through question answering over videos, or target deliberate, long-horizon tasks such as navigation and rearrangement; none measure whether a model can turn physical understanding into immediate, safety-critical action. We introduce ReactHuman, the first physics-grounded benchmark for human-like reactive decision-making, in which the evaluated MLLM acts as the brain of a simulated humanoid facing sudden household hazards; it spans 17 event families and over 1,000 bit-for-bit reproducible scenes with exact, annotation-free ground truth derived from 240 Hz rigid-body simulation, including adversarial objects whose appearance contradicts their physics (a foam anvil, a steel apple). We further design a five-metric suite that scores each reaction along three axes: reasonable, safe, and physically grounded. We physically execute every committed plan so that decisions have observable consequences. With this harness we evaluate seven representative MLLMs. Results show that reactive safety is far from solved: models mishandle roughly one hazard in three, act from fixed dispositions rather than the observed scene, trust appearance over motion, and miss interception points at meter scale even when the chosen action is correct; none of these failures shrink with model scale. ReactHuman thus offers both a fine-grained diagnosis and a scalable training signal toward physically grounded, safety-aware embodied agents. The benchmark can be found here: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Alan123/reacthuman-benchmark-scaled

</details>


### [365] [Planning along Differentiable Charts of Constraint Manifolds with General-Purpose IK Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10905)
*Thomas Cohn,Seiji Shaw,Harel Biggie,Travis Manderson,Nicholas Roy,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用逆函数定理，从普通正运动学雅可比中恢复解析逆运动学（IK）参数化的梯度，并给出最小二乘域扩展与可达性约束的优化友好描述，使IKFast等自动求解器生成的不可微IK函数可用于基于梯度的轨迹优化，并已在硬件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人机械臂在运动学等式约束下的轨迹规划，其可行运动被限制在构型空间的零测度子流形上，需要特殊算法处理。用解析IK对可行构型集参数化是一种有前景的策略，而基于梯度的轨迹优化要求该参数化可微；但绝大多数IK函数由IKFast等自动元求解器生成，难以修改为可微形式。

Method: 1）利用逆函数定理，从普通正运动学雅可比中恢复解析IK参数化所需的梯度，避免修改IKFast生成的代码；2）提出最小二乘域扩展，使梯度信号在工作空间外仍保留；3）给出可达性约束的优化友好描述。

Result: 通过数值实验与下游任务验证了方法的有效性，并完成硬件演示：RB-Y1机器人抓取箱子并将其放置到桌面上。

Conclusion: 该方法为自动生成的解析IK参数化提供了通用可微化途径，兼顾域外梯度信号与可达性约束处理，适用于基于梯度的机械臂轨迹优化并具备实机可行性。项目网站：https://cohnt.github.io/inverse-function-theorem-parameterization/

Abstract: Planning trajectories for robot manipulators under kinematic equality constraints restricts feasible motions to a measure-zero submanifold of the configuration space, requiring special algorithmic treatment. A promising strategy is parametrizing the set of feasible configurations using analytic inverse kinematics (IK). Bespoke analytic IK functions can be written to be differentiable, a necessary property for gradient-based trajectory optimization. But the vast majority of IK functions are computed by automated meta-solvers like IKFast, and are difficult to modify for differentiability. We present a new approach for computing gradients of analytic IK parameterizations: we leverage the inverse function theorem to recover the desired gradients from the ordinary forward kinematic Jacobian. Furthermore, we present a least-squares domain extension and an optimization-amenable description of the reachability constraint, which preserves gradient signal outside the reachable workspace. We demonstrate the efficacy of our approach through numerical experiments and downstream tasks, including a hardware demonstration of an RB-Y1 picking up a box and placing it on a table. Project website: https://cohnt.github.io/inverse-function-theorem-parameterization/

</details>


### [366] [IMLE-VLA: Fast Single-Step Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10915)
*Kian Hosseinkhani,Qinhe Peng,George Shramko,Mehran Aghabozorgi,Jianing Qian,Tristan Engst,Alireza Moazeni,Dinesh Jayaraman,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMLE-VLA将VLA策略中的迭代式连续动作头替换为基于cIMLE训练的单步条件生成器，在保持多模态覆盖与泛化能力的同时消除多步采样瓶颈，实现最高3.67倍推理频率提升与98.0%的LIBERO成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略常将预训练视觉-语言主干与连续动作头（扩散或流匹配）耦合，但此类动作头依赖多步迭代采样（如π0.5的10步Euler采样），造成推理瓶颈，导致机器人出现走走停停的运动并延长任务完成时间。

Method: 提出IMLE-VLA，用单步条件生成器替代迭代动作头，通过条件隐式最大似然估计（cIMLE）目标进行训练；cIMLE目标促进多模态动作覆盖，避免朴素回归头的模式崩溃，同时彻底消除多步采样。该方法被应用于π0.5。

Result: 在π0.5上，推理频率提升3.67倍（55 Hz vs. 15 Hz），动作吞吐量最高提升11倍；在40项任务的LIBERO基准上取得最高平均成功率98.0%，且推理频率领先；在LIBERO-plus测试时扰动下保持π0.5的鲁棒性，而其他基线显著退化；真实世界Franka Emika Panda四任务实验中运动更平滑（抖动降低2.2至3.0倍）、任务完成更快，每个episode的平均VLA推理时间减少3.9至6.6倍，并在所有任务上超越π0.5。

Conclusion: 用cIMLE训练的单步生成动作头可在不损失多模态性与泛化鲁棒性的前提下，显著消除VLA推理瓶颈，兼顾速度、平滑性与成功率，是替代扩散/流匹配迭代动作头的有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies leverage pretrained vision-language backbones to achieve strong cross-task generalization. A leading design couples this backbone with a dedicated continuous action head trained via diffusion or flow matching. However, such heads rely on iterative multi-step sampling, for example 10 Euler steps in $π_{0.5}$. This creates an inference bottleneck that produces stop-and-go movement in the robot and slower task completion. We introduce IMLE-VLA, which replaces the iterative action head with a single-step conditional generator trained via conditional Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (cIMLE). The cIMLE objective promotes multimodal action coverage, avoiding the mode collapse of naive regression heads while eliminating multi-step sampling entirely. When IMLE-VLA is applied to $π_{0.5}$, it increases inference frequency 3.67x (55 Hz vs. 15 Hz), enabling up to 11x higher action throughput. On the 40-task LIBERO benchmark, IMLE-VLA achieves the highest average success rate (98.0%) among all baselines while leading in inference frequency. Under the test-time perturbations of LIBERO-plus, IMLE-VLA retains $π_{0.5}$'s robustness while other baselines degrade sharply, confirming that the cIMLE head preserves generalization. Real-world experiments on a Franka Emika Panda across four tasks demonstrate smoother motion (2.2x to 3.0x lower jerk) and faster task completion, with IMLE-VLA outperforming $π_{0.5}$ on every task and reducing average VLA inference time per episode by 3.9x to 6.6x. Videos and code are available at https://kianhk6.github.io/IMLE-VLA/

</details>


### [367] [ObstaDiff: Generalizable Diffusion Policy Learning via Obstacle-aware Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10918)
*Jiawen Wang,Kevin Yao,Khalid Jawed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ObstaDiff：一种分解式扩散策略框架，配轻量障碍感知视觉编码器，将场景解耦为目标-障碍物-背景结构化表征，用于杂乱农业场景中的机器人操作，61次试验中平均任务成功率75.41%、平均碰撞率8.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法多假设干净背景，缺乏显式的障碍物感知运动生成机制，难以泛化到含非结构化障碍物的杂乱真实场景，尤其农业环境。

Method: 提出 ObstaDiff，采用分解式扩散策略框架与轻量障碍感知视觉编码器，提取目标-障碍物-背景结构化表征；下游对齐策略据此生成朝目标中心瓶颈位姿的末端执行器轨迹，并在过程中对周围障碍物进行推理。

Result: 在真实机器人温室场景中，每种方法61次试验（共366次执行）。ObstaDiff 平均任务成功率达75.41%，平均障碍物碰撞率为8.20%，优于代表性模仿学习基线。

Conclusion: ObstaDiff 通过结构化场景分解与障碍感知扩散策略，提升了杂乱农业场景下的泛化能力，验证了分解式表征在障碍物感知操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Imitation learning has achieved impressive results in robotic manipulation, yet most existing approaches assume clean backgrounds and lack explicit mechanisms for obstacle-aware motion generation. Extending such policies to cluttered, real-world scenes with unstructured obstacles remains a key generalization challenge. We present ObstaDiff, a decomposed diffusion-policy framework with a lightweight obstacle-aware visual encoder. ObstaDiff extracts a structured target-obstacle-background representation, enabling the downstream alignment policy to generate end-effector trajectories toward a target-centered bottleneck pose while reasoning about surrounding obstacles. We evaluate ObstaDiff on 61 real-robot greenhouse trials per method (366 executions in total). ObstaDiff achieves 75.41% average task success and 8.20% average obstacle collision rate, outperforming representative imitation-learning baselines and improving generalization in cluttered agricultural scenes.

</details>


### [368] [Testing Between the Test Cases: Proving End-to-End Steering in Conditions You Never Drove](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10951)
*Menuka Ghalan,Charles Rodgers,Zachary D. Asher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在CARLA中训练了四个小型端到端转向网络（公路与城市干道各两个：一个仅用晴天数据、一个用晴/雾/夜/低日照数据），并用界传播（bound propagation）这一形式化方法在无需再次仿真的情况下，计算每对图像之间在所有扰动强度下的转向漂移量。形式化验证在未进行仿真测试的条件下发现了会击破"仅晴天训练"策略的场景，并为测试用例之间的潜在失效提供了初步证据，说明其可作为仿真验证的可行补充，纳入自动驾驶验证与确认流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的自动驾驶测试存在根本性困难：一个通过了所有测试条件的模型仍可能在真实世界中失效，即测试通过无法保证实际安全性。形式化验证被认为可以直接弥合这一"测试覆盖与真实安全"之间的鸿沟，因此作者希望在仿真高速与城市干道场景中检验形式化验证能否作为仿真的补充手段。

Method: 在CARLA仿真环境中，针对高速公路与城市干道两种场景，各训练两个小型端到端转向网络，共四个模型：其一仅用晴天条件训练，其二用晴、雾、夜、低日照四种条件训练。四个模型均以2.19英尺的车道偏离预算作为安全约束进行评估。在不再次进行驾驶仿真的前提下，采用界传播（bound propagation）这一形式化方法读取已训练权重，对每两张采集图像之间、在每一扰动强度下的转向漂移范围进行计算。这一计算在单块GPU上数分钟即可完成，其覆盖范围在干道上可横跨133个位姿，若对10个强度逐一枚举则达10^133种组合。

Result: 形式化验证在未依赖仿真测试的情况下，成功找到了会使仅晴天训练策略失效的条件，并为测试用例之间的潜在失效提供了初步证据。同时，单次计算即可覆盖远超实际路测 campaign 所能遍历的条件组合（干道上覆盖133个位姿、10种强度对应10^133种组合），且计算成本低（单GPU数分钟）。

Conclusion: 形式化验证是仿真验证的可行补充手段，可在不重复仿真的情况下揭示策略失效与测试用例之间的潜在风险。作者据此建议将形式化验证纳入自动驾驶的验证与确认（V&V）框架。

Abstract: AI-based automated vehicle testing is challenging because a model that passes every test condition can still fail in the real world. Formal verification offers a way to directly address this gap. On a simulated highway and an arterial road we trained two small end-to-end steering networks each in CARLA, one on clear conditions alone and one on clear, fog, night and low sun. All four models were driven against a 2.19 ft lane-departure budget. Without driving again, we used bound propagation, a formal method that reads the trained weights, to compute how far steering can drift at every disturbance strength between two captured images. One calculation covers more than a campaign could drive: on the arterial it spans 133 poses, where ten intensities each would be 10^133 combinations, in minutes on one GPU. Not only did formal verification find conditions that broke the clear-trained policy without simulation testing, it provided some preliminary evidence for potential failures between the test cases. Our overall conclusion is that formal verification is a viable complement to simulation, and could be adopted as a part of verification and validation for automated driving.

</details>


### [369] [LTLDiff: Finite Linear Temporal Logic-Guided Data Generation and Diffusion Policies for Multi-agent Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11043)
*Chuhan Meng,Haiyan Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LTLDiff框架将有限线性时序逻辑（LTLf）规范学习与扩散策略结合，用于多智能体机器人操作任务，通过LLM从自然语言指令学习任务LTLf公式，并用抽象语法树嵌入作为条件指导数据收集和策略训练，实验显示任务成功率优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体机器人操作需要协调以满足任务级的时间、逻辑和安全约束。现有扩散策略仍存在不同步、动作顺序错误以及在需要同时或顺序多智能体交互的任务中协调失败的问题。

Method: 提出LTLDiff框架：对每个任务，使用大规模语言模型从一组自然语言指令中学习特定的LTLf公式；采用抽象语法树表示方案将学到的规范嵌入为固定维度向量；该逻辑嵌入作为条件用于(i)逻辑引导的数据收集和(ii)基于扩散的策略训练，促使轨迹符合期望的顺序与协调要求。

Result: 在多智能体LTLDiff操作任务上的实验表明，与基线相比任务成功率有所提升。

Conclusion: 这些贡献共同证明了LTLDiff在协调多智能体操作任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent robotic manipulation tasks require coordination among agents to satisfy task-level temporal, logical, and safety constraints. Recently, diffusion policies have been used to perform the task. However, they still suffer from desynchronization, incorrect action ordering, and coordination failures in tasks that require simultaneous or sequential multi-agent interaction. Therefore, LTLDiff is proposed as a framework that combines Finite Linear Temporal Logic (LTLf) specification learning for both the generation of demonstrations and learning via diffusion policies. Each task has a specific LTLf formula that is learned from a set of natural language instructions using a large-scale language model. To enable a fixed-dimensional vector embedding of the learned specification from the language model, LTLf uses an abstract syntax tree representation scheme. This embedding of logic serves as a condition for (i) logic-guided data collection and (ii) diffusion-based policy training, encouraging trajectories that are consistent with the desired ordering and coordination requirements. Experiments on multi-agent LTLDiff manipulation tasks demonstrate improved task success rates compared to the baseline. Together, these contributions demonstrate the effectiveness of LTLDiff for coordinated multi-agent manipulation.

</details>


### [370] [Gait-Dependent Effects on Quadruped Locomotion for Load-Carrying using Passive Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11059)
*Giovanni B. Dessy,Claudio Semini,Victor Barasuol*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在仿真中研究了被动机械臂的刚度-阻尼配置对四足机器人负载 locomotion 的影响，比较欠阻尼与阻尼配置，并以 gait-payload-stiffness-damping 图总结 ZMP 裕度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动机械接口是四足负载携带的轻量化替代方案，但其阻抗会将负载动力学与步态模式直接耦合，因此需要厘清被动臂刚度与阻尼如何影响负载携带下的运动稳定性。

Method: 在平地上进行仿真对比，设置阻尼与欠阻尼两种被动臂阻抗配置；以 crawl 步态作为可定义支撑多边形的直接 ZMP 裕度比较对象，将 trot 仅作为被动臂动态激励工况。

Result: 对于 crawl 步态，欠阻尼阻抗会增大被动关节振荡，并可能降低相对于支撑多边形的 ZMP 裕度；结果以 gait-payload-stiffness-damping 图进行汇总，其中 ZMP 裕度下降在 crawl 中评估，trot 仅保留为被动臂激励工况。

Conclusion: 被动臂的刚度-阻尼选择显著影响负载携带步态的 ZMP 裕度，欠阻尼配置在 crawl 中可能减小稳定性裕度；trot 不适合用于直接的 ZMP 裕度稳定性比较。

Abstract: Passive mechanical interfaces offer a lightweight alternative to actuated manipulators for quadruped payload carrying, but their impedance directly couples the payload dynamics with the locomotion pattern. This paper analyzes how passive-arm stiffness-damping selection affects payload-carrying locomotion under different gait and payload conditions. We compare damped and underdamped passive-arm impedance configurations in simulation during flat-ground locomotion. For crawl gaits, where the support polygon remains well defined, the results show that underdamped impedance increases passive-joint oscillations and can reduce the ZMP margin with respect to the support polygon. Trot is retained as a dynamic excitation case for the passive arm, but it is not used for direct ZMP-margin stability comparison. The results are summarized in gait-payload-stiffness-damping maps, where ZMP-margin reduction is evaluated for crawl gaits and trot is retained only as a passive-arm excitation case.

</details>


### [371] [Freehand Sketching for End-User Programming of Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11078)
*Riwa Karam,Ian Kuo,Magnus Egerstedt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出以徒手草图作为终端用户编程接口来指定机器人集群几何构型，通过"草图到集群"框架实现目标编队点提取、刚性编队图构建、机器人分配及分布式编队控制（并防止镜像编队），用户研究显示SUS均分84.25，表明高可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人集群应用日益需要非专家用户可访问的交互方式，而传统编程接口对无机器人或编程经验者门槛较高。

Method: 研究徒手草图作为终端用户编程接口：用户通过绘图表达空间意图，集群自主提取目标编队点、构建刚性编队图、将机器人分配至编队节点，并执行带防非预期镜像编队保证的分布式编队控制；随后通过20名参与者的人因研究评估可用性。

Result: 20名参与者共生成42个几何形状，界面获得平均84.25的SUS（系统可用性量表）得分，按常规标准属高感知可用性。

Conclusion: 徒手草图可作为直观的人-集群协作交互抽象，无需用户具备机器人或编程专业知识。

Abstract: Robot swarms are increasingly used in applications where accessible interaction with non-expert users is desirable. This paper investigates freehand sketching as an end-user programming interface for specifying robot swarm geometries. Users communicate spatial intent through a drawing, while the swarm autonomously extracts target formation points, constructs a rigid formation graph, assigns robots to formation nodes, and executes distributed formation control with a guarantee against unintended reflected formations. The resulting sketch-to-swarm framework is evaluated through a human study examining the usability of freehand formation specification. Twenty participants generated $42$ geometric shapes, and the interface achieved a mean System Usability Scale score of $84.25$, which conventionally indicates high perceived usability. The results support freehand sketching as an intuitive interaction abstraction for human-swarm collaboration without requiring robotics or programming expertise.

</details>


### [372] [RIDE: Relocalization-Informed Depth Estimation with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11079)
*Jiarong Lian,Zhe Xiao,Zhaoyang Zhang,Wei Li,Ruizhi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RIDE方法，利用render-match-PnP重定位中的几何信息，结合稀疏度量深度与视频深度先验，实现机器人RGB流的稠密度量深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: render-match-PnP重定位能建立查询图像像素与3D地图点的对应关系以恢复相机位姿，但其支持稠密深度估计的潜力常被忽视。作者希望挖掘这种几何信息，从机器人RGB流中获得稠密度量深度。

Method: 给定度量尺度的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）模型，RIDE将PnP-RANSAC内点对应导出的稀疏度量深度观测与预训练视频深度模型的几何先验相结合；针对观测不均且间歇的问题，引入全局与局部深度校正及时间记忆机制，在度量尺度初始化后支持跨越短观测缺口的深度估计。模型在公开RGB-D视频上训练。

Result: 在机器人序列上无需微调进行评估，相比仅做尺度校准的方法，RIDE在深度精度和时间一致性上均有提升。

Conclusion: 实验表明定位几何不仅能支持位姿恢复，还能支撑稠密的机器人感知。

Abstract: Render--match--PnP relocalization establishes correspondences between query image pixels and 3D map points for camera pose recovery, but their potential to support dense depth estimation is often overlooked. To exploit this geometric information, we present RIDE, which estimates dense metric depth from a robot's RGB stream. Given a metrically scaled 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model, RIDE combines sparse metric depth observations derived from PnP-RANSAC inlier correspondences with the geometric prior of a pretrained video-depth model. To handle uneven and intermittent observations, it integrates global and local depth correction with temporal memory, supporting depth estimation through short observation gaps after metric scale initialization. Trained on public RGB-D videos, RIDE is evaluated on robot sequences without fine tuning. Experiments show improved depth accuracy and temporal consistency over scale-only calibration, demonstrating how localization geometry can support both pose recovery and dense robot perception.

</details>


### [373] [Harness Robotic OS: A Unified Embodied-Agent Runtime for Closed-Loop Quadruped Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11225)
*Yaoyuan Yan,Zhiyou Heng,Haoxiang Jie,Gang Liu,Hongjie Yan,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一具身智能体运行时HROS及其社区巡检实例Argos，通过共享上下文、分层记忆与安全门控自进化实现可追溯的自主巡检闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足巡检系统多以任务专用接口集成感知、自主能力、场景理解、人机交互与企业响应，导致上下文协调、知识复用与受控自适应困难，难以形成可部署、可追溯的运维闭环。

Method: HROS将系统划分为机器人运行时、具身自主技能、认知智能体运行时与交互运维平面；以共享上下文连接物理状态与智能体推理，采用流式ASR/TTS语音任务交互，构建工作/情节/语义三层记忆，并设计安全门控自进化循环将执行轨迹转化为版本化候选更新，禁止无约束在线修改。Argos原型集成Vbot四足机器人、Fast-LIO2定位建图、Hobot-Stereo深度感知、PCT-Planner全局规划、EGO-Planner局部运动生成及OpenClaw编排的Qwen3-VL巡检分析。

Result: 在住宅社区环境中实现100%航点可达率，室外定位误差低于10 cm，局部避障响应延迟低于200 ms，典型隐患检测率85–95%，报警投递与结构化报告生成成功率99%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了所部署导航与巡检闭环的有效性，HROS为面向记忆增强、语音感知且可持续改进的具身巡检智能体提供了可扩展软件基础。

Abstract: Autonomous property inspection requires more than robust robot navigation: a deployable system must connect heterogeneous sensing, reusable autonomy capabilities, multimodal scene understanding, human interaction, and enterprise response within a traceable operational loop. Existing quadruped inspection systems commonly integrate these functions through task-specific interfaces, making contextual coordination, knowledge reuse, and controlled adaptation difficult. This paper presents \textit{Harness Robotic OS} (HROS), a unified embodied-agent runtime, and Argos, its realization for residential-community inspection. HROS organizes the system into robot runtime, embodied autonomy skills, cognitive agent runtime, and interaction and operations planes. A shared context connects physical state with agent reasoning; streaming ASR/TTS supports voice-based mission interaction; hierarchical working, episodic, and semantic memory preserves operational knowledge; and a safety-gated self-evolution loop converts execution traces into versioned candidate updates without permitting unconstrained online modification. The Argos prototype integrates a Vbot quadruped, Fast-LIO2 localization and mapping, Hobot-Stereo depth perception, PCT-Planner global planning, EGO-Planner local motion generation, and OpenClaw-orchestrated Qwen3-VL inspection analysis. Experiments in a residential property environment achieved 100\% waypoint reachability, outdoor localization error below 10~cm, local obstacle-response latency below 200~ms, representative hazard-detection rates of 85--95\%, and 99\% success in alarm delivery and structured-report generation. These results validate the deployed navigation and inspection closed loop, while HROS provides an extensible software foundation for memory-augmented, voice-aware, and continuously improvable embodied inspection agents.

</details>


### [374] [Beyond Noise Steering: Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning for Generative Robot Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11270)
*Pengfei Zhang,Teng Sun,Xianchao Xiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DLSRL框架，在冻结生成器的初始噪声引导之外，增加中间动作表征层面的控制，实现双重潜空间强化学习，加速机器人策略在线自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练生成式机器人策略虽能从示范中学到表达性动作先验，但现有强化学习方法仅操纵噪声空间，无法在生成过程中调节中间动作表征，导致性能下降与效率低下。

Method: actor网络预测两个潜变量：用于引导行为生成的初始噪声潜变量，以及用于中间特征调制的动作表征潜变量；后者映射为适配器特征，并通过残差连接注入中间动作token的隐藏状态，从而在不更新基础策略的情况下直接调节动作表征。

Result: 在多种生成式策略架构与机器人操作任务上，DLSRL有效加速在线机器人策略自适应，并取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 双重控制设计（噪声引导+表征调制）为冻结生成策略的强化学习微调提供了高效途径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Pretrained generative robot policies learn expressive action priors from demonstrations. However, existing reinforcement learning methods only steer the noisy space but fail to modulate intermediate action representations during the generation process, resulting in performance degradation and inefficiency. To address this limitation, we propose a novel Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning (DLSRL) framework, which complements initial-noise steering with representation-level control inside the frozen generator. Specifically, our actor network predicts two distinct latent variables: an initial-noise latent variable that steers behavior generation, and an action-representation latent variable for intermediate feature modulation. Moreover, this representation latent variable is mapped to adapter features and ingeniously injected into the hidden states of intermediate action tokens via residual connections. Our dual-control design enables direct adjustment of action representations without updating the base policy. Experiments across generative policy architectures and robotic manipulation tasks show that DLSRL effectively accelerates online robot policy adaptation and achieves competitive performance. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}.

</details>


### [375] [2AM: Grounding Agent-Side Memory as Guidance for Steerable Action Models in Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11308)
*Yutong Hu,Fengjiao Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Renaud Detry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 2AM将长期记忆完全置于多模态Agent侧，以单一RGB、片段无状态的Action Model作为唯一执行器，通过语言子任务与2D抓取/放置/移动提示实现跨时间尺度的意图绑定，在无深度、无在线几何、无规划器条件下于LIBERO-Mem达76.3%平均完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期机器人操作需要记忆，但现有agentic系统将VLA与规划器、几何工具结合，甚至引入额外深度或标定几何，导致归因混淆：性能提升可能来自更丰富观测或替代运动工具，失败也可能源于策略或欠规范的语言接口。本文希望厘清记忆应置于何处、以及策略能力与Agent可引导性之间的关系。

Method: 采用"工具广度更小、接口带宽更大"的约束设计：多模态Agent为任务记忆的唯一持有者，单一基于RGB、片段无状态的Action Model为唯一运动执行器。Agent将交互历史编译为子任务语言及可选的2D抓取、放置、移动提示，在不同时间尺度上绑定物理意图。为让VLA学会可引导性，用结构化提示标签增强示范，并在条件dropout、空间噪声、时间抖动下训练，以容忍不完美的Agent输出。

Result: 在LIBERO-Mem上，不使用深度、在线几何或基于规划器的物体运动，2AM取得76.3%平均完成率，较最强已报告基线14.8%提升61.5个百分点，并达到63.0%宽松成功率与11.8%严格成功率。

Conclusion: 任务记忆可以保留在Agent侧。Action Model的能力不仅取决于策略学到了什么，还取决于Agent能多精确地引导它；接口带宽而非工具数量是长期操作性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires memory, but not necessarily inside the action policy. To address such tasks, current agentic systems often combine VLAs with planners and geometric tools, sometimes using additional depth or calibrated geometry. These systems confound attribution: gains may come from richer observations or alternative motor tools, while failures may stem from either the policy or an under-specified language interface. We isolate this question through a deliberately constrained design: less tool breadth, but greater interface bandwidth. 2AM makes a multimodal Agent the sole holder of task memory and a single RGB-based, episodically stateless Action Model the sole executor of task-relevant motion. The Agent compiles interaction history into subtask language and optional 2D grasp, place, and move hints that bind its physical intention at different time scales. To teach this steerability to the VLA, we augment demonstrations with structured hint labels and train under condition dropout, spatial noise, and temporal jitter to tolerate imperfect Agent outputs. On LIBERO-Mem, without depth, online geometry, or planner-based object motion, 2AM reaches 76.3% average completion, a 61.5-point improvement over the strongest reported baseline of 14.8%, together with 63.0% relaxed and 11.8% strict success. These results show that task memory can remain Agent-side. They further show that Action Model capability depends not only on what the policy has learned, but on how precisely the Agent can steer it.

</details>


### [376] [Modular Kinematic Reduction of Closed-Chain Mechanisms Using Path Assembly and Defect Homotopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11338)
*Mohammad Dastranj,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PACDM框架，通过路径组装闭链约束、SE(3)对数映射与隐式微分实现模块化闭链解析与运动学降维，并在七自由度重载机械臂上验证精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭链结构通过非线性闭合约束耦合主动与被动坐标，给模块化建模带来困难，需要兼顾模块化闭链求解与运动学降维的统一框架。

Method: 以两个有序变换路径比较定义闭链单元，用SE(3)对数表达失配并由局部变换导数组装雅可比；由最小成对闭链单元构建多路径模块，秩揭示分析选取局部独立标量约束；采用缺陷同伦沿可行正则连续路径恢复闭链一致的被动坐标；在正则位形下隐式微分得到局部主动-被动微分映射，并用于预定运动的预测-校正连续过程。

Result: 在含两路径与三路径闭链模块的七自由度重载机械臂上评估，与Simscape Multibody对比轨迹均方根误差低于8.5×10^-10 rad，预测-校正连续比每个轨迹采样点都执行缺陷同伦约快45.8倍。

Conclusion: PACDM为模块化闭链解析与运动学降维提供了统一的路径组装与微分映射方法，在保证数值精度的同时显著提升连续运动求解效率。

Abstract: Closed kinematic chains complicate modular modeling by coupling active and passive coordinates through nonlinear closure constraints. This paper presents a Path-Assembled Closure Differential Mapping (PACDM) framework for modular closure resolution and kinematic reduction. Each closure element compares two ordered transformation paths with common endpoints, with their mismatch expressed through the logarithm on SE(3) and the corresponding Jacobian assembled from local transformation derivatives. Multi-path modules are constructed from a minimal set of pairwise closure elements, while rank-revealing analysis selects locally independent scalar constraints. A defect homotopy recovers closure-consistent passive coordinates from approximate estimates along a feasible and regular continuation path. At regular configurations, implicit differentiation yields the local active-to-passive differential mapping, which is subsequently used in a predictor-corrector continuation procedure for prescribed motion. The framework is evaluated on a seven-degree-of-freedom heavy-duty manipulator containing two-path and three-path closed-chain modules. Comparison with Simscape Multibody yields trajectory root-mean-square errors below 8.5 x 10^-10 rad, while predictor-corrector continuation is approximately 45.8 times faster than applying defect homotopy at every trajectory sample.

</details>


### [377] [Morphology-Aware Human Motion Retargeting for Wheeled-Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11357)
*Chenbo Xia,Chao Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套可复现的流水线，将多数据集SMPLX人体动作重定向为Galaxea R1 Pro轮式人形机器人的可执行移动-操作行为，涵盖形态预处理、平面基座归一化、差分逆运动学、分层手臂重定向、躯干替代、参考扭转驱动规划以及21维BaseDecode策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人到人形机器人的动作重定向多集中于足式平台，轮式人形系统支持从通用人体动作中耦合移动与操作的研究相对稀少。R1 Pro无腿关节，人体下肢运动必须重新分配到基座运动与躯干姿态上，同时不能牺牲与操作相关的臂部几何，因此需要专门的重定向与规划方案。

Method: 该流水线基于GMR的可配置通用动作重定向与BeyondMimic的物理仿真R1 Pro学习框架，包括：规范体型预处理、平面基座归一化、形态感知差分逆运动学、以肩为根的分层手臂重定向、用于弯腰和下蹲的连续躯干替代；随后由参考扭转驱动的规划层将平面基座运动解码为满足迟滞、运动学连续性、加速度与执行器速率限制的连续三轮转向与滚动指令；最后在Isaac Lab中训练21维BaseDecode策略，采用方向性关节限位缩放、聚焦式上身跟踪与分阶段轮-地接触奖励。

Result: 系统提供了从人体运动数据到可物理跟踪的轮式人形移动-操作行为的完整桥梁，而非仅用于可视化的重定向器。论文未给出定量策略比较结果，相关对比实验安排在后续修订中。

Conclusion: 该工作为轮式人形机器人的通用人体动作重定向与移动-操作提供了可复现的工程化流水线，但尚缺乏定量评估，需在后续版本中补充策略对比与验证。

Abstract: Human-to-humanoid retargeting has largely been studied on legged platforms, while comparatively few wheeled-humanoid systems support coupled locomotion and manipulation from general human motion. Building on GMR's configurable general-motion retargeting and BeyondMimic's physically simulated R1 Pro learning framework, we present a reproducible pipeline that converts multi-dataset SMPLX motion into executable loco-manipulation behavior for the Galaxea R1 Pro wheeled humanoid. The robot has a planar three-wheel base, a serial torso, and two arms but no leg joints, so human lower-body motion must be redistributed across base motion and torso posture without sacrificing manipulation-relevant arm geometry. Our pipeline combines canonical body-shape preprocessing, planar-base normalization, morphology-aware differential inverse kinematics, shoulder-rooted hierarchical arm retargeting, and continuous torso substitution for bending and squatting. A reference-twist-driven planning layer then decodes planar base motion into continuous three-wheel steering and rolling commands subject to hysteresis, kinematic continuity, acceleration, and actuator-rate limits. Finally, a 21-dimensional BaseDecode policy is trained in Isaac Lab with directional joint-limit scaling, focused upper-body tracking, and a staged wheel-contact reward. The resulting system provides a complete bridge from human motion data to physically trackable wheeled-humanoid loco-manipulation rather than a visualization-only retargeter; quantitative policy comparisons remain scheduled for a later revision.

</details>


### [378] [GeoTrussRover: Morphological Computation with Contact-Semantic Control Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11361)
*Muyuan Ma,Yi Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuanyan Zheng,Ruiqi Hu,Boxuan Ke,Zhenyu Chen,Yicong Lin,Xin Hao Yang,Daliang Xiao,Zhinan Hou,Wanhao Niu,Yuan Sun,Yan Yang,Yue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出变几何桁架（VGT）与轮式底盘结合的GeoTrussRover系统，通过接触语义形态规划与控制，将高维协调问题降维为四个接触语义基元，实现可复用、低重算的越障运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构机器人可在固定本体无法通过障碍时改变接触几何；VGT能把形变分布到承力结构中，但将其与移动底盘耦合会产生高维协调难题。需要一种既能承载又能复用运动知识的低维表示方法。

Method: 系统由电驱动VGT、轮式底盘及接触语义形态规划控制组成。求解一次源穿越任务，提取描述21个构件协调关系的四个接触语义基元；采用物理约束投影将其适配到相同接触拓扑的未知台阶高度；若各相位均可行则不重算完整运动，仅有违反新物理约束的相位才重算；最后用全空间QP跟踪适配运动并修正构件与轮子误差。

Result: 从0.10m迁移到0.075m时，目标函数评估次数较完全重算减少63.7%。接触相位可行性分析覆盖0.10–0.46m台阶高度（1.08–4.97倍轮半径），上限接近理论可行边界。电驱动原型可穿越2.11倍轮半径。

Conclusion: 该低维表示把任务协调关系存储于超冗余承力形态中，并在运动过程中复用，验证了接触语义基元方法在可重构移动机器人越障中的有效性与效率。

Abstract: Reconfigurable robots can change their contact geometry when a fixed body cannot negotiate an obstacle. A variable-geometry truss (VGT) distributes this shape change through a load-bearing structure, but coupling it to a mobile base creates a high-dimensional coordination problem. GeoTrussRover combines an electrically actuated VGT, a wheeled base, and contact-semantic morphology planning and control.
